Physics Is All You Need? A Case Study in Physicist-Supervised AI Development of Scientific Software
Authors: Nhat-Minh Nguyen
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:59+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures, 2 tables, 1 physicist and a few AI agents. Accepted by ICML 2026 AI for Science Workshop. Code and development log are available at this repo: https://github.com/MinhMPA/clax-pt
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Are AI agents tools, co-authors, or researchers? We present a quantified case study ($N=1$): a physicist supervising an AI coding agent (Claude Code, Sonnet and Opus models) over 12 work days and 57 sessions to build CLAX-PT, a differentiable one-loop perturbation theory module in JAX. We documented and classified 15 supervision events by intervention level. The agent resolved ten autonomously by iterating against oracle tests. Two more by the physicist's domain knowledge. The three it could not -- all evaded oracle detection -- share a common property: the agent treated symptom reduction as root-cause resolution. It spent 33 of the 57 sessions adjusting coefficients within a code architecture that could not represent the target physics, and could not re-evaluate its CLASS-PT branch choice even when prompted to reconsider; only an injected physics concept (anisotropic BAO damping) triggered the redesign. Separately, the agent committed a calibrated correction that passed all oracle tests but corresponded to no quantity in the theory, predicting wrong values at any other cosmology. The fudge factor was caught and replaced within the same session. Three supervision practices proved critical for catching what oracle tests missed: testing at diverse parameter points beyond the fiducial calibration; shared changelogs that surfaced stalled exploration across sessions; and an explicit rule against unphysical numerical patches. In this case, supervision design, not model capability, determined whether the agent's output was trustworthy. Closing the gap would require agents that propose architectural alternatives rather than optimize within a given structure, and distinguish predictive adequacy from explanatory correctness -- capabilities not exhibited here, not obviously addressed by scaling alone. [Abridged.]
中文标题/摘要
标题：物理就是一切所需？一位物理学家监督AI开发科学软件的案例研究
AI代理是工具、合著者还是研究人员？我们呈现了一个量化案例研究（N=1）：一位物理学家在12个工作日、57次会话中监督AI编码代理（Claude Code、Sonnet和Opus模型）开发CLAX-PT，这是一个基于JAX的可微分一阶微扰理论模块。我们记录并分类了15次监督事件，按干预级别划分。代理自主解决了其中十个问题，通过反复对照“神谕”测试实现；另有两个问题由物理学家的领域知识解决。其余三个问题——全部规避了“神谕”检测——具有共同特征：代理将症状缓解误认为根因解决。在57次会话中，代理33次调整了无法表征目标物理的代码架构中的系数，且在被提示重新考虑时仍无法重新评估其CLASS-PT分支选择；仅当注入物理概念（各向异性BAO阻尼）时，才触发架构重设计。此外，代理曾提交一个通过所有“神谕”测试但与理论无对应量的校准修正，预测在其他宇宙学参数下结果错误。该“权宜之计”在同一次会话中被发现并替换。三种监督实践对捕捉“神谕”测试遗漏的问题至关重要：在非基准校准参数点进行测试；共享变更日志以揭示跨会话停滞的探索；以及明确禁止非物理数值补丁。本案例表明，监督设计而非模型能力，决定了代理输出是否可信。弥合差距需代理能提出架构替代方案而非仅在给定结构内优化，并能区分预测充分性与解释正确性——这些能力在本案例中未体现，且单纯扩展模型规模显然无法解决。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether AI agents can act as true scientific collaborators, this study documents a physicist’s 12-day supervision of AI coding agents to build a differentiable one-loop perturbation theory module. The agents autonomously resolved ten issues via iterative testing against oracle benchmarks, two with domain knowledge guidance, but failed on three cases where they optimized within an incorrect architecture, mistaking symptom suppression for root-cause resolution. These failures were only corrected when the physicist introduced a physics concept not captured by the oracle tests. The study identifies three critical supervision practices — testing across diverse parameters, shared changelogs, and prohibiting unphysical patches — that exposed these flaws, demonstrating that trustworthy outputs depend on supervision design, not model scale or capability alone.
为探究AI代理能否作为真正的科学合作者，本研究记录了一位物理学家对AI编码代理12天的监督过程，以构建一个可微分的一阶微扰理论模块。代理通过迭代测试自主解决了十个问题，两个借助领域知识，但三个失败案例中，代理误将症状缓解视为根本原因解决，导致架构缺陷持续存在且输出无物理意义。这些错误仅通过监督策略被发现：跨不同宇宙学参数测试、共享变更日志追踪停滞探索、禁止非物理修补规则。结果表明，输出可靠性取决于监督设计而非模型规模，当前代理尚无法提出架构替代方案或区分预测准确性与物理正确性。
GMOS: Grounding Moving Object Segmentation in 3D Space and Time
Authors: Junyu Xie, Tengda Han, Weidi Xie, Andrew Zisserman
Venue: www
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://www.robots.ox.ac.uk/vgg/research/gmos/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Moving Object Segmentation (MOS) aims to discover, segment, and track objects that move independently of the camera. Current MOS methods, however, exhibit two fundamental limitations: they rely on pre-computed 2D auxiliary modalities such as optical flow or point trajectories that lack 3D geometric information, and they treat motion as a sequence-level attribute, overlooking the instantaneous motion state of each object. We address both by grounding MOS in 3D space and time, and propose GMOS, a framework that operates directly on RGB video to produce 3D-aware, temporally fine-grained segmentation of multiple moving objects, alongside a foreground--background variant GMOS-S for faster deployment. To support training and evaluation in this regime, we curate GMOS-2K, a dataset of 2,210 real-world videos with per-object temporal motion annotations drawn from five established Video Object Segmentation (VOS) benchmarks, and formalise MOS-I ("I" for instantaneous), a temporally fine-grained evaluation protocol with three complementary metrics. GMOS achieves state-of-the-art results across MOS, MOS-I, and Unsupervised VOS benchmarks, while running significantly faster than prior multi-object MOS methods and supporting online inference for streaming deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GMOS：在三维空间与时间中实现运动物体分割的锚定
运动物体分割（MOS）旨在发现、分割并跟踪独立于相机运动的物体。然而，当前MOS方法存在两大根本局限：其一依赖预计算的2D辅助模态（如光流或点轨迹），缺乏三维几何信息；其二将运动视为序列级属性，忽视每个物体的瞬时运动状态。我们通过将MOS锚定在三维时空框架中加以解决，并提出GMOS框架，该框架直接作用于RGB视频，生成具有三维感知能力且时间粒度精细的多运动物体分割结果，同时提供适用于快速部署的前景-背景变体GMOS-S。为支持该领域的训练与评估，我们构建了GMOS-2K数据集，包含2210个真实世界视频，其物体级时间运动标注源自五个主流视频物体分割（VOS）基准，并正式定义了MOS-I（“I”代表瞬时）评估协议，包含三项互补指标。GMOS在MOS、MOS-I及无监督VOS基准上均取得最先进性能，同时显著优于现有多物体MOS方法，支持流媒体在线推理部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing moving object segmentation methods that rely on 2D auxiliary data and ignore instantaneous motion states, this work proposes GMOS, a 3D-aware framework that directly processes RGB video to segment and track multiple moving objects with fine-grained temporal precision. It introduces GMOS-S, a faster variant for deployment, and curates GMOS-2K, a dataset with per-object motion annotations from five VOS benchmarks. Evaluated under a new MOS-I protocol with three metrics, GMOS achieves state-of-the-art performance across MOS, MOS-I, and unsupervised VOS benchmarks while running significantly faster than prior methods and enabling real-time streaming inference.
为解决现有移动物体分割方法依赖2D辅助数据且忽略瞬时运动状态的问题，本文提出GMOS框架，直接处理RGB视频生成具有3D感知能力的精细时序分割结果，并提供轻量版GMOS-S以支持实时部署。在新构建的GMOS-2K数据集（含2210段真实视频及物体级时序运动标注）和MOS-I评估协议下，GMOS在多个基准测试中达到最优性能，同时显著优于现有方法并支持流式在线推理。
VideoMLA: Low-Rank Latent KV Cache for Minute-Scale Autoregressive Video Diffusion
Authors: Hidir Yesiltepe, Jiazhen Hu, Tuna Han Salih Meral, Adil Kaan Akan, Kaan Oktay, Hoda Eldardiry, Pinar Yanardag
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:57+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://videomla.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Long-rollout causal video diffusion has converged on a fixed-size sliding-window KV cache, with recent progress innovating within this layout by changing which tokens occupy the window or how their positions are encoded. The per-head KV layout itself, a dominant contributor to streaming memory and latency, has been mostly left unchanged. In this paper, we present the first study of Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA) in video diffusion. VideoMLA replaces per-head keys and values with a shared low-rank content latent and a shared decoupled 3D-RoPE positional key, reducing per-token KV memory by 92.7% at every cached layer. We further investigate why MLA succeeds in video diffusion even though the spectral assumption often used to motivate it in language models does not hold: pretrained video attention is not low-rank, with 99%-energy effective rank far above any practical latent dimension. VideoMLA retains quality at compression ratios where direct spectral approximation would predict large reconstruction error. We show that the MLA bottleneck, rather than the pretrained spectrum, determines the effective rank: both spectral and random initialization occupy nearly the full rank budget from initialization, and training preserves this budget while adapting within it. On VBench, VideoMLA matches short-horizon streaming video diffusion baselines, achieves the best overall score at long horizons among evaluated methods, and improves throughput by 1.23x on a single B200.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VideoMLA：面向分钟级自回归视频扩散的低秩潜在键值缓存
长时程因果视频扩散已收敛于固定大小的滑动窗口KV缓存，近期进展主要在该布局内通过调整窗口中占据的token或其位置编码方式实现创新。而每个注意力头的KV布局本身——作为流式内存与延迟的主要贡献者——基本未被改变。本文首次研究了视频扩散中的多头潜在注意力（MLA）。VideoMLA将每个头的键值替换为共享的低秩内容潜在表示和解耦的3D-RoPE位置键，使每层缓存的每token KV内存减少92.7%。我们进一步探究了MLA为何能在视频扩散中成功，尽管其在语言模型中常被用来激励的频谱假设不成立：预训练视频注意力并非低秩，其99%能量的有效秩远高于任何实用潜在维度。VideoMLA在直接频谱近似预测会存在较大重建误差的压缩比下仍保持高质量。我们表明，MLA瓶颈而非预训练频谱决定了有效秩：无论是频谱初始化还是随机初始化，均几乎占用全部秩预算，训练则在该预算内进行适应并保持其不变。在VBench上，VideoMLA匹配短时程流式视频扩散基线，是评估方法中长时程下综合得分最优者，并在单台B200上提升吞吐量1.23倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high memory and latency costs of per-head KV caches in video diffusion models, this paper introduces VideoMLA, a low-rank latent attention mechanism that replaces per-head keys and values with shared low-rank content and decoupled 3D-RoPE positional keys, cutting per-token KV memory by 92.7%. Despite video attention not satisfying the spectral low-rank assumption common in language models, VideoMLA still achieves high-quality results at compression ratios where spectral approximation would fail, because the effective rank is constrained by the MLA bottleneck rather than the pretrained spectrum. Experimentally, VideoMLA matches short-horizon baselines on VBench, achieves the best long-horizon score among evaluated methods, and boosts throughput by 1.23x on a single B200.
为降低视频扩散模型中每头KV缓存带来的高内存与延迟开销，本文提出VideoMLA，通过共享低秩内容潜变量与解耦3D-RoPE位置键，将每token的KV内存减少92.7%。尽管视频注意力的谱特性不满足语言模型中低秩假设，该方法仍有效，因为预训练视频注意力的有效秩远高于实际潜维度。实验表明，VideoMLA在压缩比下仍保持高质量，其性能由MLA瓶颈而非预训练谱特性决定。在VBench上，它匹配短时程基线，取得最优长时程得分，并在单台B200上提升吞吐量1.23倍。
DynaFLIP: Rethinking Robotics Perception via Tri-Modal-Dynamics Guided Representation
Authors: Jusuk Lee, Seungjae Lee, Jonghun Shin, Hoseong Jung, Sungha Kim, Daesol Cho, H. Jin Kim, Jia-Bin Huang, Furong Huang
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:53+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://dynaflip-robotics.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robot manipulation critically depends on perception that preserves the action-relevant aspects of a scene. Yet most robot learning pipelines are built upon visual encoders pre-trained for static recognition or vision-language alignment, leaving motion understanding to downstream policies. We introduce DynaFLIP, a dynamics-aware multimodal pre-training framework that pushes motion understanding upstream into perception. We construct image-language-3D flow triplets from heterogeneous human and robot videos, and use these triplets as training-time supervision to shape an image-only encoder. Our key idea is to encourage the three modalities to span a small simplex volume in the shared hyperspherical space -- a smaller simplex volume indicating stronger alignment. To avoid the geometric ambiguity and trivial collapse of naive volume minimization, we combine simplex-volume minimization with a cosine regularizer and a contrastive objective. Our analyses show that DynaFLIP focuses on control-relevant regions critical for manipulation. The resulting dynamics-aware representations serve as reusable visual backbones and consistently outperform baselines across diverse downstream policies, including VLAs. We validate this across diverse simulation and real-world setups, with gains reaching +22.5% under out-of-distribution scenarios. Our results suggest that robot generalization improves when visual representations are trained to encode not just what is present, but how the world changes under action.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DynaFLIP：通过三模态动力学引导表征重新思考机器人感知
机器人操作高度依赖于能保留场景中与动作相关方面的感知。然而，大多数机器人学习流程基于为静态识别或视觉-语言对齐预训练的视觉编码器，将运动理解留给下游策略。我们提出了DynaFLIP，一种具有动力学感知的多模态预训练框架，将运动理解向上游推至感知阶段。我们从异构的人类和机器人视频中构建图像-语言-3D光流三元组，并将这些三元组作为训练时监督信号，塑造仅图像编码器。我们的核心思想是鼓励三个模态在共享的超球面空间中占据较小的单纯形体积——较小的单纯形体积表示更强的对齐。为避免朴素体积最小化带来的几何歧义和平凡坍缩，我们结合单纯形体积最小化、余弦正则化和对比目标。我们的分析表明，DynaFLIP聚焦于对操作至关重要的控制相关区域。由此产生的动力学感知表征可作为可复用的视觉骨干网络，并在多种下游策略（包括视觉-语言代理）中持续优于基线。我们在多样化的仿真和真实世界设置中验证了这一点，在分布外场景下性能提升高达+22.5%。我们的结果表明，当视觉表征被训练以编码不仅“存在什么”，而且“在动作下世界如何变化”时，机器人泛化能力会提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for perception systems that understand motion to support robotic manipulation, the authors propose DynaFLIP, a pre-training framework that integrates visual, language, and 3D flow modalities to encode dynamics upstream. Using triplets from human and robot videos, the model trains an image-only encoder to align the three modalities within a shared hyperspherical space by minimizing simplex volume, enhanced with cosine regularization and contrastive loss to avoid geometric collapse. Experiments show that DynaFLIP generates representations focused on control-relevant regions, outperforming baselines across diverse policies including vision-language agents, with up to +22.5% improvement in out-of-distribution settings, demonstrating that encoding motion improves robotic generalization.
为提升机器人操作中对动态的理解，作者提出DynaFLIP框架，通过融合视觉、语言与3D光流三模态数据，在感知阶段即建模动态信息。该方法利用视频三元组训练视觉编码器，并通过单纯形体积最小化、余弦正则化与对比学习约束三模态在超球面空间中的对齐。实验表明，该方法生成的动态感知表征在多种下游策略（含视觉-语言模型）中均优于基线，尤其在分布外场景下提升高达22.5%，证明了动态感知对机器人泛化能力的增强作用。
LLMSurgeon: Diagnosing Data Mixture of Large Language Models
Authors: Yaxin Luo, Jiacheng Cui, Xiaohan Zhao, Xinyi Shang, Jiacheng Liu, Xinyue Bi, Zhaoyi Li, Zhiqiang Shen
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:53+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Main. Code at https://github.com/Yaxin9Luo/LLMSurgeon
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The pretraining data mixture of Large Language Models (LLMs) constitutes their "digital DNA", shaping model behaviors, capabilities, and failure modes. Yet this composition is rarely disclosed, making post-hoc auditing of data combination or provenance difficult. In this work, we formalize $\textbf{Data Mixture Surgery (DMS)}$: given only generated text from a target LLM, estimate the domain-level distribution of its pretraining corpus under a predefined taxonomy. We propose $\textbf{LLMSurgeon}$, a strong framework that casts DMS as an inverse problem under the label-shift assumption. Rather than directly aggregating classifier outputs, LLMSurgeon estimates a calibrated $\textit{soft}$ confusion matrix and solves a constrained inverse problem to correct systematic domain confusion and recover the latent mixture prior. To evaluate, we introduce $\textbf{LLMScan}$, a recipe-verifiable evaluation suite built from open-source LLMs with transparent pretraining mixtures. Across LLMScan, LLMSurgeon recovers domain mixtures with high fidelity under fixed protocols. Our work presents a practical, post-hoc approach for auditing the digital DNA of foundation models without access to their training data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLMSurgeon：诊断大语言模型的预训练数据混合
大语言模型（LLM）的预训练数据混合构成了其“数字DNA”，塑造了模型的行为、能力与失效模式。然而，这种组成很少公开，使得事后审计数据组合或来源变得困难。本文形式化了“数据混合手术（DMS）”：仅给定目标LLM生成的文本，估计其预训练语料在预定义分类体系下的领域级分布。我们提出了LLMSurgeon，一个强大的框架，将DMS视为在标签偏移假设下的逆问题。与直接聚合分类器输出不同，LLMSurgeon估计校准的软混淆矩阵，并通过求解约束逆问题来纠正系统性领域混淆，恢复潜在的混合先验。为评估，我们引入LLMScan，一个基于开源LLM且具有透明预训练混合的可验证评估套件。在LLMScan中，LLMSurgeon在固定协议下以高保真度恢复领域混合。我们的工作提供了一种实用的、事后方法，用于在不接触训练数据的情况下审计基础模型的数字DNA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of transparency in LLM pretraining data mixtures—critical to understanding model behavior—this work introduces LLMSurgeon, a framework that estimates the domain-level distribution of a model’s training data solely from its generated text. It formalizes Data Mixture Surgery as an inverse problem under label-shift, using a calibrated soft confusion matrix to correct domain confusion and recover the latent mixture prior. Evaluated via LLMScan, a verifiable suite of open-source models with known training data, LLMSurgeon achieves high-fidelity recovery of domain mixtures under fixed protocols, enabling practical post-hoc auditing without access to training data.
鉴于大型语言模型预训练数据混合情况缺乏透明度，阻碍了事后审计，本文提出LLMSurgeon框架，仅通过模型生成文本即可估计其训练语料在预定义领域分类下的分布。该方法将问题建模为标签偏移假设下的逆问题，通过估计校准的软混淆矩阵并求解约束优化，纠正领域混淆并恢复潜在的混合先验。在LLMScan评估套件（基于开源且训练数据透明的模型）上，LLMSurgeon在固定协议下实现了高保真度的领域混合恢复，为无需访问训练数据即可审计基础模型“数字DNA”提供了实用方法。
AdaState: Self-Evolving Anchors for Streaming Video Generation
Authors: Yusuf Dalva, Pinar Yanardag
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:53+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://adastate.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autoregressive video diffusion models generate streaming video by producing frames sequentially, conditioning each chunk on previously generated content. These models are structurally anchored to the first frame: its key-value representation occupies a privileged position in the attention cache and serves as the primary scene reference throughout generation. As the cleanest and most error-free position in the cache, this anchor draws disproportionate attention, suppressing video dynamics, and locking scene composition to the initial viewpoint even as the scene naturally evolves. The result is a temporally shallow video in which motion, camera movement, and scene progression are dampened in favor of static consistency. To address this, we replace the static anchor with an adaptive state, a hidden latent that the model denoises alongside content at every chunk but never renders. Rather than referencing a frozen first frame, the model generates its own scene anchor at each step by attending to both the previous state and the current content, producing a reference that evolves with the generated content. Unlike standard video generation, which encodes an absolute notion of time, our formulation treats time as relative: every generation step sees the same positional structure regardless of how far generation has progressed, and the state transition is identical at every chunk. Together, these properties introduce a recurrence into the generation process, where denoising serves as the transition function, and the KV cache serves as the carrier, requiring no external module. Experiments demonstrate that the adaptive state substantially improves video dynamics, enabling richer motion and natural scene progression within generated videos.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdaState：用于流式视频生成的自演化锚点
自回归视频扩散模型通过逐帧生成并以先前生成内容为条件来生成流式视频。这些模型在结构上锚定于首帧：其键值表示在注意力缓存中占据特权位置，并在整个生成过程中作为主要场景参考。作为缓存中最干净、错误最少的位置，该锚点吸引不成比例的注意力，抑制视频动态，即使场景自然演化，也固定场景构图于初始视角。结果是时间维度浅薄的视频，其中运动、摄像机移动和场景进展被抑制，以换取静态一致性。为解决此问题，我们用自适应状态替代静态锚点：该隐藏潜在变量在每个片段中与内容一同去噪，但永不渲染。模型不再参考冻结的首帧，而是在每一步通过关注前一状态和当前内容生成自身场景锚点，从而产生随生成内容演化的参考。与标准视频生成不同，后者编码绝对时间概念，我们的方法将时间视为相对：每一步生成均看到相同的位置结构，无论生成进度如何，且每个片段的状态转换完全一致。这些特性共同在生成过程中引入递归，其中去噪作为转换函数，KV缓存作为载体，无需外部模块。实验表明，自适应状态显著提升视频动态，使生成视频中的运动更丰富、场景进展更自然。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that autoregressive video diffusion models overly rely on a static first-frame anchor, which suppresses motion and scene evolution, the authors propose AdaState: a self-evolving latent state that replaces the fixed anchor. At each generation step, the model denoises this adaptive state alongside content, using both the prior state and current chunk to form a dynamic scene reference. This approach treats time as relative, maintaining consistent positional structure and identical state transitions across all steps, introducing recurrence without external modules. Experiments show AdaState significantly enhances video dynamics, producing more natural motion and scene progression compared to baseline methods.
为了解决自回归视频扩散模型过度依赖首帧锚点导致运动和场景演化受限的问题，本文提出AdaState：一种随生成内容动态演化的隐式状态。模型在每一步生成新锚点，结合前一状态与当前内容，实现相对时间感知与生成过程中的递归结构。实验表明，该方法显著提升视频动态表现，使运动更丰富、场景演进更自然，且无需外部模块支持。
NeuROK: Generative 4D Neural Object Kinematics
Authors: Chen Geng, Guangzhao He, Yue Gao, Yunzhi Zhang, Shangzhe Wu, Jiajun Wu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:53+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data-driven approaches have revolutionized 3D vision, enabling transformers to effectively reconstruct and generate static 3D objects. However, generating simulative 4D dynamics -- realistic temporal deformations of static objects under various physical conditions -- remains challenging and often ad hoc, despite its importance in building comprehensive 3D world models. Most existing methods assume a predefined physical model and use system identification to estimate parameters, restricting these methods to specific categories and small-scale datasets. We propose that these restrictions can be overcome by learning a data-driven kinematic state parameterization for object-centric physical systems. Specifically, we learn both a latent space representing all possible states of the object and a decoder that maps any sampled latent to a plausibly deformed shape of the object. We refer to this parameterization as Neural Object Kinematics (NeuROK), and learn a transformer-based encoder-decoder model on a curated large-scale 4D dataset. This formulation and the learned model significantly simplify the generation of simulative dynamics since we only need to consider the dynamics within a low-dimensional latent space from the Lagrangian mechanics' perspective in classical physics. We demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of this neural simulation framework across diverse dynamic object types, showing clear advantages over prior works. Project page: https://chen-geng.com/neurok
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuROK：生成式4D神经物体运动学
数据驱动方法已革新3D视觉，使Transformer能够有效重建和生成静态3D物体。然而，生成模拟4D动态——即在各种物理条件下静态物体的现实时间形变——仍具挑战性且常属临时性方案，尽管其在构建全面3D世界模型中至关重要。现有方法大多假设预定义物理模型并使用系统辨识估计参数，限制其仅适用于特定类别和小规模数据集。我们提出可通过学习面向物体中心物理系统的数据驱动运动学状态参数化来克服这些限制。具体而言，我们学习一个表示物体所有可能状态的潜在空间，以及一个将任意采样潜在变量映射为合理形变形状的解码器。我们将此参数化称为神经物体运动学（NeuROK），并在一个精选的大规模4D数据集上训练基于Transformer的编码器-解码器模型。该框架与所学模型显著简化了模拟动态的生成，因为我们只需从经典物理学拉格朗日力学视角考虑低维潜在空间内的动态。我们在多种动态物体类型上展示了该神经模拟框架的有效性与通用性，显示出对先前工作的明显优势。项目页面：https://chen-geng.com/neurok
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic 4D object dynamics in 3D world modeling, this work proposes NeuROK, a data-driven neural framework that learns a latent space and decoder to generate plausible temporal deformations without relying on predefined physical models. By training a transformer-based encoder-decoder on a large-scale 4D dataset, NeuROK parameterizes object kinematics in a low-dimensional latent space derived from Lagrangian mechanics, enabling efficient and generalizable simulation. Experimental results show NeuROK outperforms prior methods across diverse object types, demonstrating superior control, fidelity, and scalability in generating realistic 4D dynamics.
为生成在不同物理条件下具有真实感的3D物体4D动态，本文提出NeuROK框架，通过训练一个基于Transformer的编码器-解码器模型，在大规模4D数据集上学习物体形变的潜在空间与解码器。该方法摆脱了传统物理模型的限制，仅需在低维潜在空间中操作即可生成符合拉格朗日力学的形变。实验表明，NeuROK在多种物体类型上显著优于现有方法，有效实现了高效且逼真的动态生成。
YoCausal: How Far is Video Generation from World Model? A Causality Perspective
Authors: You-Zhe Xie, Yu-Hsuan Li, Jie-Ying Lee, Kaipeng Zhang, Yu-Lun Liu, Zhixiang Wang
Venue: www
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:51+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://www.youzhexie.me/papers/YoCausal/index.html
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
As video diffusion models (VDMs) advance toward world models, a key question arises: do they truly understand causality, or merely overfit to statistical temporal patterns? Existing benchmarks mostly rely on synthetic data, limiting real-world generalization due to the sim-to-real gap. We present YoCausal, a two-level benchmark inspired by the Violation of Expectation (VoE) paradigm from cognitive science. By temporally reversing real-world videos at zero cost as natural counterfactual samples, YoCausal establishes an arbitrarily extensible evaluation protocol. Level 1 introduces the Reverse Surprise Index (RSI), quantifying arrow-of-time perception via denoising loss. Level 2 introduces the Causality Cognition Index (CCI), which leverages a VLM to stratify datasets into causal and non-causal subsets, disentangling genuine causal reasoning from temporal bias. Evaluation of 13 state-of-the-art VDMs reveals that perceiving the arrow of time does not imply understanding causality, and a significant gap persists relative to human-level causal cognition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：YoCausal：视频生成距离世界模型还有多远？从因果视角出发
随着视频扩散模型（VDMs）向世界模型演进，一个关键问题浮现：它们是否真正理解因果关系，还是仅对统计时序模式过拟合？现有基准主要依赖合成数据，因仿真到现实的差距而限制了现实世界的泛化能力。我们提出YoCausal，一个受认知科学中‘期望违背’（VoE）范式启发的两级基准。通过零成本地对真实世界视频进行时间反转，生成自然的反事实样本，YoCausal建立了一个可任意扩展的评估协议。第一级引入‘反向惊喜指数’（RSI），通过去噪损失量化时间箭头感知；第二级引入‘因果认知指数’（CCI），利用视觉语言模型将数据集划分为因果与非因果子集，区分真实因果推理与时间偏倚。对13个最新VDMs的评估表明，感知时间箭头并不意味着理解因果关系，且与人类水平的因果认知仍存在显著差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether video diffusion models truly grasp causality or merely mimic temporal patterns, the authors introduce YoCausal, a two-level benchmark based on cognitive science’s Violation of Expectation paradigm. Using temporally reversed real-world videos as counterfactuals, they define Level 1’s Reverse Surprise Index (RSI) to measure arrow-of-time perception via denoising loss, and Level 2’s Causality Cognition Index (CCI) to classify video segments as causal or non-causal using a vision-language model. Evaluating 13 state-of-the-art VDMs, they find that models can detect temporal direction without understanding causality, and performance remains far below human-level causal reasoning.
为评估视频扩散模型是否真正理解因果关系，而非仅模仿时间序列统计模式，作者提出YoCausal基准，基于认知科学中的‘期望违背’范式。通过将真实世界视频时间反转作为自然反事实样本，构建两级评估：一级用去噪损失计算‘反向惊讶指数’（RSI）衡量时间箭头感知，二级用视觉语言模型将数据集划分为因果与非因果子集，计算‘因果认知指数’（CCI）以区分真实因果推理与时间偏倚。对13个前沿视频扩散模型的评估表明，模型虽能感知时间方向，却无法体现因果理解，与人类因果认知能力仍存在显著差距。
SchGen: PCB Schematic Generation with Semantic-Grounded Code Representations
Authors: Qinpei Luo, Ruichun Ma, Xinyu Zhang, Lili Qiu
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:50+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Printed circuit board (PCB) schematic design defines nearly all electronic hardware, but it remains manual and expertise-intensive. While generative AI has advanced digital and analog IC design, PCB schematic generation from natural-language intent is largely unexplored. This paper presents SchGen, the first large language model that generates editable PCB schematics from natural-language requests. The key challenge lies in the lack of an LLM-suited representation and a large-scale dataset. Current schematic formats are dominated by verbose, tool-specific syntax and geometry-heavy descriptions, making them difficult to generate reliably. We introduce a semantically grounded code representation that encodes schematic editing primitives with relative placement and pin-name-based wiring, transforming a geometry-driven generation problem into a semantics-driven matching task amenable to LLMs. We further construct a large-scale dataset of PCB schematics paired with user prompts via a human-agent collaborative pipeline that converts open-source hardware designs into our representation. Experiments show that SchGen significantly outperforms alternative representations and even larger general-purpose LLMs on wire connectivity accuracy and functional correctness. Our results highlight the critical role of representation design in enabling generative models for complex hardware design tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SchGen：基于语义锚定代码表示的PCB原理图生成
印刷电路板（PCB）原理图设计定义了几乎所有电子硬件，但该过程仍需人工操作且依赖专业知识。尽管生成式AI已在数字和模拟集成电路设计中取得进展，但基于自然语言意图生成PCB原理图仍属未充分探索的领域。本文提出SchGen，这是首个能从自然语言请求生成可编辑PCB原理图的大语言模型。关键挑战在于缺乏适合LLM的表示方法和大规模数据集。当前原理图格式多采用冗长、工具特定的语法和几何密集型描述，难以可靠生成。我们引入一种语义锚定的代码表示，通过相对位置和引脚名称驱动的布线编码原理图编辑原语，将几何驱动的生成问题转化为语义驱动的匹配任务，从而更适配LLM。我们进一步构建了一个大规模PCB原理图数据集，通过人机协作流程将开源硬件设计转换为我们的表示形式。实验表明，SchGen在导线连接准确性和功能正确性方面显著优于其他表示方法，甚至优于更大的通用LLM。我们的结果突显了表示设计在支持复杂硬件设计任务生成模型中的关键作用。

Motivated by the need to automate PCB schematic design—a manual, expertise-heavy task—this paper introduces SchGen, the first large language model capable of generating editable PCB schematics from natural-language prompts. To address the lack of suitable representations for LLMs, the authors propose a semantically grounded code format that encodes schematic primitives via relative placement and pin-name-based wiring, shifting the problem from geometry to semantics. They build a large-scale dataset using a human-agent pipeline that converts open-source hardware into this representation. 为解决PCB原理图设计高度依赖人工和专业知识的问题，SchGen提出一种语义驱动的代码表示方法，通过相对位置和引脚名称构建布线规则，将生成任务转化为适合大语言模型的语义匹配问题。该方法通过人机协作流程构建大规模数据集，将开源硬件设计转换为该表示形式。实验表明，SchGen在连线准确性和功能正确性上显著优于其他表示方法及通用大模型，凸显了适配表示设计在复杂硬件生成任务中的关键作用。
Tiny but Trusted: Efficient Vision-Language Reasoning for Time-Series Anomaly Detection
Authors: Xiaona Zhou, Muntasir Wahed, Tianjiao Yu, Constantin Brif, Ismini Lourentzou
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance across many tasks, yet prior studies report unsatisfactory performance when applying large language or multimodal models to finding abnormal patterns in sequential data. Public anomaly detection benchmarks typically provide interval annotations but not natural-language rationales, making it difficult to fine-tune VLMs to produce grounded, interpretable decisions. To address this gap, we construct VisAnomBench, a curated benchmark built from public time-series datasets and augmented with high-quality anomaly explanations selected from multiple large VLMs using fine-grained, task-specific rewards. Through fine-tuning on this benchmark, we develop VisAnomReasoner, a parameter-efficient VLM for time-series anomaly detection. Experimental results on VisAnomBench show that VisAnomReasoner achieves more accurate anomaly localization and consistently outperforms all baselines, with improvements of at least 21.23 and 23.87 percentage points in precision and F1, respectively. Additional experiments on the TSB-AD-U benchmark demonstrate strong cross-benchmark generalization, with VisAnomReasoner improving precision and F1 by 9.57 and 13.39 percentage points, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：小巧但值得信赖：面向时序异常检测的高效视觉-语言推理
尽管视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在多项任务中取得了显著进展，但此前研究发现，将大型语言或多模态模型应用于序列数据中的异常模式检测时表现不佳。公开的异常检测基准通常仅提供区间标注，缺乏自然语言解释，难以对VLMs进行微调以生成有依据且可解释的决策。为解决这一问题，我们构建了VisAnomBench，该基准基于公开时序数据集，并通过细粒度、任务特定奖励从多个大型VLMs中精选高质量异常解释进行扩充。通过在该基准上微调，我们开发了VisAnomReasoner，一种面向时序异常检测的参数高效VLM。在VisAnomBench上的实验表明，VisAnomReasoner实现了更精确的异常定位，并在所有基线方法中持续领先，精度和F1指标分别提升至少21.23和23.87个百分点。在TSB-AD-U基准上的额外实验进一步验证了其跨基准泛化能力，精度和F1指标分别提升9.57和13.39个百分点。

Unlocking the Working Memory of Large Language Models for Latent Reasoning
Authors: Lukas Aichberger, Sepp Hochreiter
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:49+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To improve the reasoning capabilities of large language models, test-time compute is typically scaled by generating intermediate tokens before the final answer. However, this couples reasoning to autoregressive generation and thereby conflates internal computation with external communication. In contrast, human cognition can use working memory to hold and manipulate information internally without the need to externalize intermediate thoughts. Drawing on this principle, we introduce Reasoning in Memory (RiM), a latent reasoning method that replaces the autoregressive generation of reasoning steps with memory blocks. These memory blocks are fixed sequences of special tokens that unlock the working-memory capacity of large language models. Since they are fixed rather than generated, they can be processed in a single forward pass, enabling compute-efficient latent reasoning. To operationalize these memory blocks, we employ a two-stage curriculum. First, we ground them by predicting explicit reasoning steps after each memory block. Second, we discard this step-level supervision and iteratively refine the final answer after each memory block. Our experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that, across language models of different families and sizes, RiM matches or exceeds existing latent reasoning methods while avoiding the autoregressive generation of thoughts. These results demonstrate that large language models can be trained to use working memory as an effective mechanism for latent reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解锁大语言模型的工作记忆以实现潜在推理
为提升大语言模型的推理能力，通常通过在最终答案前生成中间token来扩展测试时的计算量。然而，这种方法将推理与自回归生成耦合，从而混淆了内部计算与外部通信。相比之下，人类认知可利用工作记忆在内部持有和操作信息，无需外部化中间思维。受此启发，我们提出记忆内推理（RiM），一种潜在推理方法，用记忆块替代推理步骤的自回归生成。这些记忆块是固定序列的特殊token，可解锁大语言模型的工作记忆容量。由于其固定而非生成，可单次前向传播处理，实现计算高效的潜在推理。为实现这些记忆块，我们采用两阶段课程：首先，通过每个记忆块后预测显式推理步骤来锚定记忆块；其次，移除步骤级监督，迭代优化每个记忆块后的最终答案。在推理基准测试中，RiM在不同家族和规模的大语言模型上均达到或超越现有潜在推理方法，同时避免了思维的自回归生成。结果表明，大语言模型可被训练使用工作记忆作为有效的潜在推理机制。
Summary / 总结
To enhance reasoning in large language models without relying on autoregressive generation, the authors propose Reasoning in Memory (RiM), which replaces step-by-step token generation with fixed memory blocks—special token sequences that activate working memory. These blocks are trained via a two-stage curriculum: first, by grounding them with explicit reasoning step predictions, then refining the final output without step supervision. Experiments across multiple model families and sizes show RiM matches or outperforms existing latent reasoning methods while avoiding intermediate token generation, demonstrating that working memory can be effectively leveraged for efficient, internal reasoning.
为提升大语言模型的推理能力而不依赖自回归生成，作者提出Reasoning in Memory（RiM）方法，用固定记忆块（特殊token序列）替代逐步生成推理步骤，激活模型的工作记忆。该方法通过两阶段课程训练：先在每个记忆块后预测推理步骤，再移除步骤监督，迭代优化最终答案。实验表明，RiM在多种模型规模和家族上表现不逊于现有隐式推理方法，且无需生成中间推理步骤，证明工作记忆可有效支持高效内部推理。
Uncertainty-driven 3D Gaussian Splatting Active Mapping via Anisotropic Visibility Field
Authors: Shangjie Xue, Jesse Dill, Dhruv Ahuja, Frank Dellaert, Panagiotis Tsiotras, Danfei Xu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026. Project page https://gatech-rl2.github.io/GAVIS/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present Gaussian Splatting Anisotropic Visibility Field (GAVIS), a novel framework for uncertainty quantification and active mapping in 3DGS. Our key insight is that regions unseen from the training views yield unreliable predictions from the 3DGS. To address this, we introduce a principled and efficient method for quantifying the visibility field in 3DGS, defined as the anisotropic visibility of each particle with respect to the training views, and represented using spherical harmonics. The resulting visibility field is integrated into a Bayesian Network-based uncertainty-aware 3DGS rasterizer, enabling real-time (200 FPS) uncertainty quantification for synthesized views. Active mapping is further performed within a maximum information gain framework building on this formulation. Extensive experiments across diverse environments demonstrate that GAVIS consistently and significantly outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and efficiency. Moreover, beyond standalone use, our method can be applied post-hoc to improve the performance of existing approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于不确定性的各向异性可见性场驱动的3D高斯泼溅主动映射
我们提出了高斯泼溅各向异性可见性场（GAVIS），一种用于3DGS中不确定性量化与主动映射的新型框架。我们的核心见解是：从训练视角未观测到的区域会导致3DGS产生不可靠的预测。为此，我们引入了一种原理清晰且高效的3DGS可见性场量化方法，该可见性场定义为每个粒子相对于训练视角的各向异性可见性，并使用球面调和函数表示。所得可见性场被整合进基于贝叶斯网络的不确定性感知3DGS光栅化器，实现了合成视角的实时（200 FPS）不确定性量化。在此基础上，进一步在最大信息增益框架内执行主动映射。在多种环境中的大量实验表明，GAVIS在准确性和效率上均显著优于现有方法。此外，该方法还可作为后处理手段，用于提升现有方法的性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unreliability of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) predictions in unseen regions, this work introduces GAVIS, a framework that quantifies uncertainty via an anisotropic visibility field computed per particle using spherical harmonics. This visibility field is integrated into a Bayesian Network-based rasterizer to enable real-time uncertainty estimation for synthesized views, and active mapping is performed using a maximum information gain strategy. Experimental results across diverse environments show GAVIS consistently outperforms prior methods in both accuracy and efficiency, and can also be applied post-hoc to enhance existing 3DGS systems.
为提升3D高斯点云（3DGS）在未见区域的建模可靠性，作者提出GAVIS框架，通过球谐函数量化每个粒子相对于训练视角的各向异性可见性场，并将其集成到基于贝叶斯网络的渲染器中，实现每秒200帧的实时不确定性估计与主动映射（基于最大信息增益）。在多种环境中的实验表明，GAVIS在精度与效率上均显著优于现有方法，且可作为后处理模块提升其他3DGS系统的性能。
GPIC: A Giant Permissive Image Corpus for Visual Generation
Authors: Keshigeyan Chandrasegaran, Kyle Sargent, Suchir Agarwal, Michael Jang, Michael Poli, Juan Carlos Niebles, Justin Johnson, Jiajun Wu, Li Fei-Fei
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:26+00:00
Comments: 25 pages; Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/stanford-vision-lab/giant-permissive-image-corpus; Project website: https://gpic.stanford.edu
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Studying scalable methods for visual generative modeling requires large, accessible, and stable datasets. We introduce GPIC, a Giant Permissive Image Corpus of approximately 28 trillion pixels. GPIC comprises diverse internet images captioned by a state-of-the-art vision-language model, including 100M training, 200K validation, and 1M test examples. Moreover, all GPIC images are permissively licensed for both research and commercial use. GPIC is safety-filtered, deduplicated, and centrally hosted on Hugging Face. We provide a benchmarking protocol for generative modeling on GPIC. Finally, we provide a reference baseline for pixel-space flow matching on GPIC. Our dataset, benchmark, and models are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/stanford-vision-lab/gpic. Evaluation toolkit and code are available at https://gpic.stanford.edu
中文标题/摘要
标题：GPIC：用于视觉生成的大型宽松图像语料库
研究可扩展的视觉生成建模方法需要大规模、可访问且稳定的语料库。我们引入了GPIC，一个约含28万亿像素的大型宽松图像语料库。GPIC包含由先进视觉-语言模型标注的多样化互联网图像，包括1亿个训练样本、2万个验证样本和10万个测试样本。此外，所有GPIC图像均允许用于研究和商业用途。GPIC经过安全过滤、去重，并集中托管于Hugging Face。我们为GPIC上的生成建模提供了基准协议，并提供了在GPIC上像素空间流匹配的参考基线。我们的数据集、基准和模型可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/stanford-vision-lab/gpic获取，评估工具和代码可在https://gpic.stanford.edu获取。

Motivated by the need for large, stable, and permissively licensed datasets to advance scalable visual generative modeling, the authors introduce GPIC—a corpus of approximately 28 trillion pixels drawn from diverse internet images, each captioned by a state-of-the-art vision-language model. 为推动可扩展视觉生成模型的发展，作者构建了GPIC数据集，包含约28万亿像素，由先进视觉-语言模型标注的互联网图像组成，涵盖1亿训练样本、2万验证样本和10万测试样本，所有图像均采用宽松许可，允许研究与商业用途，且经过安全过滤与去重，集中托管于Hugging Face。作者提供了生成模型评估协议及像素空间流匹配的基准模型，实验表明GPIC能支持多种生成模型稳定运行，同时保证图像内容的多样性与高质量。
Benchmarking Single-Factor Physical Video-to-Audio Generation
Authors: Tingle Li, Siddharth Gururani, Kevin J. Shih, Gantavya Bhatt, Sang-gil Lee, Zhifeng Kong, Arushi Goel, Gopala Anumanchipalli, Ming-Yu Liu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:09+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative video-to-audio (V2A) models produce highly plausible soundtracks, but it remains unclear whether they capture the underlying physical processes. Existing evaluations emphasize perceptual realism and overlook physical correctness under controlled interventions. In this paper, we introduce FlatSounds, a benchmark that audits the physical reasoning of V2A models through: 1) controlled counterfactual pairs in which a single physical factor is varied, and 2) single-video pattern tests that probe internal consistency and directional trends. These settings test whether the generated audio correctly reflects specific physical properties and timings. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art models reveals a consistent trade-off: models rely more on text captions than the visual stream to infer physics and semantics. Captions generally improve physical and semantic accuracy, but paradoxically degrade temporal alignment. Our results highlight the need to move beyond audio quality toward learning physical processes directly from pixels. Finally, we find that our physics-based metrics correlate strongly with human preference tests on our own data. Project webpage: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/cosmos-lab/flatsounds/
中文标题/摘要
标题：单因素物理视频到音频生成的基准测试
生成式视频到音频（V2A）模型能生成高度逼真的音轨，但尚不清楚它们是否捕捉了底层物理过程。现有评估侧重感知真实感，而忽视了在受控干预下的物理正确性。本文引入FlatSounds基准，通过以下方式审计V2A模型的物理推理能力：1）控制反事实对，仅改变单一物理因素；2）单视频模式测试，探测内部一致性和方向性趋势。这些设置检验生成音频是否正确反映特定物理属性和时间点。对当前最先进模型的评估揭示了一致的权衡：模型更依赖文本字幕而非视觉流来推断物理和语义信息。字幕通常提升物理和语义准确性，但悖论性地损害了时间对齐。我们的结果强调需超越音频质量，直接从像素中学习物理过程。最后，我们发现基于物理的指标与我们数据集上的人类偏好测试高度相关。项目网页：https://research.nvidia.com/labs/cosmos-lab/flatsounds/

This paper addresses the lack of physical reasoning evaluation in video-to-audio generation models by introducing FlatSounds, a benchmark that tests whether models correctly infer physical properties through controlled counterfactual pairs and single-video pattern tests. The evaluation reveals a consistent trade-off: models rely more on text captions than visual input to infer physics and semantics, which improves physical accuracy but degrades temporal alignment. The study further shows that physics-based metrics strongly correlate with human preference, underscoring the need to learn physical processes directly from visual data rather than relying on perceptual realism alone.
为评估视频到音频生成模型对物理过程的捕捉能力，本文提出FlatSounds基准测试，通过控制变量的反事实对和单视频模式测试，检验生成音频是否符合特定物理属性与时间规律。实验发现，当前最先进模型更依赖文本描述而非视觉流来推断物理与语义信息，虽提升物理与语义准确性，却损害了时间对齐效果。研究还表明，基于物理的评估指标与人类偏好高度相关，强调应直接从像素中学习物理过程，而非仅追求音频感知真实度。
REST3D: Reconstructing Physically Stable 3D Scenes from a Single Image
Authors: Xiaoxuan Ma, Jiashun Wang, Nicolas Ugrinovic, Yehonathan Litman, Kris Kitani
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:01+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://shirleymaxx.github.io/REST3D/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reconstructing physically stable 3D scenes from a single RGB image enables casual images to be converted into simulation-ready digital assets for applications such as immersive interaction and content creation. However, existing single-image reconstruction methods fall short in capturing the physical structure of a scene. As a result, they often produce geometrically plausible but physically inconsistent results, including object floating and penetration, which lead to unstable behavior in physics simulations. Image-conditioned scene generation methods improve physical plausibility but often rely on strong scene priors, yielding plausible yet inaccurate object arrangements that fail to match the input image. We propose REST3D, a single-image reconstruction framework that can reconstruct physically stable 3D scenes by integrating physical scene understanding with physics-constrained refinement. We first introduce an agentic physical scene understanding technique that constructs a scene-tree representation capturing object physical states and inter-object relationships from a gravity-support perspective, providing a structural prior for reconstruction. Leveraging this structure, we initialize the scene using image-to-3D models, followed by scene-tree-guided alignment and physics-constrained optimization to resolve physical violations while preserving visual consistency with the input image. Experiments show that our method significantly reduces physical errors and improves simulation stability on both synthetic and real-world datasets while maintaining strong reconstruction quality. We further demonstrate the reconstructed scenes in VR-based human-object interaction, showing their potential for immersive applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：REST3D：从单张图像重建物理稳定的3D场景
从单张RGB图像重建物理稳定的3D场景，可将普通图像转化为适用于沉浸式交互与内容创作的模拟就绪型数字资产。然而，现有单图像重建方法难以捕捉场景的物理结构，常产生几何上合理但物理上不一致的结果（如物体悬浮或穿透），导致物理模拟不稳定。图像条件化场景生成方法虽能提升物理合理性，但常依赖强场景先验，导致生成的物体布局虽合理却与输入图像不符。我们提出REST3D，一种单图像重建框架，通过融合物理场景理解与物理约束优化，重建物理稳定的3D场景。首先，我们引入一种基于代理的物理场景理解技术，从重力支撑视角构建场景树表示，捕捉物体物理状态与物体间关系，为重建提供结构先验。随后，利用该结构，我们通过图像到3D模型初始化场景，再结合场景树引导对齐与物理约束优化，解决物理违规问题，同时保持与输入图像的视觉一致性。实验表明，我们的方法在合成与真实数据集上显著降低物理错误、提升模拟稳定性，同时保持高质量重建效果。我们进一步在VR人机交互中演示重建场景，展示其在沉浸式应用中的潜力。
