Motivated by the need to generate physically stable 3D scenes from single images for simulation and immersive applications, REST3D introduces a framework that combines physical scene understanding with physics-constrained refinement. It first constructs a scene-tree representation capturing object relationships and gravity-support dynamics to guide reconstruction, then initializes the 3D scene from image-to-3D models and refines it using physics-aware optimization while preserving visual fidelity. Experimental results demonstrate significant reductions in physical violations like floating or penetration, improved simulation stability across synthetic and real datasets, and maintained high reconstruction quality, validated through VR-based human-object interaction.
为实现从单张图像重建物理稳定的3D场景以支持仿真与沉浸式应用，REST3D提出融合物理场景理解与物理约束优化的方法。首先构建捕捉物体间关系与重力支撑结构的场景树作为先验，再结合图像到3D模型的初始几何，通过场景树引导对齐与物理约束优化消除物理矛盾，同时保持视觉一致性。实验表明该方法在合成与真实数据集上显著降低物理错误、提升仿真稳定性，并在VR人机交互中验证了重建场景的有效性。
Efficient Test-Time Finetuning of LLMs via Convex Reconstruction and Gradient Caching
Authors: Alaa Khamis, Alaa Maalouf
First: 2026-05-28T17:59:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:59:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Test-time finetuning (TTFT) is a rapidly evolving paradigm that adapts a language model to each prompt by retrieving related sequences, updating the model on them, and then evaluating the prompt. However, TTFT is only practical if it is fast: selection and finetuning both happen per query, making each a direct bottleneck. Existing methods trade speed for quality: fast retrieval is often redundant, while stronger diversity-aware selection adds prohibitive per-query cost. We introduce HullFT, a geometric approach to TTFT that addresses both bottlenecks. Given a query, HullFT first represents the query embedding as a sparse convex combination of few training sequences, using efficient projection-free Frank-Wolfe optimization. This yields a support set that is inherently relevant and diverse. We then convert the fractional convex weights into an exact integer multiset for finetuning through a geometric integerization procedure. The resulting multiplicities naturally create repeated examples, which we exploit with Gradient Reuse to amortize forward-backward computation across repeated finetuning steps. Our experiments show that HullFT improves the quality-efficiency tradeoff over current state-of-the-art TTFT methods, achieving lower bits-per-byte at substantially lower total runtime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过凸重构与梯度缓存高效实现大语言模型测试时微调
测试时微调（TTFT）是一种快速发展的范式，它通过检索相关序列、在这些序列上更新模型，然后评估提示来适应语言模型。然而，TTFT仅在快速执行时才实用：检索和微调均按每个查询进行，成为直接瓶颈。现有方法在速度与质量间做权衡：快速检索常冗余，而更强的多样性感知选择则增加高昂的每查询成本。我们提出HullFT，一种几何方法，同时解决这两个瓶颈。给定查询，HullFT首先使用高效的无投影Frank-Wolfe优化，将查询嵌入表示为少数训练序列的稀疏凸组合。这生成一个内在相关且多样化的支持集。接着，我们通过几何整数化过程，将分数凸权重转换为精确的整数多重集以进行微调。由此产生的多重性自然产生重复示例，我们利用梯度重用在重复微调步骤间摊销前向-反向计算。实验表明，HullFT在当前最先进的TTFT方法之上改善了质量-效率权衡，实现更低的每字节比特数，且总运行时间显著降低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accelerate test-time finetuning of large language models without sacrificing quality, the authors propose HullFT, a geometric method that combines convex reconstruction and gradient caching. For each query, it represents the query embedding as a sparse convex combination of a few training sequences via Frank-Wolfe optimization, then converts the fractional weights into integer multiplicities to form a relevant and diverse support set. During finetuning, it reuses gradients across repeated examples to amortize computation, reducing per-query cost. Experiments show HullFT achieves higher quality per unit time, with lower bits-per-byte and significantly reduced total runtime compared to prior methods.
为提升大语言模型在测试时的高效微调能力，本文提出HullFT方法，结合凸重构与梯度缓存技术，在保证质量的同时提升效率。针对查询，HullFT通过Frank-Wolfe优化将其嵌入表示为少量训练序列的稀疏凸组合，生成自然相关且多样化的支持集；随后将分数权重转为整数多重集用于微调，利用重复样本实现梯度复用以摊销计算开销。实验表明，HullFT在比特每字节指标上优于现有方法，同时大幅降低总运行时间。
Fairness-Aware Federated Learning with Trajectory Shapley Value
Authors: Daniel Kuznetsov, Ziqi Wang
First: 2026-05-28T17:58:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:58:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at the 24th European Control Conference (ECC 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning is an emerging distributed paradigm that addresses the challenges posed by heterogeneous, privacy-sensitive data. It enables multiple clients to train a model collaboratively by aggregating their local updates at a server. However, conventional aggregation schemes typically use fixed weights that fail to reflect unequal and time-varying client contributions, leading to biased and unstable learning. To improve fairness and stability, we propose the Trajectory Shapley Value (TSV), a contribution metric that evaluates how each client influences the optimization trajectory of the global model using a validation-based, temporally consistent utility. Building on TSV, we design FedTSV, an adaptive aggregation method that converts per-round evaluations into dynamic client weights, allowing the server to respond to heterogeneous and adversarial participation in real time. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that FedTSV accelerates convergence, improves robustness, and yields more equitable contribution assessments, thereby providing a principled foundation for fairness-aware federated optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于轨迹夏普利值的公平感知联邦学习
联邦学习是一种新兴的分布式范式，旨在应对异构且隐私敏感数据带来的挑战。它通过在服务器端聚合各客户端的本地更新，使多个客户端协同训练模型。然而，传统聚合方案通常使用固定权重，无法反映客户端贡献的不均衡性和时变性，导致学习过程偏斜且不稳定。为提升公平性和稳定性，我们提出轨迹夏普利值（TSV），一种基于验证、时间一致性的效用评估指标，用于衡量每个客户端对全局模型优化轨迹的影响。基于TSV，我们设计了自适应聚合方法FedTSV，将每轮评估转化为动态客户端权重，使服务器能实时响应异构且对抗性的参与行为。在基准数据集上的实验表明，FedTSV可加速收敛、提升鲁棒性，并实现更公平的贡献评估，为公平感知联邦优化提供了理论基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to address unfair and unstable model training in federated learning due to fixed-weight aggregation, this work introduces the Trajectory Shapley Value (TSV) to measure each client’s dynamic contribution to the global model’s optimization trajectory. Based on TSV, FedTSV adapts client weights per round using validation-based, temporally consistent utility evaluations, enabling real-time response to heterogeneous and adversarial participation. Experimental results on benchmark datasets demonstrate that FedTSV improves convergence speed, enhances robustness, and delivers more equitable contribution assessments compared to conventional methods.
为了解决联邦学习中固定权重聚合导致的训练偏倚与不稳定性问题，本文提出轨迹夏普利值（TSV），这是一种基于验证、时间一致的贡献度量，用于评估每个客户端对全局模型优化轨迹的影响。在此基础上设计了自适应聚合方法FedTSV，按轮次动态调整客户端权重，实现对异构与对抗性参与的实时响应。实验表明，FedTSV在基准数据集上显著加速收敛、提升模型鲁棒性，并实现更公平的贡献评估。
Locally Coherent, Globally Incoherent: Bounding Compositional Incoherence in Multi-Component LLM Agents
Authors: Anany Kotawala
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:58:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:58:55+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 7 figures, 24 tables. Preliminary versions to appear at the ICML 2026 Workshops on Combining Theory and Benchmarks (CTB), Statistical Frameworks for Uncertainty in Agentic Systems (AgenticUQ), and Failure Modes of Agentic AI (FAGEN)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-component LLM agents assemble probabilistic claims from components that each see only part of a joint problem; the composition can violate basic probability axioms even when every component is locally coherent. We formalise this locally coherent, globally incoherent failure via the compositional residual eps*, the L2 distance from the composed quote to the joint coherent polytope, computable at runtime from system output and the declared cross-component coupling constraints. A product-structure dichotomy characterises when local coherence suffices, and a Rayleigh-quotient prediction matches the observed residual within 7% on three of four relation classes. A hierarchical Boyle-Dykstra projection repairs the composition deterministically; an anytime-valid e-process gives sequential coherence monitoring. Across 1,876 ensemble cliques on a four-LLM mid-tier panel (frontier-panel rerun in Section 5.5), eps* > 0 on 33-94% of cliques, translating to +0.115 nats per bet of regret on 1,770 resolved bets under the proportional allocation rule (the gain collapses to +0.006 under bettors that themselves coherentise). Three intuitive LLM-side mitigations(retrieval, partition-aware prompting, aggregator-LLM) each fail or regress.
中文标题/摘要
标题：局部一致、全局不一致：多组件大语言模型代理中组合不一致性的界定
多组件大语言模型代理从仅能观察联合问题部分的组件中组装概率性主张；即使每个组件局部一致，组合仍可能违反基本概率公理。我们通过组合残差eps*形式化这种局部一致、全局不一致的失效，eps*是组合主张到联合一致多面体的L2距离，可从系统输出和声明的跨组件耦合约束在运行时计算。一种乘积结构二分法刻画了局部一致性何时足够，且Rayleigh商预测在四类关系中的三类内与观测残差误差不超过7%。一种分层Boyle-Dykstra投影可确定性修复组合；一种任意时间有效的e过程提供序列一致性监控。在由四个大语言模型组成的中等层级面板（第5.5节中的前沿面板重跑）的1,876个组合群体中，eps*在33%-94%的群体中大于0，按比例分配规则在1,770个已解决赌注中转化为每赌注+0.115纳特的遗憾收益（若赌注者自身实现一致性，该收益降至+0.006）。三种直观的大语言模型侧缓解措施（检索、分区感知提示、聚合大语言模型）均失败或退化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that multi-component LLM agents can produce globally incoherent outputs despite each component being locally coherent, the paper introduces the compositional residual eps* to quantify global incoherence as the L2 distance from the combined output to the joint coherent polytope. It identifies a product-structure dichotomy that determines when local coherence suffices and uses a Rayleigh-quotient to predict eps* with 7% accuracy across three of four relation classes. The authors propose a hierarchical Boyle-Dykstra projection to deterministically repair incoherence and an anytime-valid e-process for sequential monitoring. Experiments on 1,876 ensemble cliques across a four-LLM panel show eps* > 0 in 33–94% of cases, translating to +0.115 nats per bet of regret under proportional allocation, which drops to +0.006 when bettors themselves enforce coherence. Three intuitive LLM-side mitigations—retrieval, partition-aware prompting, and aggregator-LLM—all failed or regressed.
该论文研究了多组件LLM代理中局部一致但全局不一致的问题，即各组件虽局部满足概率公理，但组合输出可能违反基本概率公理。作者提出组合残差eps*，即组合输出与联合一致多面体的L2距离，可从系统输出和声明的组件耦合约束实时计算。他们通过乘积结构二分法判断局部一致性是否足够，并发现Rayleigh商预测在四类关系中的三类与观测残差误差小于7%。提出分层Boyle-Dykstra投影以确定性修复不一致，同时e过程支持序列一致性监控。在1876个四LLM中型面板的组合测试中，eps*在33%至94%的组合中大于0，按比例分配规则对应每注0.115纳特的后悔值，若投注者自身强制一致性则降至0.006。三种直观的LLM侧缓解措施（检索、分区提示、聚合LLM）均无效或恶化。
Demystifying Data Organization for Enhanced LLM Training
Authors: Yalun Dai, Yangyu Huang, Tongshen Yang, Yonghan Wang, Xin Zhang, Wenshan Wu, Qihao Zhao, Hao Li, Yuanyuan Gao, Kim-Hui Yap, Scarlett Li
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:58:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:58:53+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized various fields, yet their training efficiency is heavily reliant on effective data curation. While data selection has been widely studied, the strategic data organization for enhanced training remains an underexplored area, particularly since current LLMs are often trained for only one or a few epochs. This paper systematically explores the influence of data organization on LLM training by reusing pre-computed sample-level scores originally generated for data efficiency, thereby incurring minimal additional computational overhead. We identify and formalize four key guidelines for optimizing data organization: Boundary Sharpening, Cyclic Scheduling, Curriculum Continuity, and Local Diversity. Guided by them, we introduce two novel data ordering methods termed STR and SAW. Extensive experiments across different model scales and data sizes, encompassing both pre-training and SFT stages, validate the effectiveness of our summarized guidelines. They also demonstrate the robustness of our proposed data ordering methods in enhancing the stability and performance of LLM training. Github Link: https://github.com/microsoft/data-efficacy/
中文标题/摘要
标题：揭开数据组织之谜以提升大语言模型训练效果
大语言模型（LLMs）已革新多个领域，但其训练效率高度依赖有效数据整理。尽管数据选择已广泛研究，针对提升训练效果的策略性数据组织仍属探索不足的领域，尤其因当前LLMs常仅训练一至数个周期。本文通过重用为数据效率预先计算的样本级评分，系统性探讨数据组织对LLM训练的影响，且额外计算开销极小。我们识别并形式化了四项优化数据组织的关键准则：边界锐化、循环调度、课程连续性与局部多样性。据此，我们提出两种新颖的数据排序方法：STR与SAW。在不同模型规模与数据量下，涵盖预训练与SFT阶段的大量实验验证了我们总结准则的有效性，亦证明所提数据排序方法在提升LLM训练稳定性与性能方面的鲁棒性。GitHub链接：https://github.com/microsoft/data-efficacy/
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the underexplored impact of data organization on LLM training efficiency, especially given that modern LLMs are often trained for only one or few epochs. It leverages pre-computed sample-level scores to propose four formal guidelines—Boundary Sharpening, Cyclic Scheduling, Curriculum Continuity, and Local Diversity—to optimize data ordering, introducing two novel methods, STR and SAW. Experimental results across multiple model scales and training stages confirm that these guidelines significantly improve training stability and performance without substantial computational cost.
本文探讨了数据组织对大语言模型训练效率的影响，尤其针对当前模型常仅训练一到几轮的情况。提出两种新型数据排序方法STR和SAW，依据四个正式化准则：边界锐化、循环调度、课程连续性和局部多样性。这些方法利用预计算的样本级评分，无需额外计算开销，实验在不同模型规模和训练阶段（预训练与SFT）均验证了其对训练稳定性和性能的提升效果。
COMPOSE: Composing Future Theorems from Citations and Formal Structure
Authors: David Busbib, Michael Werman
First: 2026-05-28T17:58:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:58:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
A plausible future mathematical claim must satisfy two constraints: it should follow the direction of prior work and respect the formal dependencies that constrain what can validly follow. Existing approaches typically model only one of these sources, producing claims that are either weakly grounded or insufficiently motivated. We introduce grounded future mathematical generation, where the goal is to generate a plausible future theorem-like claim for an anchor paper using two complementary sources of context: its scientific citation graph and aligned formal theorem dependency graph. To address this setting, we propose COMPOSE, a dual-graph framework that conditions a language model on both scientific citation context and formal theorem structure. To support this setting, we construct a dataset of 108K paired scientific-formal graph examples from arXiv and Mathlib, together with a benchmark of 47K future papers from 2024--2025. Experiments show that COMPOSE outperforms strong baselines on retrieval to real future papers and achieves the best overall performance under LLM-judge evaluation, producing more grounded and mathematically richer outputs. These results show that future mathematical generation benefits from combining scientific context with formal structure. Project page is available at https://david-busbib.github.io/COMPOSE-page/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：COMPOSE：从引用与形式结构生成未来定理
一个合理的未来数学命题必须满足两个约束：应延续先前工作方向，并尊重限制有效推导的形式依赖关系。现有方法通常仅建模其中一个来源，导致生成的命题要么根基薄弱，要么动机不足。我们提出基于锚定论文的合理未来数学生成方法，利用两种互补上下文来源：科学引用图与对齐的形式定理依赖图。为应对这一设定，我们提出COMPOSE，一种双图框架，使语言模型同时依赖科学引用上下文与形式定理结构。为支持该设定，我们从arXiv和Mathlib构建了包含10.8万个科学-形式图配对示例的数据集，并配套构建了2024-2025年4.7万篇未来论文的基准测试集。实验表明，COMPOSE在检索真实未来论文方面优于强基线方法，并在LLM评审评估中取得最佳整体表现，生成内容更具根基性与数学丰富性。结果表明，结合科学上下文与形式结构可提升未来数学生成效果。项目页面见https://david-busbib.github.io/COMPOSE-page/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate mathematically plausible future theorems grounded in both scientific context and formal structure, COMPOSE introduces a dual-graph framework that conditions a language model on citation graphs and formal theorem dependency graphs. Evaluated on a dataset of 108K paired graph examples and a benchmark of 47K real future papers from 2024–2025, COMPOSE outperforms existing baselines in retrieving actual future papers and achieves top performance under LLM-judge evaluation, producing outputs that are more grounded and mathematically richer.
为生成既符合科学演进方向又尊重形式逻辑依赖的未来数学命题，作者提出COMPOSE框架，该框架通过同时利用科学引文图谱与形式定理依赖图谱来引导语言模型生成。在包含10.8万对科学-形式图谱样本及4.7万篇真实未来论文的基准上，COMPOSE在检索真实未来论文和LLM评委评估中均优于现有基线方法，表明融合科学语境与形式结构能显著提升未来数学命题的合理性与数学深度。
Colored Noise Diffusion Sampling
Authors: Hadar Davidson, Noam Issachar, Sagie Benaim
First: 2026-05-28T17:58:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:58:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Diffusion models achieve state-of-the-art image synthesis, with their generative trajectories fundamentally exhibiting a spectral bias, resolving low-frequency global structures early and high-frequency fine details later. Conventional stochastic differential equation (SDE) solvers fail to account for this dynamic, naively injecting uniform white noise throughout the entire process and misusing the finite energy budget. In this work, we establish a mathematical framework that reconsiders SDE inference as a targeted, frequency-decoupled energy transfer. Leveraging this framework, we introduce Colored Noise Sampling (CNS), a novel, training-free stochastic solver. Rather than injecting uniform white noise, CNS utilizes a dynamic, timestep- and frequency-dependent schedule that more efficiently allocates injected energy toward structurally unresolved frequency bands. By actively exploiting the model's inherent spectral bias, CNS systematically steers the generated distribution toward the true data manifold. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CNS significantly outperforms standard ODE and SDE baselines as a strictly plug-and-play, inference-time sampler substitution across diverse architectures (SiT, JiT, FLUX). Compared to standard sampling on ImageNet-256, CNS achieves substantial unguided FID reductions, improving from 8.26 to 6.27 on SiT-XL/2, 32.39 to 26.69 on JiT-B/16, and 11.88 to 8.31 on JiT-H/16, while yielding consistent relative FID improvements with Classifier-Free Guidance. Project page is available at https://hadardavidson.github.io/CNS/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：彩色噪声扩散采样
扩散模型在图像合成方面达到最先进水平，其生成轨迹本质上具有频谱偏置，早期解决低频全局结构，后期处理高频细节。传统随机微分方程（SDE）求解器未能考虑这一动态，盲目在整个过程中注入均匀白噪声并错误使用有限的能量预算。本文建立了一个数学框架，将SDE推断重新视为定向、频率解耦的能量传递。基于此框架，我们提出了无训练的随机求解器彩色噪声采样（CNS）。与注入均匀白噪声不同，CNS采用动态、依赖时间步和频率的调度策略，更高效地将注入能量分配至结构未解决的频段。通过主动利用模型固有的频谱偏置，CNS系统性地引导生成分布逼近真实数据流形。大量实验表明，CNS作为严格即插即用的推理时采样器替代方案，在多种架构（SiT、JiT、FLUX）上显著优于标准ODE和SDE基线。与ImageNet-256上的标准采样相比，CNS在SiT-XL/2上FID从8.26降至6.27，在JiT-B/16上从32.39降至26.69，在JiT-H/16上从11.88降至8.31，同时在无分类器引导下保持一致的相对FID提升。项目页面见https://hadardavidson.github.io/CNS/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the spectral bias inherent in diffusion models—where low-frequency structures form early and high-frequency details emerge later—this work proposes Colored Noise Sampling (CNS), a training-free stochastic solver that reinterprets SDE inference as targeted energy transfer across frequencies. Instead of uniformly injecting white noise, CNS dynamically schedules noise injection based on timestep and frequency, directing energy toward unresolved spectral bands. Experimental results show CNS consistently outperforms standard ODE and SDE samplers across multiple architectures, reducing unguided FID scores from 8.26 to 6.27 on SiT-XL/2, 32.39 to 26.69 on JiT-B/16, and 11.88 to 8.31 on JiT-H/16, with comparable or better performance under Classifier-Free Guidance.
鉴于扩散模型固有的频谱偏差——低频全局结构先形成，高频细节后生成——本文提出了一种无需训练的随机采样器Colored Noise Sampling（CNS），将SDE推断重构为针对不同频段的能量定向传输。CNS根据时间步和频段动态调整噪声注入，将能量更高效地分配至尚未解析的频段。实验表明，CNS在多种架构上显著优于标准ODE和SDE采样器，将未引导FID从SiT-XL/2的8.26降至6.27，JiT-B/16从32.39降至26.69，JiT-H/16从11.88降至8.31，且在Classifier-Free Guidance下保持相似的相对FID提升。
When, why, and how do diffusion posterior samplers fail? A finite-sample lens
Authors: Benjamin A. Burns, Sara Fridovich-Keil
First: 2026-05-28T17:57:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:57:46+00:00
Comments: All code for experiments is available at: https://github.com/voilalab/diagnosing-posterior-sampling
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Diffusion models have excellent capacity to model complex distributions of natural data, which has made them a popular and effective choice for posterior sampling in imaging inverse problems. Existing methods can incorporate any measurement model at inference time but must use an inexact approximation for the likelihood at intermediate timesteps for computational tractability. Although these approximations can often work well empirically, their downstream effect on the sampled posterior is poorly understood and can result in unexplained failures. To understand when, why, and how these likelihood approximations propagate to erroneous posterior distributions, we introduce a finite-sample perspective on posterior sampling that approximates the posterior to arbitrary precision as training set size tends towards infinity, for any forward model and prior distribution. Using this finite-sample lens, we observe that popular posterior sampling approximations tend to under- or over-estimate the spread of the posterior at intermediate timesteps, causing downstream consequences including sensitivity to early stopping time, inaccurate relative weighting of posterior modes, and hallucination, both of prior modes that are not in the posterior and likelihood modes that are not supported by the prior. Moreover, we find that the cause of these posterior errors requires neither a nonlinear measurement model nor a multimodal posterior, but can arise solely due to a multimodal prior and inaccurate posterior spread at intermediate sampling times. Our finite-sample posterior sampling approach is agnostic to the type of likelihood approximation and the type of (linear or nonlinear) forward model, and can thus serve as a drop-in diagnostic to evaluate the accuracy and failure modes of existing and future posterior samplers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散后验采样器何时、为何以及如何失效？有限样本视角
扩散模型在建模自然数据复杂分布方面具有卓越能力，因此成为图像逆问题中后验采样的流行且有效选择。现有方法可在推理时引入任意测量模型，但在中间时间步必须使用不精确的似然近似以保证计算可行性。尽管这些近似在经验上通常表现良好，但其对采样后验的下游影响尚不明确，可能导致未解释的失效。为理解这些似然近似如何传播至错误的后验分布，我们引入有限样本视角，该视角在训练集规模趋于无穷时，可对任意前向模型和先验分布以任意精度逼近后验。通过此有限样本视角，我们观察到流行后验采样近似倾向于在中间时间步低估或高估后验分布的扩散程度，导致下游后果包括对早期停止时间的敏感性、后验模式相对权重不准确以及幻觉现象（包括先验模式未在后验中出现和似然模式未被先验支持的情况）。此外，我们发现导致后验误差的原因既不需要非线性测量模型，也不需要多模态后验，仅需多模态先验和中间采样时间点后验扩散度的不准确即可。我们的有限样本后验采样方法对似然近似类型和前向模型类型（线性或非线性）均无依赖，因此可作为现有及未来后验采样器的即插即用诊断工具，用于评估其准确性和失效模式。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why diffusion-based posterior samplers often fail in imaging inverse problems, despite their empirical success. The authors introduce a finite-sample framework that approximates the posterior with arbitrary precision as training data grows, revealing that inaccuracies in likelihood approximations at intermediate diffusion timesteps—common in existing methods—lead to posterior errors. These errors manifest as under- or over-estimation of posterior spread, causing issues like sensitivity to early stopping, incorrect mode weighting, and hallucination of unsupported modes. Crucially, these failures stem from multimodal priors and inaccurate spread estimates, not nonlinear measurements or multimodal posteriors. The proposed finite-sample lens serves as a universal diagnostic tool, applicable regardless of likelihood approximation or forward model type, to evaluate and improve posterior samplers.
本文研究了扩散模型在图像逆问题中后验采样失败的原因，尽管其在实践中表现良好。作者提出一种有限样本视角，通过增大训练数据量可任意精确逼近后验分布，发现中间扩散步骤中似然近似误差会导致后验分布错误，如方差估计偏差、对早期停止敏感、模式权重错误及幻觉现象。这些错误并非源于非线性测量模型或多重后验模式，而是由多重先验分布与中间采样阶段后验方差不准引起。该框架对似然近似或前向模型类型无依赖，可作为评估现有及未来后验采样器性能的通用诊断工具。
SoundnessBench: Can Your AI Scientist Really Tell Good Research Ideas from Bad Ones?
Authors: Sy-Tuyen Ho, Minghui Liu, Huy Nghiem, Furong Huang
First: 2026-05-28T17:57:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:57:37+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://hosytuyen.github.io/projects/SoundnessBench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autonomous AI research agents aim to accelerate scientific discovery by automating the research pipeline, from hypothesis generation to peer review. However, existing benchmarks rarely test a fundamental bottleneck: whether Large Language Models can judge the methodological viability of a research idea before expending time and computational resources. We introduce SoundnessBench, a curated benchmark of 1,099 machine-learning research proposals reconstructed from ICLR submissions, labeled with reviewer soundness sub-scores, and audited against source papers. SoundnessBench should be interpreted as a benchmark for recoverable proposal-stage soundness rather than exact prediction of full-paper review outcomes. Across 12 frontier LLMs, we find a pervasive optimism bias: under standard prompting, models frequently rate low-soundness proposals as sound, while aggressive prompting largely shifts errors from false positives to false negatives. Additional controls for public-corpus contamination, paper-identifying phrases, surface features, and human audit quality suggest that this behavior is not explained by a single confounder. Our results indicate that current LLMs are not yet reliable as standalone first-gate evaluators for scientific rigor.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate whether large language models can reliably assess the methodological soundness of early-stage research ideas before resource investment, the authors introduce SoundnessBench—a benchmark of 1,099 machine learning proposals derived from ICLR submissions, annotated with reviewer soundness scores and cross-verified against source papers. Using 12 frontier LLMs, they find a consistent optimism bias: models tend to rate low-soundness proposals as sound under standard prompting, though aggressive prompting shifts errors toward false negatives. Controls for contamination, surface features, and human audit quality indicate this bias is not attributable to a single confounder, suggesting current LLMs are not yet trustworthy as standalone first-gate evaluators for scientific rigor.
为评估大语言模型能否在投入资源前可靠判断研究想法的方法论合理性，作者构建了SoundnessBench基准，包含1099个从ICLR投稿中重构的机器学习研究提案，附有审稿人声学评分并经源论文核验。在12个前沿LLM上测试发现，模型普遍存在乐观偏差：标准提示下常误判低合理性提案为合理，而激进提示则使错误转向假阴性。对公共语料污染、表面特征及人工审核质量的控制表明，该偏差非单一因素所致，说明当前LLM尚不能作为独立的科研严谨性初筛工具。
Supercharging Thermal Gaussian Splatting with Depth Estimation
Authors: Manoj Biswanath, Chenxin Cai, Hannah Schieber, Daniel Roth, Benjamin Busam
First: 2026-05-28T17:57:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:57:35+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures. Accepted and will be published in ISPRS proceedings (ISPRS Congress 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Efficient and robust 3D scene representation is crucial in autonomous driving, robotics, and related fields. While RGB images provide valuable content for 3D reconstruction, other modalities like thermal or depth can enable additional information on the environment. Lately, novel view synthesis methods like 3D Gaussian Splatting have started using multiple modalities to further boost their performance. But fusing or combining multimodal data can make the process slower and can bring in additional challenges. Therefore, our project aims to use single modality based on thermal infrared domain, by removing the reliance on visible light as much as possible. This single modality can be expected to be faster as it does not rely on multimodal data. We propose a method, Thermal-to-Depth Gaussian Splatting (TDg), that uses only thermal images and depth estimation in its architecture to derive the radiance fields. Our TDg method outperforms the MSMG (Multiple Single-Modal Gaussians) baseline in most cases on our test datasets, RGBT-Scenes and ThermalMix. On average, the rendering quality metrics such as learned perceptual image patch similarity (LPIPS), structural similarity index measure (SSIM), and peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of TDg are 1.12%, 0.034%, and 0.01% better than the baseline MSMG values. It also reduces the training time significantly, by 12 mins 47 secs (55% improvement). Overall, our method is successful in deriving these thermal radiance fields, which can ultimately have several applications, such as identifying heat sources critical in surveillance, search or rescue operations, and industrial inspections where temperature is widely used to monitor machines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用深度估计增强热红外高斯点渲染
在自动驾驶、机器人等领域，高效且鲁棒的3D场景表示至关重要。虽然RGB图像为3D重建提供了有价值的内容，但热成像或深度等其他模态可提供环境的附加信息。近期，如3D高斯点渲染等新视图合成方法开始融合多模态数据以进一步提升性能。但多模态数据融合会增加计算复杂度并带来额外挑战。因此，本项目旨在基于热红外单一模态，尽可能减少对可见光的依赖。该单一模态预计更快速，因其无需多模态数据。我们提出一种名为热到深度高斯点渲染（TDg）的方法，仅使用热图像和深度估计构建辐射场。在我们的测试数据集RGBT-Scenes和ThermalMix上，TDg在多数情况下优于多模态高斯基线MSMG。平均而言，TDg在学习感知图像块相似度（LPIPS）、结构相似性指数（SSIM）和峰值信噪比（PSNR）等渲染质量指标上分别优于MSMG基线1.12%、0.034%和0.01%。同时，训练时间显著减少12分47秒（提升55%）。总体而言，我们的方法成功构建了热辐射场，可应用于监控、搜救及工业检测等场景，这些场景中温度常用于监测设备热源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient 3D scene representation in applications like autonomous driving and robotics, this work explores thermal infrared imaging as a single modality to avoid reliance on visible light and multimodal fusion. The proposed Thermal-to-Depth Gaussian Splatting (TDg) method constructs radiance fields using only thermal images and depth estimation, bypassing RGB inputs. Experimental results on RGBT-Scenes and ThermalMix show TDg outperforms the MSMG baseline in rendering quality metrics—LPIPS, SSIM, and PSNR—with average improvements of 1.12%, 0.034%, and 0.01% respectively—and reduces training time by 12 minutes 47 seconds (55% faster).
为提升自动驾驶和机器人等场景中3D重建的效率，该研究提出仅使用热红外图像构建辐射场，避免依赖可见光和多模态融合。所提方法Thermal-to-Depth Gaussian Splatting（TDg）仅结合热图像与深度估计构建辐射场，省去多模态融合的计算开销。在RGBT-Scenes和ThermalMix数据集上，TDg在LPIPS、SSIM、PSNR等渲染质量指标上均优于MSMG基线，平均提升1.12%、0.034%、0.01%，同时训练时间缩短12分47秒（提速55%）。
Reasoning While Asking: Transforming Reasoning Large Language Models from Passive Solvers to Proactive Inquirers
Authors: Xin Chen, Feng Jiang, Yiqian Zhang, Hardy Chen, Shuo Yan, Wenya Xie, Min Yang, Shujian Huang
Venue: ACL
First: 2026-01-29T18:56:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:57:34+00:00
Comments: ACL Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reasoning-oriented Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting, yet they remain fundamentally limited by a \emph{blind self-thinking} paradigm: performing extensive internal reasoning even when critical information is missing or ambiguous. We propose Proactive Interactive Reasoning (PIR), a new reasoning paradigm that transforms LLMs from passive solvers into proactive inquirers that interleave reasoning with clarification. Unlike existing search- or tool-based frameworks that primarily address knowledge uncertainty by querying external environments, PIR targets premise- and intent-level uncertainty through direct interaction with the user. PIR is implemented via two core components: (1) an uncertainty-aware supervised fine-tuning procedure that equips models with interactive reasoning capability, and (2) a user-simulator-based policy optimization framework driven by a composite reward that aligns model behavior with user intent. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning, code generation, and document editing demonstrate that PIR consistently outperforms strong baselines, achieving up to 32.70\% higher accuracy, 22.90\% higher pass rate, and 41.36 BLEU improvement, while reducing nearly half of the reasoning computation and unnecessary interaction turns. Further reliability evaluations on factual knowledge, question answering, and missing-premise scenarios confirm the strong generalization and robustness of PIR. Model and code are publicly available at: \href{https://github.com/SUAT-AIRI/Proactive-Interactive-R1}
中文标题/摘要
标题：边问边推理：将推理大语言模型从被动求解者转变为积极探究者
面向推理的大语言模型（LLMs）通过思维链（CoT）提示已取得显著进展，但其本质仍受限于“盲目自思”范式：即使关键信息缺失或模糊，仍会进行大量内部推理。我们提出主动交互推理（PIR），一种新推理范式，将LLMs从被动求解者转变为积极探究者，通过推理与澄清交替进行。与现有主要通过查询外部环境解决知识不确定性的搜索或工具框架不同，PIR通过直接与用户交互解决前提和意图层面的不确定性。PIR通过两个核心组件实现：（1）一种不确定性感知的监督微调过程，赋予模型交互推理能力；（2）基于用户模拟器的策略优化框架，由复合奖励驱动，使模型行为与用户意图对齐。在数学推理、代码生成和文档编辑上的大量实验表明，PIR持续优于强基线方法，准确率提升最高达32.70%，通过率提升22.90%，BLEU值提升41.36%，同时推理计算量减少近一半，不必要的交互轮次减少。在事实知识、问答和前提缺失场景上的进一步可靠性评估证实了PIR的强泛化能力和鲁棒性。模型与代码已公开：https://github.com/SUAT-AIRI/Proactive-Interactive-R1
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current LLMs that perform blind internal reasoning despite missing or ambiguous information, this paper introduces Proactive Interactive Reasoning (PIR), a paradigm that turns reasoning models into proactive inquirers by interleaving reasoning with user clarification. PIR employs an uncertainty-aware fine-tuning procedure to equip models with interactive reasoning skills and a user-simulator-based policy optimization framework guided by a composite reward to align model behavior with user intent. Experiments across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and document editing show PIR achieves up to 32.70% higher accuracy, 22.90% higher pass rate, and 41.36 BLEU improvement while cutting reasoning computation and interaction turns nearly in half, with strong generalization confirmed in factual knowledge and missing-premise scenarios.
为解决当前大语言模型在信息缺失或模糊时仍进行冗余内部推理的问题，本文提出主动交互推理（PIR）范式，使模型从被动求解者转变为通过与用户交互来澄清前提和意图的主动探究者。PIR通过不确定性感知的监督微调赋予模型交互推理能力，并结合基于用户模拟器的策略优化与复合奖励机制，使模型行为更贴合用户意图。在数学推理、代码生成和文档编辑等任务上，PIR相较强基线模型准确率提升最高达32.70%，通过率提升22.90%，BLEU值提升41.36，同时推理计算量和交互轮次减少近一半，且在事实知识、问答及前提缺失场景中展现出强泛化能力与鲁棒性。
Reasoning with Sampling: Cutting at Decision Points
Authors: Felix Zhou, Anay Mehrotra, Quanquan C. Liu
First: 2026-05-28T17:57:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:57:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frontier reasoning models are produced by posttraining base language models with reinforcement learning. Recent work has challenged this by showing that sampling from a sharpened version of the base model's distribution, a so-called power distribution, elicits comparable reasoning without additional training, curated datasets, or verifiers. However, making this method practical requires efficiently sampling from the power distribution. A sampler needs to "mix" to the power distribution, which necessitates moving between modes of the target distribution; intuitively, e.g., trying different reasoning strategies. The samplers proposed in prior works repeatedly select a "cut" position in the current reasoning trace uniformly at random and resample the suffix from that position onward. However, reasoning traces typically contain a few consequential decisions (e.g., the choice of proof strategy or algorithm), and we observe that a uniformly chosen cut tends to rewrite local details rather than revisit decision points. We introduce an algorithm (Entropy-Cut Metropolis-Hastings) that uses the base model's next-token entropy as a proxy to identify key decision points and resample from those positions. We empirically verify that entropy jumps are a useful proxy for decision points and, in a stylized model of reasoning, prove that our method's mixing time scales with the number of decisions in a trace rather than with the number of tokens, which can be much larger. Across MATH500, HumanEval, GPQA Diamond, and AIME26, our method consistently improves over baselines and RL-trained models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过采样进行推理：在决策点处切割
前沿推理模型是通过对基础语言模型进行强化学习后训练得到的。近期研究表明，从基础模型分布的“幂分布”中采样，无需额外训练、精选数据集或验证器，即可获得相当的推理能力。然而，要使该方法实用，需高效采样幂分布。采样器需“混合”至幂分布，这要求在目标分布的不同模式间切换（例如尝试不同的推理策略）。先前工作提出的采样器在当前推理轨迹中随机均匀选择一个“切割”位置，并从该位置重新采样后续部分。然而，推理轨迹通常仅包含少数关键决策（如证明策略或算法的选择），我们观察到随机均匀选择的切割点往往重写局部细节，而非重新访问决策点。我们提出一种算法（熵切割Metropolis-Hastings），利用基础模型的下一个词熵作为代理，识别关键决策点并从这些位置重新采样。我们实证验证熵跳跃是决策点的有效代理，并在推理模型的简化框架中证明，该方法的混合时间随轨迹中决策数量增长，而非随词数增长（后者可能远大于前者）。在MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA Diamond和AIME26数据集上，我们的方法始终优于基线和强化学习训练模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to make sampling-based reasoning practical without reinforcement learning or curated datasets, this paper introduces Entropy-Cut Metropolis-Hastings, a sampler that identifies key decision points in reasoning traces using next-token entropy as a proxy. Unlike prior methods that randomly cut reasoning traces, this approach targets high-entropy positions—where critical decisions occur—and resamples from them, avoiding redundant local changes. Empirical results across MATH500, HumanEval, GPQA Diamond, and AIME26 show consistent performance gains over baseline sampling and even RL-trained models, with theoretical support that mixing time scales with the number of decisions rather than total tokens.
为在无需额外训练或数据集的情况下提升推理能力，该研究提出一种基于熵的采样方法，通过在推理轨迹中识别熵突增点（即关键决策点）进行重采样，而非随机切割。所提出的“熵切梅特罗波斯-哈斯特”算法利用基础模型的下一词熵作为代理指标，有效聚焦决策节点。在MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA Diamond和AIME26等多个基准上，该方法持续优于现有采样和强化学习方法，且其混合时间随决策点数量增长而非总词数增长。
Causal-JEPA: Learning World Models through Object-Level Latent Masking
Authors: Heejeong Nam, Quentin Le Lidec, Lucas Maes, Yann LeCun, Randall Balestriero
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-11T21:47:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:57:16+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://hazel-heejeong-nam.github.io/cjepa/ ICML 2026 Accepted
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
World models require robust relational understanding to support prediction, reasoning, and control. While object-centric representations provide a useful abstraction, they are not sufficient to capture interaction-dependent dynamics. We therefore propose C-JEPA, a simple and flexible object-centric world model that extends masked joint embedding prediction from image patches to object-centric representations. By masking object-level latents and requiring each masked object state to be inferred from the surrounding context, C-JEPA imposes structured partial observability during training, creating counterfactual-like prediction queries that discourage shortcut solutions and make interaction-dependent prediction necessary under the learning objective. Empirically, C-JEPA leads to consistent gains in visual question answering, with an absolute improvement of about 20% in counterfactual reasoning over the same architecture without object-level masking. On agent control tasks, C-JEPA enables substantially more efficient planning by using only 1% of the total latent input features required by patch-based world models, while achieving comparable performance. Finally, we provide a formal analysis demonstrating that object-level masking induces useful inductive bias by controlling observability. Our code is available at https://github.com/galilai-group/cjepa.
中文标题/摘要
标题：因果JEPA：通过对象级潜在掩码学习世界模型
世界模型需要强大的关系理解以支持预测、推理和控制。虽然以对象为中心的表示提供了一种有用的抽象，但它们不足以捕捉依赖交互的动力学。因此，我们提出了C-JEPA，一种简单且灵活的以对象为中心的世界模型，它将掩码联合嵌入预测从图像块扩展到以对象为中心的表示。通过掩码对象级潜在变量，并要求每个被掩码的对象状态从周围上下文推断，C-JEPA在训练期间施加了结构化的部分可观测性，生成类似反事实的预测查询，从而抑制捷径解法，并在学习目标下使依赖交互的预测成为必要。实证上，C-JEPA在视觉问答任务中带来一致的性能提升，在无对象级掩码的相同架构上，反事实推理绝对提升约20%。在智能体控制任务中，C-JEPA通过仅使用基于块世界模型所需总潜在输入特征的1%，实现了显著更高效的规划，同时保持相当的性能。最后，我们提供了形式化分析，证明对象级掩码通过控制可观测性诱导了有用的归纳偏置。我们的代码可在https://github.com/galilai-group/cjepa获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for world models to capture interaction-dependent dynamics beyond object-centric representations, the authors propose Causal-JEPA, which extends masked joint embedding prediction to object-level latents. By masking individual object states and requiring inference from contextual surroundings, the method enforces structured partial observability, encouraging counterfactual reasoning and discouraging shortcuts. Experimentally, C-JEPA improves visual question answering by about 20% in counterfactual reasoning over a non-masked baseline and enables agent control with only 1% of the latent features used by patch-based models while maintaining comparable performance.
为使世界模型能捕捉依赖交互的动态，作者提出Causal-JEPA，将掩码联合嵌入预测扩展至对象级潜变量。通过掩码对象状态并要求从上下文推断，该方法强制结构化部分可观测性，促进反事实推理并抑制捷径。实验表明，相比无对象掩码的基线，Causal-JEPA在反事实推理任务上提升约20%，且在代理控制中仅需原基于图像块模型所需潜变量的1%即可达到相当性能。
RoboWits: Unexpected Challenges for Robotic Creative Problem Solving
Authors: Chunru Lin, Hongxin Zhang, Fenghao Yu, Zhehuan Chen, Thomas L. Griffiths, Yejin Choi, David Held, Chuang Gan
First: 2026-05-28T17:57:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:57:15+00:00
Comments: The first two authors contributed equally
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The ability to reason, adapt, and creatively solve problems under unexpected challenges is essential for robots operating in real-world environments. However, current robotic benchmarks primarily emphasize skill-level execution and provide limited insight into such cognitive reasoning capabilities. We introduce RoboWits, a bi-manual robotic benchmark designed to systematically evaluate cognitive reasoning, creative tool use, and robustness to unexpected conditions. To enable scalable construction of high-quality reasoning-centric unexpected scenarios, we propose an automated task generation pipeline formulated as a multi-agent cooperative framework, comprising agents for seed task generation and verification, metric generation, scene generation, and task mutation. Using the pipeline, we curated 30 diverse seed tasks and 208 tasks with mutations and graded difficulty across geometry, material, and assembly-based reasoning. We benchmark popular robot policies, pre-trained VLAs, and oracle-state planners. Our results reveal a significant performance gap: while pre-trained VLAs exhibit preliminary success on seed tasks after single-task fine-tuning, they struggle to perform on mutated tasks, implying their brittleness in manipulation tasks requiring reasoning, strategy adaptation, and robustness to deceptive or constrained environments. Project page is available at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/RoboWits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboWits：机器人创造性问题解决中的意外挑战
在现实环境中操作的机器人需要具备在意外挑战下进行推理、适应和创造性解决问题的能力。然而，当前的机器人基准测试主要强调技能执行层面，对认知推理能力的评估有限。我们提出了RoboWits，一个双臂机器人基准测试，旨在系统评估认知推理、创造性工具使用及对意外条件的鲁棒性。为实现高质量推理导向的意外场景的可扩展构建，我们提出了一种自动化任务生成管道，该管道以多智能体协作框架形式构建，包含种子任务生成与验证、指标生成、场景生成和任务变异等智能体。通过该管道，我们精选了30个多样化种子任务，并生成了208个经过变异和难度分级的任务，涵盖几何、材料和装配推理。我们对流行机器人策略、预训练视觉语言代理（VLAs）和oracle状态规划器进行了基准测试。结果表明，存在显著性能差距：预训练VLAs在单任务微调后对种子任务表现出初步成功，但在变异任务上表现不佳，暗示其在需要推理、策略适应和对欺骗性或受限环境鲁棒性的操作任务中存在脆弱性。项目页面见：https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/RoboWits。
