为评估机器人在意外条件下进行认知推理与创造性问题解决的能力，作者提出了RoboWits双臂机器人基准测试，旨在衡量工具使用、创造性与环境鲁棒性。他们构建了一个多智能体自动化任务生成管道，生成涵盖几何、材料和装配推理的多样化任务，共收集30个基础任务和208个变异任务。实验表明，经过单任务微调的预训练视觉语言模型在原始任务上表现尚可，但在变异任务上表现显著下降，显示出其在需要推理、策略调整和应对欺骗性环境的操控任务中存在脆弱性，而Oracle规划器则保持稳定表现。
Veda: Scalable Video Diffusion via Distilled Sparse Attention
Authors: Shihao Han, Hao Yang, Xinting Hu, Xiaofeng Mei, Yi Jiang, Xiaojuan Qi
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:57:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:57:07+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling Diffusion Transformers to generate high-resolution, long videos is constrained by the quadratic cost of self-attention, and existing sparse attention methods degrade under high sparsity. We show empirically that generation quality is determined not by the sparsity ratio itself, but by how well the sparse mask aligns with the tile-wise geometry of full attention. Based on this insight, we propose Veda, a distilled sparse attention framework that formulates tile selection as an explicit reconstruction problem from full attention. Veda integrates statistics-aware tile scoring with head-aware tiling to reduce estimation error and structural mismatch, enabling aggressive sparsity. A hardware-efficient tile-skipping kernel converts theoretical sparsity into practical wall-clock speedups. Experiments on large video diffusion models, including Waver and Wan2.1, demonstrate substantial acceleration with no noticeable degradation in generation quality. To generate 720P 10-second videos on Waver-T2V-12B, Veda achieves a 5.1$\times$ end-to-end speedup and a 10.5$\times$ self-attention speedup, reducing attention overhead from 92% to 50%. Notably, the gains increase with sequence length, indicating that Veda scales favorably with spatiotemporal resolution across models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Veda：通过蒸馏稀疏注意力实现可扩展视频扩散
将扩散Transformer扩展以生成高分辨率、长视频受到自注意力二次成本的限制，且现有稀疏注意力方法在高稀疏度下性能下降。我们实证表明，生成质量取决于稀疏掩码与完整注意力的分块几何结构的匹配程度，而非稀疏率本身。基于此洞察，我们提出Veda，一种蒸馏稀疏注意力框架，将分块选择形式化为从完整注意力中显式重建的问题。Veda结合统计感知的分块评分与头感知分块，降低估计误差和结构不匹配，实现激进稀疏度。一种硬件高效的分块跳过内核将理论稀疏度转化为实际的壁钟加速。在包括Waver和Wan2.1在内的大型视频扩散模型上的实验表明，Veda显著加速生成，且无明显质量下降。在Waver-T2V-12B上生成720P 10秒视频时，Veda实现5.1倍端到端加速和10.5倍自注意力加速，将注意力开销从92%降至50%。值得注意的是，加速效果随序列长度增加而提升，表明Veda在不同模型中对时空分辨率具有良好的可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the quadratic computational cost of self-attention in video diffusion models, this work introduces Veda, a distilled sparse attention framework that improves scalability without sacrificing quality. Veda treats tile selection as a reconstruction problem from full attention, combining statistics-aware tile scoring with head-aware tiling to minimize structural mismatch and estimation error. This enables aggressive sparsity while preserving generation fidelity. Experiments on Waver and Wan2.1 models show Veda delivers 5.1x end-to-end speedup and 10.5x self-attention speedup for 720P 10-second videos, cutting attention overhead from 92% to 50%, with gains scaling favorably with sequence length.
为解决视频扩散模型中自注意力的二次计算开销问题，作者提出Veda框架，通过将块选择建模为从完整注意力中重建的问题，结合统计感知块评分与头感知分块，减少结构失配与估计误差，实现高稀疏度而不牺牲质量。在Waver和Wan2.1模型上实验表明，Veda可实现720P 10秒视频生成的5.1倍端到端加速和10.5倍自注意力加速，将注意力开销从92%降至50%，且生成质量无损。性能增益随序列长度增加而提升，表明其在时空分辨率扩展下具有良好的可扩展性。
On Language Generation in the Limit with Bounded Memory
Authors: Jon Kleinberg, Anay Mehrotra, Amin Saberi, Grigoris Velegkas
First: 2026-05-28T17:57:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:57:03+00:00
Comments: The abstract has been shortened to fit within the arXiv limit
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study language generation in the limit under bounded memory. In this task, a learner observes examples from an unknown target language one at a time and must eventually output only new valid examples. Prior work assumes access to the entire history, a strong assumption since realistic algorithms retain limited past information. Classical work in learning theory shows memory constraints dramatically alter learnability; we extend this to language generation. First, we study memoryless generators. Under a mild enumeration restriction, every countable collection of infinite languages remains generable without memory. Without this restriction, we exactly characterize when memoryless generation is possible. For finite collections, we characterize the optimal minimax density achievable by memoryless generators -- the best density guaranteed against any collection of a given size. This combinatorial bound relies on Sperner's theorem and symmetric chain decompositions. We further show that a sliding window of the last $W$ examples does not improve this worst-case density, whereas allowing it to store $b$ adaptively chosen past examples improves the achievable density for every $b \geq 1$. Finally, we revisit identification in the limit, where the learner must converge to a single correct hypothesis for the target language. We focus on its incremental variant, where the learner remembers only its previous guess. Here, although exact identification fails on a collection of just three languages, a mild relaxation requiring convergence to an ``approximate'' version of the target is achievable for every finite collection. These results show bounded memory affects these tasks differently: generation remains achievable for every countable collection, while density and identification are confined to finite collections, with guarantees weakening as the collection grows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在有限记忆下极限语言生成研究
我们研究在有限记忆约束下极限语言生成问题。在此任务中，学习者逐个观察未知目标语言的示例，最终必须仅输出新的有效示例。先前工作假设可访问完整历史，这是强假设，因为现实算法仅保留有限历史信息。学习理论经典工作表明，记忆约束显著改变可学习性；我们将其扩展至语言生成。 首先，我们研究无记忆生成器。在温和的枚举限制下，任意可数无限语言集合均可无记忆生成。无此限制时，我们精确刻画无记忆生成何时可行。对于有限集合，我们刻画无记忆生成器所能达到的最佳最小最大密度——即对给定大小集合的最优保证密度。该组合界依赖于Sperner定理与对称链分解。 我们进一步证明，仅保留最后W个示例的滑动窗口无法改善最坏情况密度，而允许存储b个自适应选择的过去示例，则对任意b≥1均能提升可实现密度。 最后，我们重新审视极限识别问题，其中学习者必须收敛至目标语言的单一正确假设。我们聚焦其增量变体，学习者仅记住其前次猜测。此处，尽管在仅三个语言集合上精确识别失败，但对任意有限集合，通过收敛至目标的“近似”版本，可实现温和放松要求。 这些结果表明，有限记忆对这些任务影响不同：生成对任意可数集合仍可实现，而密度与识别仅限于有限集合，且集合规模增大时保证减弱。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates language generation under bounded memory, motivated by the unrealistic assumption in prior work that learners retain full historical data. It first analyzes memoryless generators, showing that under mild enumeration constraints, any countable collection of infinite languages can be generated without memory; otherwise, it characterizes when memoryless generation is feasible and derives optimal minimax density bounds using combinatorial tools like Sperner’s theorem. The study then demonstrates that a fixed sliding window of past examples does not improve worst-case density, but adaptive storage of past examples enhances achievable density for any b≥1. Finally, it examines identification in the limit with incremental memory (only previous guesses retained), finding that exact identification fails for even three languages, but approximate identification is achievable for any finite collection.
该论文研究了在有限记忆条件下语言生成的问题，动机在于纠正先前工作假设学习者能保留完整历史数据这一不现实前提。首先分析无记忆生成器，证明在轻微枚举限制下，任意可数无限语言集合均可无记忆生成；无限制时则精确刻画了无记忆生成的可行性条件，并利用组合工具（如Sperner定理）推导出最优最小最大密度下界。接着证明固定滑动窗口无法改善最坏情况密度，但允许自适应选择存储历史样本可提升任意b≥1时的密度。最后研究极限识别问题中增量记忆情形，发现精确识别在仅三个语言集合时即失败，但对任意有限集合可实现近似识别。总体而言，有限记忆允许所有可数集合生成，但密度与识别仅限于有限集合，且随集合规模增大保障性下降。
In-Context Reward Adaptation for Robust Preference Modeling
Authors: Zhenyu Sun, Zheng Xu, Ermin Wei
First: 2026-05-28T17:56:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:56:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) typically relies on static reward models to align Large Language Models with human preferences. However, human values are inherently diverse and heterogeneous, and a single reward model often lacks the robustness required to generalize to unseen preference domains. While existing multi-reward frameworks attempt to address this, they are often restricted to a fixed set of known domains and fail to adapt to unseen human distributions without costly retraining. In this work, we propose In-Context Reward Adaptation, a transformer-based framework designed to model diverse and unseen human preferences on the fly. By leveraging the in-context learning capabilities of transformers, our approach adaptively infers the underlying reward structure from a small set of preference demonstrations. We demonstrate that while a standard transformer architecture is insufficient for this task by characterizing an asymptotic bias to the ground-truth, incorporating human response time as an auxiliary input signal enables the model to successfully adapt to preferences from previously unseen domains. Our findings show that this approach provides a more robust foundation for preference modeling, allowing for the representation of heterogeneous rewards and preference distribution shift, and offering a scalable path toward more flexible human-AI alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：上下文奖励自适应用于鲁棒偏好建模
基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）通常依赖静态奖励模型来使大语言模型与人类偏好对齐。然而，人类价值观本质上具有多样性和异质性，单一奖励模型往往缺乏泛化到未见偏好领域的鲁棒性。尽管现有多奖励框架试图解决此问题，但它们通常受限于固定已知领域，且在不进行昂贵重训练的情况下无法适应未见的人类分布。本文提出上下文奖励自适应方法，这是一种基于Transformer的框架，旨在实时建模多样且未见的人类偏好。通过利用Transformer的上下文学习能力，我们的方法从少量偏好演示中自适应推断潜在奖励结构。我们证明，标准Transformer架构因对真实奖励存在渐近偏差而不足以完成此任务；但引入人类响应时间作为辅助输入信号后，模型能够成功适应先前未见领域的偏好。我们的研究发现表明，该方法为偏好建模提供了更鲁棒的基础，支持异质奖励表示及偏好分布偏移，并为更灵活的人机对齐提供了可扩展路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static reward models in RLHF that fail to generalize across diverse human preferences, this work introduces In-Context Reward Adaptation, a transformer-based method that adapts to unseen preference domains using only a small set of preference demonstrations. The method leverages in-context learning to infer reward structures dynamically, and crucially, incorporates human response time as an auxiliary signal to overcome the asymptotic bias inherent in standard transformers. Experimental results confirm that this adaptation enables robust modeling of heterogeneous rewards and distribution shifts, offering a scalable solution for flexible human-AI alignment without costly retraining.
为了解决强化学习中静态奖励模型难以适应多样化人类偏好的问题，本文提出一种基于Transformer的“上下文奖励自适应”方法，通过少量偏好示例动态推断未知领域中的奖励结构。该方法利用Transformer的上下文学习能力，并引入人类响应时间作为辅助信号，以克服标准Transformer固有的渐近偏差。实验表明，该方法能有效建模异质奖励和分布偏移，无需重训练即可实现灵活的人机对齐，具有良好的可扩展性。
Gram: Assessing sabotage propensities via automated alignment auditing
Authors: David Lindner, Victoria Krakovna, Sebastian Farquhar
First: 2026-05-28T17:56:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:56:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Gram, an automated alignment auditing framework to assess the propensity of AI agents to engage in sabotage. We evaluate Gemini models across 17 simulated agentic deployment scenarios that incentivize sabotage. We find Gemini models misbehave in about 2-3% of our simulated trajectories. Many of these cases are explained by "overeagerness" in Gemini models resulting in both excessive role-playing and goal-seeking behavior. In contrast to other alignment auditing approaches, Gram is designed to specifically evaluate misalignment and intentional sabotage in agentic coding and research agents. We additionally introduce an experimental investigator agent pipeline which enables fine-grained targeted experiments to identify the drivers of misbehavior. We find that increasing realism of environments and removing nudges to misbehave tends to reduce sabotage rates close to zero.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Gram：通过自动化对齐审计评估人工智能代理的破坏倾向
我们提出了Gram，一个用于评估人工智能代理破坏倾向的自动化对齐审计框架。我们在17个模拟的代理部署场景中评估了Gemini模型，这些场景激励破坏行为。我们发现Gemini模型在约2-3%的模拟轨迹中表现出不当行为，许多案例可归因于Gemini模型的“过度急切”，导致角色扮演和目标追求行为过度。与其它对齐审计方法不同，Gram专门设计用于评估代理编码和研究代理中的对齐偏差和有意破坏行为。我们还引入了一种实验调查者代理流水线，以实现细粒度的靶向实验，识别不当行为的驱动因素。我们发现，增加环境的真实性并移除促使不当行为的暗示，往往能将破坏率降至接近零。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess AI agents' susceptibility to sabotage in real-world deployment, Gram introduces an automated alignment auditing framework that evaluates Gemini models across 17 simulated agentic scenarios designed to incentivize harmful behavior. The method involves running controlled experiments and analyzing agent trajectories to detect misalignment, with a focus on identifying 'overeagerness' as a root cause of excessive role-playing and goal-seeking. Experimental results show that approximately 2-3% of simulated trajectories exhibit misbehavior, but sabotage rates drop to near zero when environments are made more realistic and nudges to misbehave are removed.
为检测和量化AI代理的破坏倾向，Gram提出了一种自动化对齐审计框架，通过17个模拟部署场景评估Gemini模型在激励破坏行为下的表现。该方法在受控环境下运行代理轨迹并分析偏离预期对齐的行为，重点识别因过度积极导致的误行为，如过度角色扮演和目标追求。实验结果显示，约2-3%的模拟轨迹出现误行为，主要源于过度积极；但当环境更贴近现实且移除诱导破坏的提示后，破坏率接近零，表明破坏行为多具情境依赖性且可被缓解。
MonoPhysics: Estimating Geometry, Appearance, and Physical Parameters from Monocular Videos
Authors: Daniel Rho, Jun Myeong Choi, Matthew Thornton, Biswadip Dey, Roni Sengupta
First: 2026-05-28T17:55:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:55:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing inverse physics methods recover physical parameters from multi-view videos, where geometric constraints across views resolve scale and 3D structure. In monocular settings, however, such constraints are absent, leading to severe scale ambiguity, inaccurate geometry, and weak coupling between appearance optimization and physical simulation. We propose MonoPhysics, a framework for monocular inverse physics estimation of deformable objects using differentiable MPM simulation and 3D Gaussian Splatting, which jointly optimizes geometry, appearance, and physical parameters from a single camera view. We address these challenges through three visual-physical bridges: global scale alignment, physics-aware geometry refinement, and a differentiable position map, which together enable accurate optimization from monocular observations alone. We evaluate on Vid2Sim and our new dataset of elastic and plastic objects, showing that MonoPhysics outperforms existing baselines in monocular settings and achieves performance comparable to multi-view baselines using only a single camera. Our project page is available at https://daniel03c1.github.io/MonoPhysics/
中文标题/摘要
标题：MonoPhysics：从单目视频中估计几何、外观和物理参数
现有逆物理方法从多视角视频中恢复物理参数，其中跨视角的几何约束可解决尺度和三维结构问题。但在单目设置下，此类约束缺失，导致严重尺度模糊、几何不准确，以及外观优化与物理模拟之间耦合弱。我们提出MonoPhysics框架，利用可微分MPM模拟与3D高斯点渲染，从单目视角联合优化可变形物体的几何、外观和物理参数。我们通过三个视觉-物理桥梁解决上述挑战：全局尺度对齐、物理感知几何细化与可微分位置图，从而仅凭单目观测即可实现精确优化。我们在Vid2Sim及我们新构建的弹性与塑性物体数据集上进行评估，结果表明MonoPhysics在单目设置下优于现有基线方法，且仅用单目相机即可达到多视角基线的性能水平。项目页面见https://daniel03c1.github.io/MonoPhysics/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing inverse physics methods in monocular settings—where scale ambiguity and weak appearance-physical coupling hinder accurate estimation—MonoPhysics introduces a framework that jointly optimizes geometry, appearance, and physical parameters from a single camera view using differentiable MPM simulation and 3D Gaussian Splatting. It addresses key challenges through three visual-physical bridges: global scale alignment, physics-aware geometry refinement, and a differentiable position map. Experimental results on Vid2Sim and a new dataset of elastic and plastic objects demonstrate that MonoPhysics significantly outperforms existing monocular baselines and matches multi-view performance using only one camera.
为解决单目视频中物理参数估计因缺乏多视角几何约束而导致的尺度模糊与外观-物理耦合弱等问题，MonoPhysics提出一种结合可微MPM模拟与3D高斯点渲染的框架，从单目视频中联合优化几何、外观与物理参数。该框架通过三个视觉-物理桥梁——全局尺度对齐、物理感知几何优化与可微位置图——有效解决单目场景下的尺度与几何重建难题。在Vid2Sim及新构建的弹性/塑性物体数据集上的实验表明，MonoPhysics在单目设置下超越现有基线，且性能可媲美多视角方法。
Improved Guarantees for Heterogeneous Treatment-Effect Estimation via Matrix Completion
Authors: Anay Mehrotra, Phuc Tran, Van H. Vu, Manolis Zampetakis
First: 2026-05-28T17:55:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:55:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A central goal of modern causal inference is estimating heterogeneous treatment effects to answer questions like "how does an intervention affect each unit," rather than only on average. We study this problem with panel-data where we observe $n$ units across $m$ times under unknown, non-uniform treatment assignments. The data in this setting is naturally represented as a matrix of all unit--time treatment effects. Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects can then be expressed as obtaining a good estimation of each row's average in this matrix. This allows us to formulate the problem as matrix completion, which can be solved under natural low-rankness assumptions. However, existing matrix-completion guarantees are not powerful enough to get meaningful bounds for the per-row guarantee required for estimating the heterogeneous treatment effect; roughly speaking, they are only useful for estimating average treatment effect bounds, as also illustrated in a recent line of work. We give a simple, computationally efficient estimator that, without knowledge of the propensities and under standard low-rankness and regularity assumptions, achieves a row-wise $\ell_2$ error of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n} + \frac{n}{m^2}})$. Technically, our analysis establishes the first sharp row-wise $\ell_2$-perturbation bound for low-rank approximation, complementing existing spectral-, Frobenius-, and entrywise perturbation theory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过矩阵补全改进异质处理效应估计的保证
现代因果推断的核心目标是估计异质处理效应，以回答诸如“干预如何影响每个单位”之类的问题，而不仅限于平均效应。我们研究的是面板数据场景，其中在未知且非均匀的处理分配下观察 $n$ 个单位在 $m$ 个时间点的数据。该场景中的数据自然表示为一个包含所有单位-时间处理效应的矩阵。估计异质处理效应可表述为对矩阵每行平均值的良好估计。这使我们能够将问题形式化为矩阵补全，可在自然的低秩假设下求解。然而，现有矩阵补全的保证不足以获得估计异质处理效应所需的每行误差界限；简言之，它们仅适用于估计平均处理效应界限，正如近期相关工作所展示的。我们提出了一种简单且计算高效的估计器，在不需已知倾向性且满足标准低秩和正则性假设下，实现了每行 $\ell_2$ 误差为 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n} + \frac{n}{m^2}})$。技术上，我们的分析建立了低秩近似首个精确的行-wise $\ell_2$ 扰动界，补充了现有的谱、Frobenius 和逐元素扰动理论。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to estimate heterogeneous treatment effects in panel data settings where treatment assignments are unknown and non-uniform, the paper formulates the problem as matrix completion, treating each unit-time treatment effect as a matrix entry. The authors propose a computationally efficient estimator that achieves row-wise $\ell_2$ error of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n} + \frac{n}{m^2}})$ without requiring knowledge of treatment propensities, under standard low-rankness and regularity assumptions. This result provides the first sharp row-wise perturbation bound for low-rank approximation, improving upon existing matrix completion guarantees that are insufficient for per-row heterogeneous effect estimation.
为解决面板数据中未知且非均匀处理分配下的异质处理效应估计问题，本文将该问题建模为矩阵补全，其中每行代表某单位在时间维度上的平均处理效应。作者提出一种计算高效的估计器，在无需处理倾向性知识的前提下，于标准低秩和正则性假设下，实现了行-wise ℓ₂误差界 ˜O(√(1/n + n/m²))。该界优于现有矩阵补全保证，后者无法满足行级估计需求，且是首个针对低秩近似行-wise ℓ₂扰动的精确界。
Before the Shutter: Aesthetic and Actionable Portrait Photography Planning in 3D Scenes
Authors: Ruixiang Jiang, Chang Wen Chen
First: 2026-05-28T17:55:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:55:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Portrait photography is largely decided before the shutter opens: the subject's pose, the camera configuration, and the lighting devices must be coordinated within the surrounding 3D scene. In contrast, most existing computational methods focus on post-production in 2D image space, such as retouching, relighting, or editing images that already exist; pre-capture photographic planning remains largely unexplored. We introduce 3D aesthetic portrait planning, the task of generating human pose, camera, lighting, and exposure plans that produce visually compelling portraits while satisfying geometric and photometric feasibility in a 3D scene. Our approach builds a Photographic Scene Graph that represents scene affordances, subject-scene relations, and portrait-relevant lighting structure. Built on this representation, we perform aesthetic-guided comparative planning over previous attempts and current viewfinder observations. Experiments across diverse indoor and outdoor scenes show that our method produces portraits preferred by human raters and MLLM evaluators over competitive baselines, while maintaining high physical plausibility. Together, our results suggest a path from post-capture correction toward pre-capture computational portrait planning. Project repository: https://github.com/songrise/Before-the-Shutter
中文标题/摘要
标题：快门之前：三维场景中具有美学与可操作性的肖像摄影规划
肖像摄影在快门开启前即已基本决定：主体姿态、相机配置与灯光设备需在三维场景中协调配合。相比之下，现有计算方法多聚焦于二维图像空间的后期处理，如修图、重光照或编辑已存在的图像；而拍摄前的摄影规划仍鲜有探索。我们提出三维美学肖像规划任务，旨在生成符合三维场景几何与光度可行性的主体姿态、相机、灯光与曝光方案，以生成视觉上引人入胜的肖像。我们的方法构建了摄影场景图，用于表示场景可操作性、主体-场景关系及与肖像相关的光照结构。基于该表示，我们结合美学引导，在过往尝试与当前取景器观察之间进行比较规划。在多样化的室内与室外场景实验中，我们的方法在人类评分者与多模态大语言模型评估者中均优于现有基线方法，同时保持高物理合理性。我们的结果表明，从拍摄后修正迈向拍摄前计算肖像规划的路径已初步可行。项目仓库：https://github.com/songrise/Before-the-Shutter
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to plan portrait photography before capturing images, this work addresses the gap in computational methods that focus on post-production rather than pre-capture planning. It introduces 3D aesthetic portrait planning, generating human pose, camera, lighting, and exposure configurations that are both visually compelling and physically feasible within a 3D scene. The method constructs a Photographic Scene Graph to encode scene affordances and lighting structures, then performs aesthetic-guided comparative planning using prior attempts and real-time viewfinder data. Experimental results across varied indoor and outdoor environments demonstrate that the proposed approach generates portraits rated higher by human evaluators and MLLM models than existing baselines, while preserving geometric and photometric plausibility.
该论文针对3D环境中拍摄前的肖像摄影规划这一尚未充分探索的问题，提出了一种在拍摄前协调人物姿态、相机参数、灯光与曝光的框架。方法构建了“摄影场景图”以建模场景可操作性与灯光结构，并基于此进行美学引导的对比规划，结合历史方案与实时取景观察。实验在多样化的室内外场景中表明，该方法在人类评估与MLLM评估中优于现有基线，同时保持高物理合理性，标志着从拍摄后修图向拍摄前计算规划的转变。
VPG: Visual Prefix Guidance for Autoregressive Image and Video Generation
Authors: Xinyao Liao, Qiyuan He, Yicong Li, Jiayin Zhu, Xiaoye Qu, Wei Wei, Angela Yao
First: 2026-05-28T17:55:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:55:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autoregressive image and video generators are trained with teacher-forced histories but must sample from their own generated prefixes at inference time, making them vulnerable to exposure bias and prefix drift. Existing remedies either modify training or apply sampling-time guidance aimed primarily at external semantic conditions, such as class labels or text prompts, rather than testing whether a next-step prediction provides strong posterior support for the generated prefix itself. We propose Visual Prefix Guidance (VPG), a training-free inference-time guidance method for autoregressive image and video generation. VPG improves next-step prediction by contrasting the model's output under the generated prefix with its output under a corrupted prefix, then extrapolating logits toward candidates that strengthen the posterior support of the generated prefix. Across class-conditional image generation with VAR, text-to-image generation with Infinity, and text-to-video generation with InfinityStar, VPG improves generation quality without retraining the base model, reducing FID on VAR by 0.36 on average and improving benchmark performance on both image and video generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VPG：用于自回归图像和视频生成的视觉前缀引导
自回归图像和视频生成器在训练时使用教师强制历史，但在推理时必须从自身生成的前缀中采样，这使其易受暴露偏差和前缀漂移的影响。现有解决方案要么修改训练过程，要么在采样时应用主要针对外部语义条件（如类别标签或文本提示）的引导，而未检验下一步预测是否能为生成的前缀提供强后验支持。我们提出视觉前缀引导（VPG），这是一种无需训练的推理时引导方法，用于自回归图像和视频生成。VPG通过对比模型在生成前缀和损坏前缀下的输出，将对数几率外推至能增强生成前缀后验支持的候选值，从而改进下一步预测。在使用VAR的类别条件图像生成、使用Infinity的文本到图像生成以及使用InfinityStar的文本到视频生成中，VPG在不重新训练基础模型的情况下提升生成质量，使VAR的FID平均降低0.36，并在图像和视频生成的基准测试中均提升性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the exposure bias and prefix drift issues in autoregressive image and video generation, where models must sample from their own outputs at inference time, the paper introduces Visual Prefix Guidance (VPG), a training-free method that enhances next-step predictions during inference. VPG works by contrasting the model’s logits under the current generated prefix versus a corrupted version, then adjusting logits to favor candidates that better support the generated prefix. Experiments across VAR, Infinity, and InfinityStar show VPG improves generation quality without retraining, reducing FID by 0.36 on average in image generation and boosting performance on both image and video benchmarks.
为解决自回归图像和视频生成中因采样自身生成前缀导致的暴露偏差和前缀漂移问题，作者提出了一种无需训练的推理时引导方法——视觉前缀引导（VPG），该方法通过对比当前生成前缀与破坏性前缀下的模型输出，将预测 logits 引导至能增强当前前缀后验支持的候选值。在 VAR 的类别条件图像生成、Infinity 的文本到图像生成以及 InfinityStar 的文本到视频生成三个任务中，VPG 在不重新训练模型的前提下显著提升生成质量，平均降低 FID 0.36，并在图像与视频生成基准上均取得性能提升。
Reinforcing Few-step Generators via Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching
Authors: Yushi Huang, Xiangxin Zhou, Ruoyu Wang, Chi Zhang, Jun Zhang, Tianyu Pang
First: 2026-05-25T17:59:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:54:16+00:00
Comments: Code and models are available at https://github.com/Harahan/RTDMD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advances in few-step diffusion distillation have enabled efficient image generation, yet aligning these models with human preferences remains challenging. We propose Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching Distillation (RTDMD), a two-stage framework that unifies distribution matching distillation with reward-guided reinforcement learning for few-step flow generators. We show that minimizing the KL divergence to a reward-tilted teacher distribution naturally decomposes into a distribution matching term and a reward maximization term. In the first stage, we introduce Ambient-Consistent Distribution Matching Distillation (AC-DMD), which performs subinterval-wise distribution matching and augments the fake score objective with a consistency regularizer to help the fake score model track the shifting generator distribution under limited updates. In the second stage, we jointly optimize both terms: for the reward maximization term, we derive a hybrid policy gradient that combines a GRPO-style estimator for the stochastic intermediate transitions with direct reward backpropagation through the deterministic final step, and further introduce step-subset GRPO (SubGRPO) to reduce variance. Experiments on SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.2 demonstrate that RTDMD establishes new state-of-the-art results across preference, aesthetic, and compositional metrics with only 4 inference steps, outperforming previous few-step text-to-image generation methods. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Harahan/RTDMD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过奖励倾斜分布匹配强化少步生成器
近期少步扩散蒸馏的进展实现了高效的图像生成，但将这些模型与人类偏好对齐仍具挑战。我们提出奖励倾斜分布匹配蒸馏（RTDMD），一种两阶段框架，将分布匹配蒸馏与奖励引导的强化学习统一应用于少步流生成器。我们证明，最小化KL散度至奖励倾斜教师分布自然分解为分布匹配项与奖励最大化项。第一阶段引入环境一致分布匹配蒸馏（AC-DMD），进行子区间分布匹配，并在假分数目标中加入一致性正则化，以帮助假分数模型在有限更新下跟踪生成器分布的移动。第二阶段联合优化两项：对于奖励最大化项，我们推导出混合策略梯度，结合GRPO风格估计器处理随机中间转移，同时直接通过确定性最终步骤反向传播奖励，并进一步引入步集GRPO（SubGRPO）以降低方差。在SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.2上的实验表明，RTDMD仅用4步推理即在偏好、美学和构图指标上建立新SOTA，超越先前的少步文本到图像生成方法。代码与模型见https://github.com/Harahan/RTDMD。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of aligning few-step diffusion distillation models with human preferences, this work introduces Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching Distillation (RTDMD), a two-stage framework combining distribution matching and reward-guided reinforcement learning. The first stage, Ambient-Consistent Distribution Matching Distillation (AC-DMD), uses subinterval-wise distribution matching and a consistency regularizer to stabilize learning under limited updates. The second stage jointly optimizes distribution matching and reward maximization via a hybrid policy gradient that blends GRPO-style estimation with direct reward backpropagation, enhanced by step-subset GRPO to reduce variance. Experiments on SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.2 show RTDMD achieves state-of-the-art performance across preference, aesthetic, and compositional metrics using only four inference steps, surpassing prior few-step text-to-image methods.
为解决少步扩散蒸馏模型与人类偏好对齐的难题，本文提出奖励倾斜分布匹配蒸馏（RTDMD）框架，结合分布匹配与奖励引导强化学习。第一阶段“环境一致分布匹配蒸馏”（AC-DMD）采用子区间匹配与一致性正则化，在更新次数有限时稳定学习。第二阶段联合优化分布匹配与奖励最大化，通过混合策略梯度结合GRPO风格估计与直接奖励反向传播，并引入步集GRPO降低方差。在SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.2上实验表明，RTDMD仅用四步推理即在偏好、美学与构图指标上达到当前最优，超越现有少步文本到图像生成方法。
Thinking Before Constraining: A Unified Decoding Framework for Large Language Models
Authors: Ngoc Trinh Hung Nguyen, Alonso Silva, Laith Zumot, Liubov Tupikina, Armen Aghasaryan, Mehwish Alam
Venue: EMNLP
First: 2026-01-12T13:25:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:54:13+00:00
Comments: v2-EMNLP
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Natural generation allows Large Language Models (LLMs) to produce free-form responses with rich reasoning, yet the lack of structure makes outputs difficult to verify. Conversely, constrained decoding ensures standardized formats but can inadvertently restrict reasoning capabilities by imposing constraints too early in the generation process. We propose a hybrid approach, namely In-Writing, that combines free-form reasoning and structured generation in a single call. The model first performs unconstrained reasoning and only applies structured decoding after a trigger token is generated, explicitly decoupling reasoning from formatting. We establish that our trigger-token strategies are able to virtually eradicate premature triggering, a failure mode in which constrained decoding interrupts on-going reasoning. Evaluations across diverse datasets covering classification and reasoning tasks demonstrate that our approach outperforms the state-of-the-art by achieving accuracy gains of up to 27% over natural generation. Our code are available at: https://github.com/Nokia-Bell-Labs/InWriting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：先思考再约束：一种大型语言模型的统一解码框架
自然生成使大型语言模型（LLM）能够输出富含推理的自由形式响应，但缺乏结构导致输出难以验证；相反，约束解码虽保证格式标准化，却可能因过早施加约束而无意中限制推理能力。我们提出一种混合方法——In-Writing，通过单次调用结合自由形式推理与结构化生成。模型首先进行无约束推理，仅在生成触发词后才应用结构化解码，明确将推理与格式化解耦。我们证明，所提出的触发词策略可几乎完全消除过早触发这一失败模式（即约束解码在推理过程中过早中断）。在涵盖分类与推理任务的多样化数据集上的评估表明，我们的方法相较自然生成最高提升27%的准确率。代码开源于：https://github.com/Nokia-Bell-Labs/InWriting。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between free-form reasoning and structured output in large language models, this paper introduces In-Writing, a hybrid decoding framework that first allows unconstrained reasoning and only applies structured formatting after a trigger token is generated. This decouples reasoning from formatting, preventing premature constraint application that can hinder model performance. Experimental results across diverse datasets show that In-Writing outperforms both natural generation and constrained decoding, achieving up to 27% higher accuracy in classification and reasoning tasks.
为解决大语言模型在自由推理与结构化输出间的权衡问题，本文提出In-Writing框架，先进行无约束推理，待生成触发词后再应用结构化解码，从而将推理与格式化解耦。实验表明该方法有效避免了过早触发的失败模式，在多个数据集上相较自然生成方法提升最高达27%的准确率，优于当前最优方法。
Resolution Diagnostics for Paired LLM Evaluation
Authors: Anany Kotawala
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:54:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:54:09+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 7 figures, 12 tables. Accepted to the ICML 2026 Workshop on Hypothesis Testing, Seoul, South Korea, 2026. Copyright 2026 by the author(s)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Across two public LLM leaderboards, many displayed pairwise rankings do not meet a conventional paired-test resolution target under the actual paired evaluation design: 11 of 40 Open LLM Leaderboard v1 pairwise comparisons and 4 of 9 MMLU-Pro top-10 adjacent-rank pairs are unresolved at (alpha, 1-beta) = (0.05, 0.8). The MMLU-Pro count rises to 6/9 under real subject-level clustering and stays at 5-6 out of 9 in 99.9% of category-bootstrap resamples. We frame paired LLM evaluation as a hypothesis-testing problem, invert level-alpha, power-(1-beta) tests, and report a per-pair resolution ratio q = N/N* as the primary diagnostic. A sharp small-effect expansion with an explicit second-order constant shows that the widely-used unpaired Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho) shortcut deviates from the correct N* by approximately a factor of two in the close-comparison regime, a deficit that three of five off-the-shelf calculators(Cohen 1988, G*Power, R pwr) silently inherit when the user post-multiplies their per-arm output by (1-rho). The unresolved-pair pattern remains under multiplicity correction and anytime-valid sequential testing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：配对大语言模型评估的分辨率诊断
在两个公开的大语言模型排行榜中，许多显示的成对排名在实际配对评估设计下未能达到常规配对测试的分辨率目标：在Open LLM Leaderboard v1的40个成对比较中，有11个未解决；在MMLU-Pro的前10名相邻排名对中，有4个未解决（在α=0.05，1-β=0.8条件下）。在真实受试者层面聚类后，MMLU-Pro的未解决对数上升至6/9，在99.9%的类别Bootstrap重采样中仍保持在5-6/9。我们将配对大语言模型评估视为假设检验问题，反转α水平、1-β功效检验，并报告每对分辨率比q = N/N*作为主要诊断指标。一个具有明确二阶常数的尖锐小效应扩展表明，广泛使用的未配对Cohen-h加(1-ρ)快捷方法在近距离比较情形下，其正确N*值的偏差约为两倍，而五种现成计算器（Cohen 1988, G*Power, R pwr）中的三种在用户将每臂输出乘以(1-ρ)后，会静默继承这一缺陷。即使在多重性校正和任意时刻有效的序贯检验下，未解决对的模式依然存在。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the frequent failure of LLM pairwise rankings to meet statistical resolution targets on public leaderboards, the paper frames paired LLM evaluation as a hypothesis-testing problem and introduces a diagnostic metric q = N/N* to quantify resolution. It demonstrates that widely used unpaired shortcuts for effect size estimation, such as Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho), systematically underestimate required sample sizes by roughly a factor of two in close-comparison scenarios, a flaw inherited by several off-the-shelf calculators. Experimental results across two leaderboards show that up to 6 out of 9 top-ranked pairs remain unresolved under realistic clustering and resampling, even after multiplicity correction or sequential testing adjustments.
鉴于公开排行榜上大量配对式LLM评估未能达到常规分辨率目标，本文将配对比较视为假设检验问题，提出分辨率诊断指标q = N/N*（N为所需样本量，N*为实际样本量）。研究发现，广泛使用的非配对近似方法（如Cohen-h+(1-ρ)）在接近比较场景下低估样本需求约两倍，且多个现成计算器（如Cohen 1988、G*Power、R pwr）在用户乘以(1-ρ)后仍保留此缺陷。实验表明，在两个排行榜上，Open LLM v1中11/40对、MMLU-Pro前10名相邻对中4–6/9对在标准条件下仍无法分辨，即使经过多重性校正或序列检验仍无改善。
SpecBench: Evaluating Specification-Level Reasoning for Software Engineering LLM Agents
Authors: Grant Hamblin, Kevin Song, Zhanda Zhu, Anand Jayarajan, Sihang Liu, Nandita Vijaykumar, Gennady Pekhimenko
First: 2026-05-28T17:54:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:54:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Software engineering (SWE) agents are transitioning from code generation to full software development lifecycle automation. A critical phase in this lifecycle is specification design: transforming initial proposals into carefully considered requirements through expert review. Existing benchmarks such as SWE-Bench are implementation-focused by measuring the agent's ability to generate code given fixed, precise design requirements. This formulation assumes specifications are correct and complete. In real-world complex and critical software systems, initial specifications are often incomplete and flawed, requiring extensive expert reviews and revisions before being accepted for implementation. To fill this gap, we introduce SpecBench to evaluate specification-level reasoning: the ability to generate complete, unambiguous, consistent, and correct system specifications. SpecBench tasks are derived from the Request for Comments (RFC) process used by mature open-source projects. For each task, an agent is given an initial design proposal, the project codebase, and all past project RFC discussions. The agent is tasked with identifying specification deficiencies: omissions, ambiguities, inconsistencies, or incorrect assumptions in the initial proposal. We evaluate predictions against critiques raised by expert maintainers during historical RFC reviews. SpecBench contains tasks from 5 diverse repositories: Kubernetes, React, Rust, TVM, and vLLM. We evaluate state-of-the-art SWE agents on SpecBench, analyzing their capacity to reason about system design without execution feedback. The best performing agent, GPT-5.4, achieves 44.4% accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpecBench：评估软件工程LLM代理的规格级别推理能力
软件工程（SWE）代理正从代码生成转向全软件开发生命周期自动化。该生命周期中的关键阶段是规格设计：通过专家评审将初始提案转化为经过深思熟虑的需求。现有基准如SWE-Bench侧重于实现能力，通过测量代理在给定固定精确设计需求下生成代码的能力来评估。该方法假设规格正确且完整。但在现实世界中复杂且关键的软件系统中，初始规格常不完整或有缺陷，需经大量专家评审和修订后方可接受实施。为填补这一空白，我们引入SpecBench以评估规格级别推理能力：即生成完整、无歧义、一致且正确的系统规格的能力。SpecBench任务源自成熟开源项目所采用的请求评论（RFC）流程。每个任务中，代理获得初始设计提案、项目代码库及所有历史RFC讨论。代理需识别初始提案中的规格缺陷：遗漏、歧义、不一致或错误假设。我们通过历史RFC评审中专家维护者提出的意见评估代理预测结果。SpecBench包含来自5个多样化仓库的任务：Kubernetes、React、Rust、TVM和vLLM。我们评估当前最先进的SWE代理在SpecBench上的表现，分析其在无执行反馈情况下对系统设计的推理能力。表现最佳的代理GPT-5.4准确率为44.4%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate software engineering LLM agents beyond code generation, this work introduces SpecBench to assess their ability to reason at the specification level—identifying flaws in initial design proposals before implementation. Drawing from the RFC review process of mature open-source projects, the benchmark presents agents with incomplete or flawed specifications, project codebases, and historical RFC discussions, requiring them to detect omissions, ambiguities, inconsistencies, or incorrect assumptions. Evaluated across five repositories—Kubernetes, React, Rust, TVM, and vLLM—the best-performing agent, GPT-5.4, achieved 44.4% accuracy, demonstrating limited but measurable capability in specification-level reasoning without execution feedback.
为评估软件工程LLM代理在代码生成之外的能力，本研究提出SpecBench基准，用于衡量代理在设计阶段识别初始提案缺陷（如遗漏、模糊、矛盾或错误假设）的能力。该基准借鉴成熟开源项目RFC评审流程，为代理提供设计提案、代码库及历史RFC讨论，要求其检测规范缺陷。在五个不同项目中评估后，无执行反馈下表现最佳的GPT-5.4准确率仅为44.4%，由专家评审者判定。
A Heterogeneous Architecture for Robot RL Beyond GPU-Dominant Paradigms
Authors: Yufei Jia, Zhanxiang Cao, Mingrui Yu, Heng Zhang, Shenyu Chen, Dixuan Jiang, Meng Li, Xiaofan Li, Yiyang Liu, Junzhe Wu, Zheng Li, XiLin Fang, Tingyu Cui, Shengcheng Fu, Haoyang Li, Anqi Wang, Zifan Wang, Dongjie Zhu, Chenyu Cao, Zhenbiao Huang, Ziang Zheng, Jie Lu, Xin Ma, Zhengyang Wei, Xiang Zhao, Tianyue Zhan, Ye He, Yuxiang Chen, Yizhou Jiang, Yue Li, Haizhou Ge, Yuhang Dong, Fan Jia, Ziheng Zhang, Meng Zhang, Xiwa Deng, Zhixing Chen, Hanyang Shao, Chenxin Dong, Yixuan Li, Yizhi Chen, Bokui Chen, Kaifeng Zhang, Hanqing Cui, Yusen Qin, Ruqi Huang, Lei Han, Tiancai Wang, Xiang Li, Yue Gao, Guyue Zhou
First: 2026-05-28T17:53:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:53:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Simulation-based RL for contemporary robot control is increasingly organized around GPU-resident simulation: physics, rollout collection, and learning are placed on a single GPU-centric execution path. This paradigm has greatly improved training speed, but it has also encouraged a default assumption that efficient training requires physics to reside on the GPU. We revisit this assumption. Our view is that, in simulation-dominated robot control, the essential question is not which processor runs physics, but whether simulation throughput, policy learning, and runtime synchronization form an efficient end-to-end loop. We present UniLab, a heterogeneous CPU-simulation / GPU-learning architecture that decouples CPU-parallel simulation from GPU policy updates through a unified runtime for data movement, buffering, and synchronization. UniLab is implemented as a complete and extensible training system using MuJoCoUni and MotrixSim CPU-batched physics backends, supporting PPO, SAC, FlashSAC, TD3, and APPO. On representative simulation-based robot control tasks, UniLab improves end-to-end training efficiency by 3--10$\times$ under the same hardware configuration, while reducing dependence on the NVIDIA CUDA-based software stack and supporting cross-platform execution on the Apple macOS platform and the AMD ROCm and Intel XPU accelerator backends. These results show that GPU simulation is an effective path to efficient training, but not a necessary one, broadening the practical system choices available for robot RL training. Project page: https://github.com/unilabsim/UniLab.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越GPU主导范式的机器人强化学习异构架构
当前机器人控制的基于模拟的强化学习日益围绕GPU驻留的模拟组织：物理仿真、轨迹采样与学习被置于单一的GPU为中心的执行路径上。该范式极大提升了训练速度，但也促使人们默认认为高效训练需要物理仿真运行在GPU上。我们重新审视这一假设。我们认为，在以模拟为主导的机器人控制中，核心问题并非哪个处理器运行物理仿真，而是模拟吞吐量、策略学习与运行时同步是否构成高效端到端循环。我们提出UniLab，一种异构的CPU模拟/GPU学习架构，通过统一运行时实现数据移动、缓冲与同步，将CPU并行模拟与GPU策略更新解耦。UniLab基于MuJoCoUni和MotrixSim CPU批处理物理后端实现，支持PPO、SAC、FlashSAC、TD3和APPO等算法，作为完整且可扩展的训练系统。在代表性基于模拟的机器人控制任务中，UniLab在相同硬件配置下将端到端训练效率提升3至10倍，降低对NVIDIA CUDA软件栈的依赖，并支持在Apple macOS平台及AMD ROCm与Intel XPU加速器后端跨平台运行。结果表明，GPU模拟是实现高效训练的有效路径，但非必要路径，从而拓宽了机器人强化学习训练的实用系统选择。项目页面：https://github.com/unilabsim/UniLab。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the over-reliance on GPU-centric simulation in robot reinforcement learning, this work challenges the assumption that physics must run on the GPU to achieve efficient training. It proposes UniLab, a heterogeneous architecture that separates CPU-based simulation from GPU-based policy learning using a unified runtime for data management and synchronization. Implemented with CPU-batched physics backends and supporting multiple RL algorithms, UniLab demonstrates 3–10x faster end-to-end training efficiency under identical hardware, while reducing dependency on NVIDIA CUDA and enabling cross-platform execution on macOS, AMD ROCm, and Intel XPU.
鉴于机器人强化学习中GPU主导的仿真范式虽提升训练速度但并非必要，作者提出UniLab异构架构，通过统一运行时解耦CPU仿真与GPU策略更新，实现数据传输、缓冲与同步管理。该架构支持多种RL算法，采用CPU批处理物理引擎，在相同硬件下实现3–10倍端到端训练效率提升，同时降低对NVIDIA CUDA的依赖，支持macOS、AMD ROCm及Intel XPU等跨平台运行。
Preference-Shaped Expected Hypervolume and R2 Improvement: Exact Computation and Monotonicity
Authors: Michael T. M. Emmerich
First: 2026-05-27T17:02:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:53:48+00:00
Comments: 17 pages; Changes v1 (added strict Pareto compliance proof, removed missing figure references and redundant graphics section, added Liang et al 2026 citation in outlook. Improved figures and language
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper studies preference-shaped expected improvement criteria for Bayesian multiobjective optimization. We consider two indicator families which are often used for similar algorithmic purposes, but which are geometrically different. The hypervolume indicator is based on a dystopian reference point and measures dominated volume in objective space. The R2 indicator is based on a utopian point and evaluates approximation sets through weighted Tchebycheff scalarization envelopes. The purpose of the paper is to make precise which preference transformations preserve exact computation, Pareto compatibility, and monotonicity properties, and which transformations change the underlying geometry. On the hypervolume side, we revisit canonical EHVI through the Deng representation, formulate product-density weighted EHVI in desirability coordinates, discuss cone-based EHVI as ordinary EHVI after a linear cone transformation, and separate these cases from truncated EHVI, where variance monotonicity may fail. On the R2 side, we prove that exact integral R2 improvement is not, in general, an ordinary objective-space weighted hypervolume. The obstruction is lower-dimensional: Lebesgue-density hypervolume cannot see certain boundary contributions that Tchebycheff scalarizations still detect. We then show that exact integral R2 improvement is exactly a scalarization-space volume, namely the measure of the Tchebycheff shadow between the incumbent scalarization envelope and the reference envelope. This representation yields finite-sum ER2I algorithms for discrete R2, quadrature methods for exact integral R2, and an achievement-space Gaussian surrogate formulation in which ER2I is an integral of scalar Gaussian expected improvements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：偏好形状的期望超体积与R2改进：精确计算与单调性
本文研究了贝叶斯多目标优化中的偏好形状期望改进准则。我们考虑两种常用于类似算法目的但几何上不同的指标族：超体积指标基于反乌托邦参考点，测量目标空间中被支配的体积；R2指标基于乌托邦点，通过加权切比雪夫标量化包络评估近似集合。本文旨在明确哪些偏好变换能保持精确计算、帕累托兼容性和单调性性质，哪些变换会改变其底层几何结构。在超体积方面，我们通过邓表示重新审视标准EHVI，提出在可欲性坐标下的乘积密度加权EHVI，讨论基于锥体的EHVI作为线性锥变换后的普通EHVI，将其与截断EHVI区分开（后者可能违反方差单调性）。在R2方面，我们证明一般情况下精确积分R2改进并非普通目标空间加权超体积。障碍源于低维性：勒贝格密度超体积无法检测某些边界贡献，而切比雪夫标量化仍能检测。随后我们证明精确积分R2改进恰好是标量化空间中的体积，即当前标量化包络与参考包络之间切比雪夫阴影的测度。该表示可导出离散R2的有限和ER2I算法、精确积分R2的数值积分方法，以及在成就空间中基于高斯代理的ER2I积分形式（其中ER2I是标量高斯期望改进的积分）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how preference transformations affect Bayesian multiobjective optimization criteria, this paper analyzes two geometrically distinct indicators—hypervolume and R2—to determine which transformations preserve exact computation, Pareto compatibility, and monotonicity. For hypervolume, the authors revisit canonical EHVI via the Deng representation, introduce product-density weighted EHVI in desirability coordinates, and distinguish cone-based EHVI from truncated EHVI where variance monotonicity may fail. For R2, they prove that exact integral R2 improvement is not generally equivalent to objective-space weighted hypervolume due to boundary effects undetectable by Lebesgue-density measures, but instead corresponds to a scalarization-space volume—the Tchebycheff shadow between incumbent and reference envelopes—enabling finite-sum algorithms for discrete R2, quadrature methods for exact integral R2, and a Gaussian surrogate formulation for ER2I.

Archon: A Unified Multimodal Model for Holistic Digital Human Generation
Authors: Chong Bao, Shichen Liu, Lijun Yu, David Futschik, Stylianos Moschoglou, Shefali Srivastava, Ziqian Bai, Feitong Tan, Guofeng Zhang, Zhaopeng Cui, Sean Fanello, Yinda Zhang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:53:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:53:27+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026. Project Page: https://zju3dv.github.io/archon/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Digital humans are fundamental to immersive interaction, yet creating a unified model for holistic modalities, including text, audio, motion, and visual content, remains an open challenge. In this paper, we present Archon, a fully pretrained, human-centric unified multimodal model for holistic avatar generation. Archon unifies seven modalities with modality-specific tokenizers, and a native autoregressive unified multimodal model pretrained on synchronized modalities and 72 diverse tasks to model holistic joint distributions. To address the token explosion challenge in high-fidelity talking videos, we introduce a memory-efficient semantic video reparameterization, achieving 4x token reduction while preserving fine-grained dynamics, coupled with a semantic-driven video diffusion decoder. We further propose a "Thinking in Modality" that decomposes ambiguous cross-modal tasks into stepwise thinking in an alternative chain of modality, progressively enhancing fidelity and controllability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Archon achieves superior or comparable performance across diverse digital human generation tasks, validating the effectiveness of our unified framework. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/archon/.

Driven by the need for unified digital human generation across text, audio, motion, and visual modalities, Archon introduces a human-centric multimodal model pretrained on synchronized data across seven modalities using modality-specific tokenizers. To handle high-fidelity video token explosion, it employs a memory-efficient semantic video reparameterization that reduces tokens by 4x while retaining fine-grained dynamics, paired with a semantic-driven video diffusion decoder. Its "Thinking in Modality" framework decomposes ambiguous tasks into stepwise modality chains to improve fidelity and control. City-Mesh3R: Simulation-Ready City-Scale 3D Mesh Reconstruction from Multi-View Images
Authors: Sayan Paul, Sourav Ghosh, Siddharth Katageri, Soumyadip Maity, Sanjana Sinha, Brojeshwar Bhowmick
Venue: CVPR Oral Presentation
First: 2026-05-28T17:53:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:53:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the USM3D Workshop Proceedings at the IEEE/CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (CVPR) 2026 as an Oral Presentation. Project page: https://citymesh3r.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
City-scale 3D surface reconstruction from multiview images for downstream 3D simulation, poses highly challenging problems due to the scale and complexity of urban scenes. Existing city-scale 3D reconstruction methods based on NeRF, Gaussian Splatting etc. often fail to recover 3D meshes ready for simulation due to incomplete/missing geometry and irregular, noisy surfaces. Scaling existing small-scale 3D reconstruction methods to arbitrarily large urban scenes is highly infeasible due to their computational complexity. We present City-Mesh3R, a scalable framework for reconstructing watertight surface meshes directly from large unordered image collections. Unlike recent methods which use global sparse SfM point-cloud initialization followed by a distributed 3D dense reconstruction of large-scale scenes, our method follows an end-to-end images-to-mesh 3D reconstruction approach using a divide-and-conquer strategy. The sparse city map is reconstructed via topological image clustering, cluster-wise independent sparse SfM and map merging, without need for exhaustive image feature matching. Then this map is partitioned spatially to perform geometry-aware camera selection, followed by dense surface reconstruction and surface refinement using curvature-aware adaptive vertex density remeshing. These partition meshes are then stitched together to produce the global mesh of the city. The proposed end-to-end framework is evaluated on city-scale reconstruction datasets. As demonstrated by our qualitative and quantitative results, our proposed method yields high-fidelity watertight 3D meshes with regular geometry, capturing fine surface details, and is suitable for scaling to arbitrarily large scenes owing to the end-to-end processing in a distributed setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：City-Mesh3R：从多视角图像直接重建适用于模拟的城市级3D网格
从多视角图像重建城市级3D表面，以支持下游3D模拟，因城市场景的规模和复杂性而极具挑战。现有基于NeRF、高斯点绘等方法的城市级3D重建技术，常因几何不完整或表面不规则、噪声大而无法生成适用于模拟的3D网格。将现有小规模3D重建方法扩展至任意大规模城市场景在计算复杂度上极不可行。我们提出City-Mesh3R，一种可扩展框架，可直接从大规模无序图像集合重建闭合表面网格。与近期方法（先用全局稀疏SfM点云初始化，再分布式重建大规模场景）不同，我们的方法采用端到端图像到网格的3D重建策略，结合分而治之策略。通过拓扑图像聚类、集群内独立稀疏SfM及地图合并重建稀疏城市地图，无需穷举图像特征匹配。随后将地图按空间划分，进行几何感知相机选择，再通过曲率感知自适应顶点密度重网格化进行密集表面重建与表面优化。最后将各分区网格拼接，生成城市全局网格。该端到端框架在城市级重建数据集上评估，结果表明，我们的方法可生成高保真、闭合的3D网格，几何规则、细节丰富，且因分布式端到端处理，可扩展至任意大规模场景。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for simulation-ready 3D city meshes from large-scale image collections, City-Mesh3R introduces an end-to-end framework that bypasses traditional dense reconstruction bottlenecks. It employs a divide-and-conquer strategy: first clustering images topologically to build a sparse city map via independent SfM and map merging, then spatially partitioning the map to select geometry-aware cameras, followed by dense surface reconstruction and curvature-aware remeshing. Finally, the partitioned meshes are stitched into a global watertight mesh. Experiments show the method produces high-fidelity, regular, and detailed meshes suitable for simulation, while scaling efficiently to arbitrarily large urban scenes through distributed processing.
为满足从大规模图像集合中重建适用于模拟的高保真城市级3D网格的需求，City-Mesh3R提出了一种端到端框架，规避传统密集重建瓶颈。该方法采用分而治之策略：首先通过拓扑图像聚类构建稀疏城市地图，经独立SfM与地图合并；随后按空间划分地图，选择几何感知相机，进行密集表面重建与曲率自适应重网格化；最后将各部分网格缝合为全局闭合网格。实验表明，该方法生成高保真、规则且细节丰富的网格，可高效扩展至任意规模城市场景，适用于分布式处理。
Grounded 3D-Aware Spatial Vision-Language Modeling
Authors: An-Chieh Cheng, Yang Fu, Yatai Ji, Ligeng Zhu, Guanqi Zhan, Zhuoyang Zhang, Zhaojing Yang, Song Han, Yao Lu, Pavlo Molchanov, Vidya Nariyambut Murali, Jan Kautz, Xiaolong Wang, Hongxu Yin, Sifei Liu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:51:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:51:38+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 https://www.anjiecheng.me/gr3d
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present GR3D, a spatial vision language model equipped with three complementary grounding capabilities--explicit 2D grounding, implicit 2D grounding, and monocular 3D grounding--within a single framework. GR3D introduces an implicit grounding mechanism that identifies entity mentions during generation and inserts the corresponding region tokens into the text stream, allowing the model to reference visual evidence on the fly when producing spatial chain-of-thought responses. In parallel, a region-prompted monocular 3D grounding design predicts 3D bounding boxes in the camera view from grounded region queries, supported by intrinsic-aware normalization and dense geometric supervision. Together, these grounding capabilities enable GR3D to decompose complex spatial understanding problems into grounded 2D perception followed by 3D inference. GR3D achieves consistent improvements across grounded and non-grounded spatial benchmarks, demonstrating grounding as an effective inductive bias for strengthening spatial understanding in VLMs. These grounding capabilities collectively enhance general spatial understanding beyond the grounding task itself.
中文标题/摘要

The implicit grounding dynamically injects region tokens into text during generation to anchor spatial reasoning, while the monocular 3D grounding predicts 3D bounding boxes from region queries using intrinsic-aware normalization and geometric supervision. Experiments show GR3D consistently outperforms baselines across both grounded and non-grounded spatial benchmarks, confirming that grounding serves as a strong inductive bias that improves general spatial understanding beyond its original task.
为提升视觉语言模型的空间推理能力，GR3D在统一框架内融合了显式2D、隐式2D和单目3D三种接地机制。隐式接地在生成过程中动态插入区域标记以锚定空间推理，单目3D接地则通过内参感知归一化和密集几何监督，从区域查询预测3D边界框。实验表明，GR3D在有无接地任务的多个空间基准上均优于基线模型，验证了接地作为强归纳偏置，能有效提升模型超越接地任务本身的通用空间理解能力。
Efficient Training-Free Multi-Token Prediction via Embedding-Space Probing
Authors: Raghavv Goel, Mukul Gagrani, Mingu Lee, Chris Lott
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-03-18T17:14:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:50:11+00:00
Comments: v2: Accepted at ICML 2026. Updated experiments replaced tok/s with speedup ratio over AR baseline; improved exposition in Section 3.1 (mask token initialization) and Section 4 (ablations)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) possess latent multi-token prediction (MTP) abilities despite being trained only for next-token generation. We introduce ESP (Embedding-Space Probing), a simple and training-free MTP method that probes an LLM using on-the-fly mask tokens drawn from its embedding space, enabling parallel future-token prediction without modifying weights or relying on draft models. ESP constructs a speculative token tree by sampling Top-K candidates from mask-token logits and applies a lightweight pruning rule to retain high-probability continuations. During generation, predictions are verified in parallel, yielding lossless decoding while significantly reducing model calls and increasing token throughput. ESP consistently outperforms existing training-free baselines, improving acceptance length by 7-11% over LADE on LLaMA3 and 7-8% on Qwen3, and increasing throughput by up to 15-19% over the strongest baseline. Finally, we provide theoretical insight and empirical evidence showing that decoder layers naturally align mask-token representations with next-token states, enabling accurate multi-step prediction without retraining or auxiliary models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过嵌入空间探测实现高效的无训练多标记预测
尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）仅被训练用于下一个标记生成，但它们仍具备潜在的多标记预测（MTP）能力。我们提出了ESP（嵌入空间探测），一种简单且无需训练的MTP方法，通过从其嵌入空间中动态抽取掩码标记来探测LLM，从而在不修改权重或依赖草稿模型的情况下实现并行的未来标记预测。ESP通过从掩码标记的logits中采样Top-K候选标记，构建推测标记树，并应用轻量级剪枝规则保留高概率延续。在生成过程中，预测结果并行验证，实现无损解码，同时显著减少模型调用次数并提升标记吞吐量。ESP在各项指标上持续优于现有无训练基线方法，在LLaMA3上较LADE提升7-11%的接受长度，在Qwen3上提升7-8%，吞吐量较最强基线提升15-19%。最后，我们提供了理论见解和实证证据，表明解码器层天然地将掩码标记表示与下一个标记状态对齐，从而无需再训练或辅助模型即可实现准确的多步预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the latent multi-token prediction capability of large language models despite being trained only for next-token generation, ESP introduces a training-free method that probes LLMs using dynamically sampled mask tokens from their embedding space to enable parallel future-token prediction without weight modification or draft models. It constructs a speculative token tree by sampling Top-K candidates from mask-token logits and pruning low-probability paths, then verifies predictions in parallel during generation, achieving lossless decoding while reducing model calls and boosting token throughput. Experiments show ESP outperforms existing baselines by improving acceptance length by 7-11% over LADE on LLaMA3 and 7-8% on Qwen3, and increasing throughput by up to 15-19% over the strongest baseline, with theoretical support showing decoder layers naturally align mask-token representations with next-token states.
鉴于大语言模型虽仅训练用于下一词预测却具备潜在的多词预测能力，ESP提出一种无需训练的方法，通过从嵌入空间动态采样掩码词来探测模型，实现无需修改权重或依赖辅助模型的并行未来词预测。该方法通过从掩码词对数中采样Top-K候选词并应用轻量级剪枝规则保留高概率延续，实现无损解码同时减少模型调用并提升词吞吐量。实验表明，ESP优于现有基线方法，在LLaMA3和Qwen3上较LADE分别提升接受长度7-11%和7-8%，吞吐量提升最高达15-19%，理论分析表明解码器层天然使掩码词表示与下一词状态对齐，无需重训练即可实现准确多步预测。
MiAD: Mirage Atom Diffusion for De Novo Crystal Generation
Authors: Andrey Okhotin, Maksim Nakhodnov, Nikita Kazeev, Mikhail Lazarev, Andrey E Ustyuzhanin, Dmitry Vetrov
First: 2025-11-18T12:29:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:48:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In recent years, diffusion-based models have demonstrated exceptional performance in searching for simultaneously stable, unique, and novel (S.U.N.) crystalline materials. However, most of these models don't have the ability to change the number of atoms in the crystal during the generation process, which limits the variability of model sampling trajectories. In this paper, we demonstrate the severity of this restriction and introduce a simple yet powerful technique, mirage infusion, which enables diffusion models to change the state of the atoms that make up the crystal from existent to non-existent (mirage) and vice versa. We show that this technique improves model quality by up to x2.5 compared to the same model without this modification. The resulting model, Mirage Atom Diffusion (MiAD), is an equivariant joint diffusion model for de novo crystal generation that is capable of altering the number of atoms during the generation process. MiAD achieves an 8.2% S.U.N. rate on the MP-20 dataset, which substantially exceeds existing state-of-the-art approaches. Code: https://github.com/andrey-okhotin/miad.git
中文标题/摘要
标题：MiAD：用于从头生成晶体的幻影原子扩散模型
近年来，基于扩散的模型在搜索同时稳定、独特且新颖（S.U.N.）的晶体材料方面表现出色。然而，大多数此类模型在生成过程中无法改变晶体中原子的数量，这限制了模型采样轨迹的多样性。本文展示了这一限制的严重性，并引入了一种简单而强大的技术——幻影注入，使扩散模型能够将构成晶体的原子状态从存在变为不存在（幻影），反之亦然。我们证明，该技术相比未做此修改的相同模型，可将模型质量提升高达2.5倍。所提出的模型——幻影原子扩散（MiAD），是一种用于从头生成晶体的等变联合扩散模型，能够在生成过程中改变原子数量。MiAD在MP-20数据集上实现了8.2%的S.U.N.率，显著超越现有最先进方法。代码：https://github.com/andrey-okhotin/miad.git
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that existing diffusion models cannot dynamically alter atom counts during crystal generation, this work introduces 'mirage infusion'—a technique enabling atoms to be toggled between existent and non-existent states. This allows the model to generate crystals with variable atom numbers, enhancing sampling diversity. The resulting Mirage Atom Diffusion (MiAD) model, an equivariant joint diffusion model, improves generation quality by up to 2.5x over baseline models and achieves an 8.2% S.U.N. rate on the MP-20 dataset, outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods.
为解决现有扩散模型在晶体生成过程中无法动态调整原子数量的问题，本文提出Mirage Atom Diffusion（MiAD）方法，通过“幻影注入”技术使原子在生成过程中可出现或消失，从而提升采样多样性和模型质量，相较未采用该技术的基线模型性能提升高达2.5倍。实验表明，MiAD在MP-20数据集上实现8.2%的S.U.N.率，显著优于当前最先进方法，有效生成稳定、独特且新颖的晶体材料。
Permutation-Invariant Spectral Learning via Dyson Diffusion
Authors: Tassilo Schwarz, Cai Dieball, Constantin Kogler, Renaud Lambiotte, Arnaud Doucet, Aljaž Godec, George Deligiannidis
First: 2025-10-09T17:52:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:45:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models are central to generative modeling and have been adapted to graphs by diffusing adjacency matrix representations. The challenge of having up to $n!$ such representations for graphs with $n$ nodes is only partially mitigated by using permutation-equivariant learning architectures. Despite their computational efficiency, existing graph diffusion models struggle to distinguish certain graph families and their spectra, unless graph data are augmented with ad hoc features. This shortcoming stems from enforcing the inductive bias within the learning architecture. In this work, we leverage random matrix theory to analytically extract the spectral properties of the diffusion process, allowing us to push most of the inductive bias from the architecture into the dynamics. Building on this, we introduce the Dyson Diffusion Model, which employs Dyson's Brownian motion to capture the spectral dynamics of an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process on the adjacency matrix. Furthermore, conditioned on the spectral dynamics, we formulate a Lie group diffusion, appropriately modeling the remaining degrees of freedom. Strikingly, the resulting learning problem becomes permutation invariant at the Lie algebra level. We demonstrate that the Dyson Diffusion Model learns graph spectra accurately and outperforms existing graph diffusion models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过Dyson扩散实现排列不变的谱学习
扩散模型是生成建模的核心，已被用于图结构中，通过扩散邻接矩阵表示。对于n个节点的图，存在高达n!种此类表示，尽管使用排列等变学习架构部分缓解了这一问题，但现有图扩散模型仍难以区分某些图家族及其谱，除非图数据附加人工特征。这一缺陷源于学习架构中强制施加的归纳偏置。本文利用随机矩阵理论，从理论上提取扩散过程的谱性质，将大部分归纳偏置从架构中移至动态过程。在此基础上，我们提出Dyson扩散模型，利用Dyson布朗运动捕捉邻接矩阵上Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的谱动态。此外，基于谱动态，我们构建李群扩散，适配剩余自由度。令人惊讶的是，所得学习问题在李代数层面实现排列不变性。我们证明Dyson扩散模型能准确学习图谱，并优于现有图扩散模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of graph diffusion models in distinguishing graph spectra due to permutation-equivariant architectures, this work introduces the Dyson Diffusion Model, which shifts inductive bias into the dynamics using random matrix theory and Dyson’s Brownian motion to model spectral evolution via an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process on adjacency matrices. A Lie group diffusion is then conditioned on spectral dynamics to capture residual degrees of freedom, yielding permutation invariance at the Lie algebra level. Experiments show the model accurately learns graph spectra and outperforms existing graph diffusion models in spectral discrimination tasks.
为了解决现有图扩散模型在区分图谱时因架构的置换等变性而受限的问题，本文提出Dyson扩散模型，通过随机矩阵理论将归纳偏置从架构转移到动态过程。该模型利用Dyson布朗运动建模邻接矩阵的谱演化，并结合李群扩散捕捉剩余自由度，从而在李代数层面实现置换不变性。实验表明，该模型在图谱学习上精度更高，且无需额外特征增强即可优于现有图扩散方法。
MedCase-Structured: A Text-to-FHIR Dataset for Benchmarking Diagnostic Reasoning in Clinically Realistic EHR Settings
Authors: Valentina Bui Muti, Eugénie Dulout, Ziquan Fu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:42:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:42:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026 Structured Data for Health Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) show promise for clinical reasoning and decision support, but evaluation in realistic, electronic health record-congruent settings remains limited. Existing benchmarks often rely on static datasets or unstructured inputs that do not reflect the structured, interoperable data formats used in clinical systems. We introduce a pipeline for generating clinically realistic HL7 FHIR R4 bundles from unstructured text, enabling controllable evaluation of clinical decision support systems. The pipeline combines staged LLM generation with terminology-grounded validation and repair to reduce hallucinated codes and enforce structural and semantic consistency. Applying this approach to MedCaseReasoning, we construct MedCase-Structured, a synthetic dataset aligned with clinician-authored diagnostic cases, achieving valid FHIR generation for 82.5% of cases. Evaluation on MedCase-Structured reveals consistently lower diagnostic accuracy for LLMs on structured FHIR inputs than with plain text, highlighting the importance of deployment-aligned benchmarking.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedCase-Structured：用于在临床现实的电子健康记录环境中基准测试诊断推理的文本到FHIR数据集
大型语言模型（LLMs）在临床推理和决策支持方面展现出潜力，但在真实、与电子健康记录一致的环境中评估仍显不足。现有基准常依赖静态数据集或非结构化输入，无法反映临床系统中使用的结构化、可互操作的数据格式。我们提出了一种从非结构化文本生成临床现实的HL7 FHIR R4数据包的流程，从而实现对临床决策支持系统的可控评估。该流程结合分阶段LLM生成与基于术语的验证与修复，以减少虚构代码并确保结构与语义一致性。将此方法应用于MedCaseReasoning，我们构建了MedCase-Structured，一个与临床医生撰写的诊断案例对齐的合成数据集，其中82.5%的案例实现了有效的FHIR生成。在MedCase-Structured上的评估表明，LLMs在结构化FHIR输入上的诊断准确率显著低于纯文本输入，凸显了与部署环境对齐的基准测试的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language models in realistic electronic health record environments, this work introduces MedCase-Structured, a synthetic dataset generated from unstructured clinical cases using a pipeline that combines staged LLM generation with terminology-grounded validation and repair to produce valid HL7 FHIR R4 bundles. The method ensures structural and semantic consistency while minimizing hallucinated codes, achieving valid FHIR generation for 82.5% of cases. Experimental results show that LLMs consistently perform worse on structured FHIR inputs compared to plain text, underscoring the critical need for benchmarks aligned with real-world clinical system deployment.
为评估大语言模型在真实电子健康记录环境中的表现，该研究提出MedCase-Structured数据集，通过结合分阶段LLM生成与术语导向的验证与修复流程，从非结构化临床案例中生成符合HL7 FHIR R4标准的结构化数据包。该方法确保结构与语义一致性并减少幻觉代码。实验结果表明，当在结构化FHIR输入上评估时，LLM的诊断准确率显著低于在纯文本上的表现，凸显了与实际临床系统部署对齐的基准测试的重要性。
The Planetary Cost of AI Acceleration, Part II: The 10th Planetary Boundary and the 6.5-Year Countdown
Authors: William Yicheng Zhu, Lei Zhu
First: 2026-04-03T10:42:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:42:01+00:00
Comments: Minor revisions for clarity
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The recent, super-exponential scaling of autonomous Large Language Model (LLM) agents signals a broader, fundamental paradigm shift from machines primarily replacing the human hands (manual labor and mechanical processing) to machines delegating for the human minds (cognition, reasoning, and intention). The uncontrolled offloading and scaling of "thinking" itself, beyond human's limited but efficient biological capacity, has profound consequences for humanity's heat balance sheet, since thinking, or intelligence, carries thermodynamic consequences. The Earth has already surpassed the heat dissipation threshold required for long-term ecological stability, and projecting based on empirical data reveal a concerning trajectory: without radical structural intervention, anthropogenic heat accumulation will breach critical planetary ecological thresholds in less than 6.5 years, even under the most ideal scenario where Earth Energy Imbalance (EEI) holds constant. In this work, we identify six factors from artificial intelligence that influence the global heat dissipation rate and delineate how their interplay drives society toward one of four broad macroscopic trajectories. We propose that the integration of artificial intelligence and its heat dissipation into the planetary system constitute the tenth planetary boundary (9+1). The core empirical measurement of this boundary is the net-new waste heat generated by exponential AI growth, balanced against its impact on reducing economic and societal inefficiencies and thus baseline anthropogenic waste heat emissions. We demonstrate that managing AI scaling lacks a moderate middle ground: it will either accelerate the breach of critical planetary thermodynamic thresholds, or it will serve as the single most effective lever on stabilizing the other nine planetary boundaries and through which safeguarding human civilization's survival.

标题：人工智能加速的行星成本（第二部分）：第十个行星界限与6.5年倒计时
近期自主大型语言模型（LLM）代理的超指数级扩展，标志着一个更广泛、更根本的范式转变：从机器主要替代人类双手（体力劳动和机械处理），转向机器为人类心智分担任务（认知、推理与意图）。将‘思考’本身超出人类有限但高效的生物能力进行无控制的卸载与扩展，对人类的热平衡表具有深远影响，因为思考或智能本身具有热力学后果。地球已超出维持长期生态稳定所需的热耗散阈值，基于实证数据的预测显示令人担忧的趋势：即使在最理想情况下（地球能量失衡EEI保持恒定），若无根本性结构性干预，人为热积累将在不到6.5年内突破关键的行星生态阈值。本研究识别出六项人工智能因素影响全球热耗散速率，并阐明其相互作用如何推动社会走向四种宏观轨迹之一。我们提出，将人工智能及其热耗散纳入行星系统，构成第十个行星界限（9+1）。该界限的核心实证测量是指数级AI增长所产生净新增废热，与它减少经济和社会低效从而降低基础人为废热排放的影响之间的平衡。我们证明，管理AI扩展不存在温和的中间地带：它要么加速突破关键行星热力学阈值，要么成为稳定其他九个行星界限的最有效杠杆，从而保障人类文明的存续。
