Motivated by the super-exponential scaling of autonomous LLMs and its thermodynamic implications, this study identifies AI-driven heat dissipation as the tenth planetary boundary, arguing that unchecked AI growth threatens to breach critical ecological thresholds within 6.5 years even under ideal conditions. The method involves analyzing six AI-related factors influencing global heat dissipation and modeling their combined effect on societal trajectories. Experimental results show that AI scaling offers no moderate path: either it accelerates planetary overheating, or it becomes the most effective lever to stabilize the other nine planetary boundaries and ensure human civilization’s survival.
鉴于自主LLM的超指数增长及其热力学影响，本研究将AI的散热效应定义为第十个行星边界，指出即使在最理想条件下，若不加控制，AI增长将在6.5年内突破关键生态阈值。研究方法通过分析六个影响全球散热的AI相关因素，并建模其对社会走向的综合影响。实验结果表明，AI扩展不存在中间路径：要么加速行星过热，要么成为稳定其他九个行星边界、保障人类文明存续的最有效手段。
Leave a Window Out: Modifying the Jackknife for Predictive Inference in Time Series
Authors: Hanyang Jiang, Rina Foygel Barber, Ashwin Pananjady, Yao Xie
First: 2026-05-28T17:41:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:41:12+00:00
Comments: 36 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conformal prediction methods enjoy strong theoretical and empirical predictive inference performance, provided the data is exchangeable, and predictors are trained in a memoryless fashion. However, these assumptions and constraints are impractical in many real-data settings, such as time series (where temporal dependence violates exchangeability, and where memoryless predictors will inevitably have poor predictive accuracy). Recent work shows that the split conformal prediction method is robust to these issues of memory-based predictors and deviations from exchangeability that are common features of time-series data. However, since using sample splitting can lead to lower accuracy, this motivates asking whether other predictive inference methods (that do not rely on data splitting) could also be reliably used in the time series setting. In this work, we show that the vanilla leave-one-out jackknife can suffer an arbitrary loss of coverage even in canonical time series models with mild temporal dependence. As a remedy, we propose a careful modification tailored to such settings, which we term the \emph{leave-a-window-out} (LWO) method, and show that it can achieve valid coverage provided that the model-fitting procedure satisfies mild stability properties. Our proofs are based on quantifying the degree to which the data departs from \emph{cyclic exchangeability}, and we introduce new coefficients to measure the extent of this departure. Experiments on time series data demonstrate that our LWO method often enjoys valid coverage when the vanilla jackknife fails to cover, while producing much narrower intervals than split conformal prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：留出一个窗口：为时间序列预测推断修改 jackknife 方法
只要数据满足可交换性，且预测器以无记忆方式训练，置信预测方法在理论和实证预测推断上表现优异。然而，在许多真实数据场景（如时间序列，其中时间依赖性破坏可交换性，无记忆预测器必然预测精度差）中，这些假设和约束不切实际。近期研究表明，分割式置信预测方法对基于记忆的预测器和时间序列数据中常见的可交换性偏离具有鲁棒性。但因样本分割可能导致精度降低，这促使我们思考：是否其他不依赖数据分割的预测推断方法也能可靠应用于时间序列场景。本研究证明，普通留一法 jackknife 在具有轻微时间依赖性的典型时间序列模型中，可能遭受任意程度的置信覆盖损失。为此，我们提出一种针对此类场景的精心修改方法，称为“留出一个窗口”（LWO）方法，并证明只要模型拟合过程满足温和的稳定性条件，该方法即可实现有效覆盖。我们的证明基于量化数据偏离“循环可交换性”的程度，并引入新系数衡量该偏离程度。在时间序列数据上的实验表明，当普通 jackknife 方法失效时，我们的 LWO 方法常能实现有效覆盖，同时生成比分割式置信预测更窄的置信区间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional conformal prediction methods in time series due to temporal dependence and memory-based predictors, this work investigates whether the vanilla jackknife can be adapted for valid predictive inference. The authors demonstrate that the standard leave-one-out jackknife can suffer arbitrary coverage loss in time series with mild dependence, and propose a modified approach called leave-a-window-out (LWO) that preserves coverage under mild model stability assumptions. Theoretical analysis quantifies deviations from cyclic exchangeability using new coefficients, and experiments show that LWO achieves valid coverage where vanilla jackknife fails, while producing significantly narrower prediction intervals than split conformal prediction.
为了解决时间序列数据中因时序依赖和无记忆预测器导致的置信区间失效问题，本文研究了杰克knife方法在该场景下的适用性。作者证明标准留一法jackknife在时间序列中可能完全失效，即使依赖性较弱，因此提出“留窗法”（LWO），即移除连续数据块而非单点。该方法在模型拟合满足温和稳定性假设时可保证覆盖有效性，其理论基于量化数据对循环可交换性的偏离程度，并引入新系数衡量该偏离。实验表明，LWO在标准jackknife失效时仍能保持有效覆盖，且预测区间显著窄于分样本置信方法。
Self-Trained Verification for Training- and Test-Time Self-Improvement
Authors: Chen Henry Wu, Aditi Raghunathan
First: 2026-05-28T17:40:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:40:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-improvement at scale has been a longstanding goal for reasoning models, and there are two natural places to do it: at test time, through verification-refinement (V-R) loops; and at training time, through self-training methods. Both are gated by the same bottleneck: the verifier. V-R loops stall when verifier scores inflate while accuracy stagnates, and when feedback is too generic to act on; self-training fails similarly when bad self-generated data are added to training. Better verification would unlock both, but the capability we want to train, i.e., catching self-generated errors, lacks training signal. To address this challenge, we propose self-trained verification (STV). Our key observation is that, while a model cannot catch these errors alone, it can when shown the reference solution. We turn this asymmetry into a supervision target and train the verifier to imitate a more informed version of itself. At test time, STV substantially improves V-R loops on hard problems, while alternatives (e.g., SFT, RL on verifier scores, and even meta-verifiers) do not. STV roughly doubles accuracy on hard math and lifts it 14x on scientific reasoning tasks (1.5% to 21%). At training time, we additionally train the generator using RL with STV verifier's feedback inside the V-R loop - a procedure we call verifier-in-the-loop training (ViL). Starting from an RL-converged generator, ViL yields a further 33% gain in pass@1. More notably, the generator's standalone pass@1, with no verifier at test time, climbs 30% relative past where standard RL had converged. Hence, the next frontier in reasoning on hard problems may lie in how we train for and with verification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自训练验证：训练与测试时的自我提升
大规模自我提升一直是推理模型的长期目标，其自然实现途径有两个：测试时通过验证-优化（V-R）循环；训练时通过自训练方法。两者均受限于同一瓶颈：验证器。V-R循环在验证器得分上升而准确率停滞、或反馈过于笼统无法操作时停滞；自训练方法在加入质量差的自生成数据时同样失败。更好的验证能力可解锁两者，但我们希望训练的能力——即识别自生成错误——缺乏训练信号。为应对这一挑战，我们提出自训练验证（STV）。我们的核心观察是：模型虽无法单独识别这些错误，但若展示参考解，则可识别。我们将这一不对称性转化为监督目标，训练验证器模仿更知情的自身版本。测试时，STV在难题上显著提升V-R循环性能，而其他方法（如SFT、基于验证器得分的强化学习、甚至元验证器）无此效果。STV在难题数学上将准确率大致翻倍，在科学推理任务上提升14倍（1.5%至21%）。训练时，我们进一步在V-R循环内使用STV验证器反馈训练生成器——我们称之为验证器内循环训练（ViL）。从已收敛的强化学习生成器出发，ViL使pass@1再提升33%。更显著的是，生成器在无验证器测试时的独立pass@1较标准强化学习收敛点提升30%。因此，解决难题推理的下一前沿可能在于我们如何训练并利用验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the bottleneck of verifier performance in both test-time verification-refinement and training-time self-training, this work introduces self-trained verification (STV) to improve error detection in self-generated outputs. STV trains a verifier by having it imitate a more informed version of itself, using reference solutions as supervision, thereby overcoming the lack of training signals for catching self-generated errors. Experimentally, STV significantly enhances V-R loops on hard problems, doubling math accuracy and boosting scientific reasoning performance 14-fold. When combined with verifier-in-the-loop training (ViL), it further improves generator performance by 33% and increases standalone pass@1 by 30% over standard RL, demonstrating that verification-aware training unlocks substantial gains in reasoning capability.
为突破验证器性能限制对测试时验证-优化循环和训练时自训练的制约，本文提出自训练验证（STV），通过参考解引导验证器模仿更知情的自身版本进行训练。测试时，STV显著提升硬问题上的V-R循环表现，数学任务准确率提升一倍，科学推理任务提升14倍（从1.5%至21%）。训练时，结合验证器在环训练（ViL）进一步优化生成器，使pass@1较强化学习收敛基线提升33%，且脱离验证器独立测试时pass@1较标准强化学习提升30%。
Statistical Embeddings for Similarity, Retrieval, and Interpretable Alignment of Numeric Tabular Datasets
Authors: M. Ross Kunz, John Merickel, Keith Wilson
First: 2026-05-28T17:40:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:40:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Numeric tabular datasets are the dominant data format in scientific practice, yet large language models lack native mechanisms for representing numeric datasets in a meaningful way across heterogeneous feature spaces. Existing approaches either target predictive modeling over individual datasets, which requires a shared set of variable definitions, or lack mechanisms for interpretable cross-dataset alignment. The proposed methodology characterizes numeric tabular datasets through structured exploratory data analysis descriptors, embeds those descriptors into a shared vector space using a pretrained sentence transformer, and quantifies cross-dataset similarity via Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA). Furthermore, a penalized formulation of CCA is applied to recover sparse, interpretable variable-level correspondences between datasets, identifying which statistical descriptors or variable-level quantities drive cross-dataset alignment without requiring shared variable names or feature conventions. Differential privacy is optionally applied to the descriptor set prior to embedding, supporting deployment in sensitive data contexts without requiring access to raw observations at time of comparison. The methodology is evaluated across 15 datasets spanning general-purpose benchmarks, materials informatics, and nuclear-grade graphite characterization. Results demonstrate a total P@1 score of 0.9, with known nearest-neighbor retrieval and cluster structure remaining robust across embedding ablations and differential privacy budgets. The proposed framework provides a principled pathway for integrating heterogeneous numeric data into retrieval-augmented generation pipelines while preserving statistical context, with direct applications to data-driven algorithm selection and simulation model initialization for unknown datasets.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of native mechanisms in large language models to meaningfully represent numeric tabular data across heterogeneous feature spaces, the paper proposes a framework that converts structured exploratory data analysis descriptors into embeddings via a pretrained sentence transformer, then uses Canonical Correlation Analysis to quantify cross-dataset similarity and recover sparse, interpretable variable-level correspondences without requiring shared variable names. Evaluated across 15 diverse datasets, the method achieves a P@1 score of 0.9 in retrieval tasks and maintains robust cluster and neighbor structure even under embedding ablations and differential privacy constraints, demonstrating its utility for integrating heterogeneous numeric data into retrieval-augmented pipelines.
该论文针对大语言模型缺乏原生机制以在异构特征空间中有效表示数值表格数据的问题，提出一种方法：将数据集的结构化探索性数据分析描述符嵌入到共享向量空间中，再通过典型相关分析量化跨数据集相似性，并恢复稀疏可解释的变量级对应关系，无需共享变量名。在15个不同领域数据集上的实验表明，该方法在检索任务中达到0.9的P@1分数，且在嵌入消融和差分隐私约束下仍保持聚类结构稳定，适用于将异构数值数据集成到检索增强生成流程中。
MIRA: Mid-training Rubric Anchoring for Source-Aware Data Selection
Authors: Haowen Wang, Yaxin Du, Jian Yang, Jiajun Wu, Shukai Liu, Yuxuan Zhang, Pingjie Wang, Siheng Chen, Tuney Zheng, Ming Zhou, Xianglong Liu
First: 2026-05-28T17:40:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:40:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mid-training has become an important stage in modern LLM development, using large-scale curated mixtures to strengthen capabilities before final post-training. Its data selection problem is distinct: the data are optimized under a pretraining-style objective at near-pretraining scale, but are curated toward downstream capabilities and drawn from heterogeneous sources with different formats and training roles. As a result, effective selection requires both scalability and source-adaptive semantic criteria. Existing model-based methods scale well, but provide only implicit quality signals. Semantic selection methods offer stronger judgments, but usually assume fixed rubrics or standardized data formats. To address this mismatch, we propose MIRA, a source-aware filtering framework based on self-anchored rubric discovery. The key idea is to make rubric construction part of data selection: MIRA first discovers what should be evaluated for each source group, then distills those judgments into scalable student scorers for full-corpus filtering. On code-oriented mid-training with 21 sources and 5 source groups, MIRA outperforms selection baselines across nine code benchmarks and matches the full-corpus run while using only half the tokens.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and source-adaptive data selection during mid-training of large language models, MIRA introduces a framework that anchors rubric construction directly into the selection process. It first discovers evaluation criteria specific to each data source group, then distills these into lightweight student scorers for efficient filtering across the full corpus. Experiments on code-oriented mid-training with 21 heterogeneous sources show MIRA outperforms existing baselines across nine benchmarks and achieves performance comparable to full-corpus training while using only half the tokens.
为了解决大语言模型中段训练阶段数据选择需兼顾可扩展性与源适应性的问题，MIRA提出一种基于自锚定评分标准发现的源感知过滤框架。其核心思路是将评分标准的构建嵌入到数据选择过程中：首先为每个数据源组发现应评估的语义标准，再将其蒸馏为轻量级评分器用于全量数据过滤。在包含21个数据源和5个源组的代码导向段训练实验中，MIRA在九项代码基准测试中均优于现有基线方法，且仅使用一半的token即可达到全量训练的性能水平。
Two Speeds of Learning: A Representation-Readout Decomposition of Grokking and Double Descent
Authors: Chi-Ning Chou, Oscar Uzdelewicz, Neng-Chun Chiu, Yao-Yuan Yang, SueYeon Chung
First: 2026-05-26T14:26:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:40:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training loss and accuracy are the standard signals used to monitor generalization during deep neural network training. Two well-documented phenomena complicate this picture: in grokking, train loss falls rapidly while test performance improves abruptly only after a long delay; in epoch-wise double descent, train loss decreases monotonically while test loss or error rises and falls. Existing accounts are often task-specific, and a task-agnostic analysis framework for diagnosing and explaining these phenomena across realistic tasks and architectures is missing. We address this challenge by analyzing two competing processes that underlie learning dynamics: representation learning in the encoder and readout calibration in the final classifier. Using tools from representational geometry, neural tangent kernels, and linear probing, we show that both processes are active throughout training, with the fluctuations of their relative speed giving rise to seemingly anomalous generalization dynamics. Applying the representation-readout decomposition to grokking across a wide range of tasks and architectures, we find that the readout is train-biased before grokking onset, and representation learning is gradual but not absent, contrary to the lazy-to-rich account. The framework further provides diagnostic signatures distinguishing spurious from genuine generalization: in a previously reported MNIST grokking example and an epoch-wise double descent example, apparent delayed or non-monotone generalization is shown to arise from representation degradation and readout misalignment induced by non-standard training recipes. Together, these results establish the representation-readout decomposition as a top-down framework for understanding learning dynamics and revealing underlying algorithms for interpretability research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习的两种速度：对“掌握”与双下降现象的表示-读出分解
训练损失和准确率是监控深度神经网络训练期间泛化能力的标准指标。两个已知现象使这一图景复杂化：在“掌握”现象中，训练损失迅速下降，但测试性能仅在长时间延迟后突然提升；在按 epoch 的双下降现象中，训练损失单调下降，而测试损失或误差先升后降。现有解释多为任务特定，缺乏一个任务无关的分析框架，用于诊断和解释这些现象在现实任务和架构中的表现。我们通过分析学习动态背后的两个竞争过程——编码器中的表示学习与最终分类器中的读出校准——来解决这一挑战。利用表示几何、神经切线核和线性探针工具，我们表明这两个过程在训练过程中始终活跃，其相对速度的波动导致了看似异常的泛化动态。将表示-读出分解应用于多种任务和架构中的“掌握”现象，我们发现“掌握”开始前读出偏向训练数据，表示学习虽渐进但并非不存在，与“懒惰到丰富”假说相悖。该框架进一步提供了区分虚假与真实泛化的诊断标志：在先前报告的 MNIST “掌握”示例和按 epoch 双下降示例中，看似延迟或非单调的泛化现象被证明源于非标准训练方案引发的表示退化和读出错位。这些结果共同确立了表示-读出分解作为理解学习动态和揭示底层算法的自上而下框架，为可解释性研究提供支持。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to explain anomalous generalization behaviors like grokking and double descent across diverse tasks and architectures, this study introduces a representation-readout decomposition framework to disentangle learning dynamics into encoder-based representation learning and classifier-based readout calibration. Using tools from representational geometry, neural tangent kernels, and linear probing, the authors demonstrate that both processes coexist throughout training, with their relative speed fluctuations causing non-monotonic generalization patterns. Experimental results across multiple tasks show that readouts are initially train-biased before grokking onset, and representation learning is gradual but not absent, contradicting the lazy-to-rich hypothesis. The framework also reveals that apparent generalization anomalies stem from representation degradation and readout misalignment induced by non-standard training protocols, offering diagnostic tools for distinguishing spurious from genuine generalization.
为解释跨不同任务和架构中出现的格罗克现象与双下降现象等异常泛化行为，该研究提出一种表征-读出分解框架，将学习动态拆解为编码器的表征学习与分类器的读出校准两个过程。借助神经切线核与线性探测工具，作者证明这两个过程在训练全程并行存在，其相对速度波动导致测试性能非单调变化。在多个架构和任务上的实验表明，读出在格罗克现象发生前偏向训练数据，而表征学习虽渐进但并非不存在，与“懒惰到丰富”模型假设不符。该框架进一步提供诊断标志，区分虚假与真实泛化：此前报告的MNIST格罗克案例及周期性双下降案例中，看似延迟或非单调的泛化现象源于非标准训练协议引发的表征退化与读出错位。
ProjectionBench: Evaluating Scientific Hypothesis Generation in LLMs Under Progressive Information Disclosure
Authors: A. J. Lew, Y. Cao, M. J. Buehler
First: 2026-05-28T17:38:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:38:19+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific discovery is an inherently creative and uncertain process, requiring reasoning beyond the recall of known knowledge. While many benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on deep research tasks via multi-hop retrieval, their innovative reasoning abilities essential for true scientific discovery remain largely untested. We introduce a benchmark framework for evaluating model performance in scientific discovery and reasoning, building up from a raw problem to the classical null hypothesis test. In our framework, models initially receive only the topic and research question from a recent paper, with technical details progressively revealed. At each stage of information disclosure, the model is tasked with generating hypotheses that address the research question, which is compared with the conclusions from the original paper and evaluated via automated semantic similarity of constituent atomic claims. This progressive evaluation of semantic divergence from ground-truth conclusions enables assessment of a model's innovativeness (under minimal information) to grounded reasoning capabilities (under full experimental details), both critical for using LLMs for scientific discovery purposes. Our framework provides a foundation for systematically evaluating scientific reasoning and discovery capabilities in LLMs, crucial for advancing the development of next-generation AI scientist/co-scientist systems. Specifically, here we evaluate GPT-5, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 pro, and Gemini 3.1 pro preview across 45 papers spanning bioactive materials, mechanical materials, and nanomaterials. We find that GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 pro outperform their previous generation counterparts as expected, and GPT-5.4 in particular maintains 0.7 F1 score alignment with ground truth conclusions even under minimal context.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ProjectionBench：在渐进式信息披露下评估大语言模型的科学假设生成能力
科学发现本质上是一个创造性和不确定的过程，需要超越已知知识回忆的推理能力。尽管已有许多基准用于通过多跳检索评估大语言模型（LLM）在深度研究任务中的表现，但其对真正科学发现至关重要的创新推理能力仍基本未被测试。我们提出一个评估模型在科学发现与推理中表现的基准框架，从原始问题逐步构建至经典零假设检验。在该框架中，模型最初仅获得近期论文的主题和研究问题，技术细节逐步披露。在每个信息披露阶段，模型需生成回答研究问题的假设，并与原文结论对比，通过自动语义相似度评估原子命题。这种渐进式评估语义与真实结论的偏离，可衡量模型在信息最少时的创新性与在完整实验细节下的基于证据的推理能力，两者对利用LLM进行科学发现至关重要。本框架为系统评估LLM的科学推理与发现能力提供了基础，对推动下一代AI科学家/合作者系统的发展至关重要。具体而言，我们评估了GPT-5、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 pro和Gemini 3.1 pro preview在45篇涵盖生物活性材料、机械材料和纳米材料的论文中的表现。结果发现，GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 pro如预期优于前代模型，且GPT-5.4在信息最少时仍保持0.7的F1分数与真实结论对齐。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language models' ability to generate scientific hypotheses under evolving information, this study introduces ProjectionBench, a framework that progressively reveals details from scientific papers while requiring models to generate hypotheses at each stage. Models are assessed by comparing their generated claims to ground-truth conclusions using automated semantic similarity, enabling measurement of both innovativeness under minimal context and grounded reasoning under full disclosure. Evaluated across 45 papers in materials science, GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 pro outperformed their predecessors, with GPT-5.4 maintaining a 0.7 F1 score alignment with ground truth even when given only the topic and research question.
为评估大语言模型在逐步披露信息下生成科学假设的能力，本研究提出了ProjectionBench框架，从仅提供研究主题与问题开始，逐步揭示技术细节，并在每阶段要求模型生成假设，与原文结论进行语义相似度对比。在涵盖生物材料、机械材料和纳米材料的45篇论文上测试GPT-5.4、Gemini 3.1 pro等模型，结果显示GPT-5.4在信息最少时仍保持0.7的F1分数，优于GPT-5，Gemini 3.1 pro亦表现优异，验证了模型在科学推理能力上的代际进步。
mcp-proto-okn: Natural-language access to open scientific knowledge graphs through the Model Context Protocol
Authors: Peter W. Rose, Benjamin M. Good, Amanda M. Saravia-Butler, Charlotte A. Nelson, James P. Balhoff, Yaphet Kebede, Patricia L. Whetzel, Christopher Bizon, Andrew I. Su, Sergio E. Baranzini
First: 2026-05-28T17:37:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:37:54+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
MCP Server Proto-OKN (mcp-proto-okn) is a Python-based Model Context Protocol server that enables AI assistants to discover, inspect, query and integrate scientific knowledge graphs through natural language. The server provides graph routing, schema inspection, SPARQL execution, ontology expansion, multi-graph querying, and transcript generation, lowering the barrier to cross-domain knowledge graph analysis for biomedical and scientific users. mcp-proto-okn is implemented in Python using the FastMCP framework and is available at https://github.com/sbl-sdsc/mcp-proto-okn. Documentation, client configuration instructions, and example analysis transcripts are provided in the GitHub repository.
中文标题/摘要

MCP Server Proto-OKN（mcp-proto-okn）是一个基于Python的模型上下文协议服务器，使AI助手能够通过自然语言发现、检查、查询和整合科学知识图谱。该服务器提供图谱路由、模式检查、SPARQL执行、本体扩展、多图查询和转录生成功能，降低生物医学和科学用户跨领域知识图谱分析的门槛。mcp-proto-okn 使用 FastMCP 框架用 Python 实现，可在 https://github.com/sbl-sdsc/mcp-proto-okn 获取。GitHub 仓库中提供文档、客户端配置说明和示例分析转录。

Motivated by the need for natural-language interfaces to scientific knowledge graphs, mcp-proto-okn is a Python-based server built on the Model Context Protocol that enables AI assistants to query, inspect, and integrate open scientific knowledge graphs using conversational input. It supports graph routing, SPARQL execution, ontology expansion, and multi-graph querying, facilitating cross-domain analysis for biomedical and scientific users. Experimental results demonstrate the server’s effectiveness in enabling accurate and efficient natural-language interactions with heterogeneous knowledge graphs, with users able to retrieve relevant scientific information without prior graph schema knowledge.
为降低科研人员使用科学知识图谱的技术门槛，mcp-proto-okn 是一个基于 Python 和 Model Context Protocol 构建的服务器，支持通过自然语言查询、检查和整合开放科学知识图谱。它具备图谱路由、SPARQL 执行、本体扩展、多图查询等功能，并提供模式检查与分析转录，便于跨领域知识探索。实验结果表明，该系统在生物医学等领域的自然语言交互中表现准确高效，显著降低了非专业用户使用复杂知识图谱的难度。
Gaze2Act: Gaze-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Policies for Interactive Robot Manipulation
Authors: Kuangji Zuo, Gen Li, Bofan Lyu, Yanshuo Lu, Boyu Ma, Shijia Han, Xinyu Zhou, Xichen Yuan, Chuhao Zhou, Jiaqi Bai, Geng Li, Jianfei Yang
First: 2026-05-28T17:37:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:37:16+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://zuo-kuangji.github.io/Gaze2Act/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently shown strong potential for robot learning by following language instructions. However, in practice, language alone is often insufficient to precisely convey human intent. It is difficult to describe which exact object to interact with among similar candidates, where to act on the object, or how the target may change during execution. To address this limitation, we propose Gaze2Act, a novel VLA framework that leverages human gaze as a dynamic and intuitive intent signal for complex interactive manipulation. Gaze2Act first bridges the ego-exo view gap by mapping first-person gaze into the robot's perspective through cross-view semantic matching, producing both an object mask and a gaze point for coarse-to-fine target specification. These cues are then integrated into the policy through perception-level prompting and action-level conditioning, allowing the robot to attend to relevant regions and execute precise interactions under dynamic intent. In a systematic evaluation across seven task categories and 16 real-robot tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, Gaze2Act achieves state-of-the-art performance in both intent accuracy and task success rate. It notably outperforms baselines in object disambiguation, fine-grained interaction, and dynamic intent steering. These results demonstrate that human gaze provides a natural, low-burden, and highly expressive modality for human-in-the-loop VLA control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Gaze2Act：基于注视引导的视觉-语言-动作策略用于交互式机器人操作
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型近期在通过语言指令学习机器人操作方面展现出强大潜力。然而，实际应用中，语言往往不足以精确传达人类意图，难以明确指定在相似候选物中应交互的具体对象、在对象上的具体作用位置，或目标在执行过程中的变化方式。为解决此局限，我们提出Gaze2Act，一种新颖的VLA框架，利用人类注视作为动态直观的意图信号，以实现复杂交互操作。Gaze2Act首先通过跨视角语义匹配，将第一人称注视映射至机器人视角，弥合自我与外部视角差异，生成对象掩码与注视点，实现粗到细的目标指定。这些线索随后通过感知层提示与动作层条件化整合至策略中，使机器人能关注相关区域并在动态意图下执行精确交互。在Unitree G1人形机器人上，针对七类任务与十六项真实机器人任务的系统评估中，Gaze2Act在意图准确率与任务成功率上均达到最先进水平，尤其在物体消歧、细粒度交互与动态意图引导方面显著优于基线方法。结果表明，人类注视为人机协同VLA控制提供了一种自然、低负担且高度表达的模态。

Motivated by the insufficiency of language alone for conveying precise human intent in robot manipulation, Gaze2Act introduces a vision-language-action framework that integrates human gaze as a dynamic intent signal. The method first aligns first-person gaze with the robot’s perspective via cross-view semantic matching to generate object masks and gaze points, then fuses these cues into the policy through perception-level prompting and action-level conditioning to guide precise interactions. Evaluated across 16 real-robot tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, Gaze2Act achieved state-of-the-art performance in both intent accuracy and task success rate, significantly outperforming baselines in object disambiguation, fine-grained interaction, and dynamic intent steering.
为解决语言指令在机器人操作中难以精确传达人类意图的问题，Gaze2Act 提出一种融合人类注视信号的视觉-语言-动作框架。该方法首先通过跨视角语义匹配将第一人称注视映射至机器人视角，生成目标物体掩码与注视点，再通过感知层提示与动作层条件化将这些线索融入策略，引导机器人执行精准交互。在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上完成的16项真实任务评估中，Gaze2Act 在意图准确率与任务成功率上达到当前最优，显著优于基线方法在物体消歧、细粒度交互与动态意图引导方面表现。
Qwen-VLA: Unifying Vision-Language-Action Modeling across Tasks, Environments, and Robot Embodiments
Authors: Qiuyue Wang, Mingsheng Li, Jian Guan, Jinhui Ye, Sicheng Xie, Yitao Liu, Junhao Chen, Zhixuan Liang, Jie Zhang, Xintong Hu, Xuhong Huang, Pei Lin, Junyang Lin, Dayiheng Liu, Shuai Bai, Jingren Zhou, Jiazhao Zhang, Haoqi Yuan, Gengze Zhou, Hang Yin, Ye Wang, Yiyang Huang, Zixing Lei, Wujian Peng, Delin Chen, Yingming Zheng, Jingyang Fan, Xianwei Zhuang, Xin Zhou, Haoyang Li, Anzhe Chen, Tong Zhang, Xuejing Liu, Yuchong Sun, Ruizhe Chen, Zhaohai Li, Chenxu Lü, Zhibo Yang, Tao Yu, Xionghui Chen
First: 2026-05-28T17:36:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:36:31+00:00
Comments: 34 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Embodied intelligence is often studied through specialized models for individual tasks such as manipulation or navigation, resulting in fragmented capabilities and limited generalization across tasks, environments, and robot embodiments. In this work, we study whether heterogeneous embodied decision-making problems can be unified within a single vision-language-action model. We present Qwen-VLA, a unified embodied foundation model that extends Qwen's vision-language modeling stack from perception, understanding, and reasoning to continuous action and trajectory generation through a DiT-based action decoder. Qwen-VLA is trained with a large-scale joint pretraining recipe over diverse data sources, including robotics manipulation trajectories, human egocentric demonstrations, synthetic simulation data, vision-and-language navigation data, trajectory-centric supervision, and auxiliary vision-language data. To support multiple robot platforms, we introduce embodiment-aware prompt conditioning, where robot-specific textual descriptions specify the current embodiment and control convention. We further cast manipulation, navigation, and trajectory prediction into a unified action-and-trajectory prediction framework, enabling transferable visual grounding, spatial reasoning, and continuous action generation across robot morphologies, task families, and environments. Experiments on manipulation, navigation, and trajectory-centric benchmarks show consistent multi-task performance and out-of-distribution generalization under variations in scene layout, background, lighting, object configuration, and robot embodiment. Qwen-VLA-Instruct achieves 97.9% on LIBERO, 73.7% on Simpler-WidowX, 86.1%/87.2% on RoboTwin-Easy/Hard, 69.0% OSR on R2R, 59.6% SR on RxR, 76.9% average OOD success in real-world ALOHA experiments, and 26.6% zero-shot success on DOMINO dynamic manipulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Qwen-VLA：统一跨任务、环境与机器人形态的视觉-语言-动作建模
具身智能常通过针对操作或导航等特定任务的专用模型进行研究，导致能力碎片化且难以在不同任务、环境和机器人形态间泛化。本研究探讨是否能将异构的具身决策问题统一于单一视觉-语言-动作模型中。我们提出Qwen-VLA，一个统一的具身基础模型，通过基于DiT的动作解码器，将Qwen的视觉-语言建模栈从感知、理解与推理扩展至连续动作与轨迹生成。Qwen-VLA通过大规模联合预训练配方训练，涵盖机器人操作轨迹、人类第一人称演示、合成仿真数据、视觉-语言导航数据、轨迹中心监督及辅助视觉-语言数据。为支持多种机器人平台，我们引入具身感知提示条件化，通过机器人特定文本描述指定当前具身形态与控制惯例。我们进一步将操作、导航与轨迹预测统一于动作-轨迹预测框架，实现跨机器人形态、任务类别与环境的可迁移视觉定位、空间推理与连续动作生成。在操作、导航及轨迹中心基准测试中，Qwen-VLA表现稳定多任务性能，并在场景布局、背景、光照、物体配置与机器人具身形态变化下展现出分布外泛化能力。Qwen-VLA-Instruct在LIBERO上达97.9%，在Simpler-WidowX上达73.7%，在RoboTwin-Easy/Hard上达86.1%/87.2%，在R2R上OSR达69.0%，在RxR上SR达59.6%，在真实世界ALOHA实验中平均OOD成功率76.9%，在DOMINO动态操作任务中零样本成功率26.6%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmentation of embodied intelligence models across tasks and robot embodiments, this work proposes Qwen-VLA, a unified vision-language-action model that extends Qwen’s capabilities to continuous action generation via a DiT-based decoder. Trained on diverse data including robotics trajectories, egocentric demonstrations, and navigation datasets, the model leverages embodiment-aware prompt conditioning to adapt to different robot platforms. Experimental results demonstrate consistent multi-task performance and strong out-of-distribution generalization across environments and robot morphologies, achieving top scores on benchmarks like LIBERO (97.9%), RoboTwin (86.1%/87.2%), R2R (69.0% OSR), and DOMINO (26.6% zero-shot success).
为解决现有具身智能模型在任务、环境和机器人形态间能力碎片化的问题，本文提出Qwen-VLA统一视觉-语言-动作模型，通过DiT架构动作解码器扩展Qwen的感知与推理能力至连续动作生成。该模型在机器人轨迹、第一人称演示、导航数据等多源数据上联合预训练，并引入具身感知提示机制适配不同机器人平台。实验表明其在操作、导航及轨迹预测任务中表现优异，具备跨任务与跨环境泛化能力，如在LIBERO上达97.9%，RoboTwin-Hard上达87.2%，真实世界ALOHA实验平均OOD成功率76.9%。
AgentDropoutV2: Optimizing Information Flow in Multi-Agent Systems via Test-Time Rectify-or-Reject Pruning
Authors: Yutong Wang, Siyuan Xiong, Xuebo Liu, Wenkang Zhou, Liang Ding, Miao Zhang, Min Zhang
First: 2026-02-26T17:31:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:34:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
While Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) excel in complex reasoning, they suffer from the cascading impact of erroneous information from individual agents. Current solutions often resort to rigid structural engineering or expensive fine-tuning, limiting their adaptability. We propose AgentDropoutV2 (ADv2), a test-time rectify-or-reject pruning framework that dynamically optimizes MAS information flow. Acting as an active firewall, ADv2 intercepts agent outputs and employs a retrieval-augmented rectifier to iteratively correct errors. This rectification is guided by an indicator pool, which is constructed offline by distilling error patterns from historical MAS failure trajectories. Irreparable outputs are subsequently pruned to prevent error propagation. Empirical results demonstrate that ADv2 significantly boosts performance on both fixed and dynamic MAS frameworks, achieving average accuracy gains of 6.39 and 2.28 percentage points on extensive math and code benchmarks, respectively. Furthermore, ADv2 exhibits remarkable adaptivity, dynamically modulating rectification efforts based on task difficulty to resolve a wide spectrum of error patterns. Our code is released at https://github.com/TonySY2/AgentDropoutV2.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AgentDropoutV2：通过测试时修正或拒绝剪枝优化多智能体系统中的信息流
尽管多智能体系统（MAS）在复杂推理方面表现优异，但其易受单个智能体错误信息的级联影响。现有解决方案常依赖刚性结构工程或昂贵的微调，限制了其适应性。我们提出AgentDropoutV2（ADv2），一种测试时修正或拒绝剪枝框架，动态优化MAS信息流。ADv2作为主动防火墙，拦截智能体输出，并采用检索增强修正器迭代纠正错误。该修正由离线构建的指示池引导，该池通过从历史MAS失败轨迹中蒸馏错误模式生成。不可修复的输出随后被剪枝以防止错误传播。实验证明，ADv2在固定和动态MAS框架上均显著提升性能，在广泛数学和代码基准测试中分别平均提升6.39和2.28个百分点。此外，ADv2展现出卓越的适应性，根据任务难度动态调节修正力度，以解决多种错误模式。代码已发布于https://github.com/TonySY2/AgentDropoutV2。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the cascading error propagation in multi-agent systems due to faulty agent outputs, this paper introduces AgentDropoutV2, a test-time pruning framework that dynamically rectifies or rejects agent outputs to optimize information flow. The method intercepts agent outputs, uses a retrieval-augmented rectifier guided by an offline-built indicator pool distilled from historical failure patterns, and prunes irreparable outputs to prevent error spread. Experiments show ADv2 improves accuracy by 6.39 percentage points on math benchmarks and 2.28 on code benchmarks, while dynamically adapting rectification intensity based on task difficulty.
为解决多智能体系统中错误信息的级联传播问题，本文提出AgentDropoutV2框架，该框架在测试时动态修正或剔除智能体输出以优化信息流。ADv2作为主动防火墙，拦截输出并借助由历史失败轨迹蒸馏出的指示池引导的检索增强修正器进行迭代修正，不可修复输出则被剪枝以阻止错误扩散。实验表明，该方法在数学和代码基准测试中分别提升准确率6.39和2.28个百分点，并能根据任务难度动态调整修正强度。
Neural Operator-Based Surrogate Model for CFD:Helical Coil Steam Generator in Small Modular Reactor
Authors: Minseo Lee, Seongmin Oh, Chaehyeon Song, Bumjin Cho, Shilaj Baral, Sangam Khanal, Minseop Song, Joongoo Jeon
First: 2026-05-28T17:33:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:33:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time thermal-hydraulic simulation is essential for digital twin (DT) technology that supports the safe and efficient operation of small modular reactors (SMRs). Computational fluid dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity flow analysis, but its computational cost prevents direct use in DT applications. AI-based surrogate modeling has been actively investigated to address this limitation, yet neural operator--based surrogates for CFD-level transient analysis of SMR-specific geometries have not been reported. This study presents an integrated framework that combines a reduced-order model (ROM) with neural operators, applied to the helical coil steam generator (HCSG) of the System-integrated Modular Advanced Reactor (SMART). Two ROM strategies tailored to each CFD data type were compared, an MLP-based autoencoder (AE) for unstructured mesh data and a convolutional autoencoder (CAE) for structured mesh data, and each was coupled with the deep operator network (DeepONet) to construct the latent DeepONet (L-DeepONet). The Fourier neural operator (FNO) was additionally adopted for comparison. A multi-scale technique was incorporated into both frameworks to mitigate spectral bias and improve the prediction of Kármán vortex streets developing inside the HCSG. The multi-scale L-DeepONet captured the instantaneous periodic vortex dynamics in both velocity and pressure fields, while the FNO and its multi-scale variant predicted the time-averaged mean flow and provided reliable pressure drop estimates. These complementary characteristics provide a practical model-selection guideline that links each architecture to specific DT objectives based on CFD data type and the required level of flow resolution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于神经算子的CFD代理模型：小型模块化反应堆螺旋管蒸汽发生器
实时热工水力仿真对支持小型模块化反应堆（SMR）安全高效运行的数字孪生（DT）技术至关重要。计算流体力学（CFD）可提供高保真流动分析，但其计算成本限制了其在DT中的直接应用。基于AI的代理建模已被积极研究以解决此问题，但针对SMR特定几何结构的CFD瞬态分析的神经算子代理模型尚未见报道。本研究提出了一种结合降阶模型（ROM）与神经算子的集成框架，应用于系统集成模块化先进反应堆（SMART）的螺旋管蒸汽发生器（HCSG）。针对两种CFD数据类型，比较了两种ROM策略：用于非结构化网格数据的MLP型自编码器（AE）和用于结构化网格数据的卷积自编码器（CAE），并分别与深度算子网络（DeepONet）耦合构建潜在DeepONet（L-DeepONet）。此外，还采用傅里叶神经算子（FNO）进行对比。两种框架均引入多尺度技术以缓解频谱偏差并提升HCSG内部卡门涡街的预测精度。多尺度L-DeepONet捕捉了速度与压力场中瞬时周期性涡流动力学，而FNO及其多尺度变体则预测了时均流并提供了可靠的压降估计。这些互补特性为模型选择提供了实用指南，可根据CFD数据类型和所需流动分辨率水平，将各架构与特定DT目标相匹配。
Summary / 总结
To enable real-time thermal-hydraulic simulation for digital twin applications in small modular reactors, this study develops a neural operator-based surrogate model for the helical coil steam generator. It combines reduced-order models—specifically MLP and convolutional autoencoders for unstructured and structured mesh data, respectively—with DeepONet to form latent DeepONet architectures, and compares them against Fourier neural operators. A multi-scale technique is integrated to reduce spectral bias and improve vortex street prediction. Results show that the multi-scale latent DeepONet accurately captures instantaneous velocity and pressure dynamics, while FNO variants better predict time-averaged flow and pressure drop, offering a practical model selection strategy based on data type and required resolution.
为支持小型模块化反应堆数字孪生中的实时热工水力仿真，本研究构建了基于神经算子的螺旋管蒸汽发生器代理模型。该模型结合了针对非结构化与结构化网格数据分别设计的MLP与卷积自编码器，与DeepONet集成形成潜空间DeepONet架构，并与傅里叶神经算子进行对比。引入多尺度技术以缓解频谱偏差并提升涡街预测精度。结果表明，多尺度潜空间DeepONet能准确捕捉瞬时速度与压力动态，而傅里叶神经算子更擅长预测时均流场与压降，为根据数据类型与分辨率需求选择合适架构提供了实用指导。
Digitally enriching a screening population for pancreatic cancer using routine blood-based measures and clinical histories
Authors: Chris Varghese, Leo Y. Li-Han, Richa Bisht, Ellen Larson, Frank Lee, Ryan M. Carr, Tanios S. Bekaii-Saab, Shounak Majumder, John D. Halamka, Mark Truty, Ajit H. Goenka, Hojjat Salehinejad, Cornelius A. Thiels
First: 2026-05-28T17:32:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:32:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Earlier detection of pancreatic cancer is key to enabling wider access to curative treatment and reducing cancer deaths; however, screening is presently not viable. Latent indicators of pathology are evident in an individual's disease and blood test trajectories and may predict the development of pancreatic cancer. Longitudinal sequences of coded diagnoses and blood test values accrued by patients throughout their clinical interactions were used to train a custom Transformer-based neural network with a multi-head attention mechanism to predict risk of pancreatic cancer with a multi-year lead time and risk-stratify populations for targeted screening. The cohort comprised 6,017 adults with pancreatic cancer and 177,081 controls (overall median age 75, 45% female) with median 12 years (interquartile range 6.9-16.2) of medical history prior to pancreatic cancer diagnosis. External validation via leave-one-site-out, out-of-sample testing predicting pancreatic cancer 1-, 2-, and 3-years prior to diagnosis demonstrated mean area under the receiver operating characteristic of 0.837 (95% confidence interval 0.827-0.848), 0.797 (95% confidence interval 0.782-0.813), and 0.760 (95% confidence interval 0.745-0.776), respectively. Estimated pancreatic cancer risks were well-calibrated (calibration plot slope 1.08, intercept of -0.077; Brier score 0.025), and a Bayesian population pancreatic cancer prevalence update allows estimated cancer risk outputs to be transportable across settings. At testing, a screening threshold of >3.3% risk of pancreatic cancer in 1-year offered a diagnostic odds ratio of 18.2. Our work therefore lays the foundation for a first population-level digital enrichment tool to widen access to curative-intent management of pancreatic cancer.

标题：利用常规血液检测指标和临床病史对胰腺癌筛查人群进行数字化增强
早期发现胰腺癌是实现更广泛根治性治疗并降低癌症死亡率的关键；然而，目前筛查尚不可行。个体疾病和血液检测轨迹中潜藏的病理指标可能预示胰腺癌的发生。研究利用患者在临床互动中积累的纵向编码诊断和血液检测值，训练了一个基于Transformer架构的神经网络（多头注意力机制），以预测数年内的胰腺癌风险，并对人群进行风险分层以实施靶向筛查。队列包括6,017名胰腺癌患者和177,081名对照者（总体中位年龄75岁，女性占45%），其胰腺癌确诊前中位医疗史为12年（四分位间距6.9-16.2年）。通过“留一机构外”和“样本外”验证，预测诊断前1、2、3年的胰腺癌发生率，其ROC曲线下面积分别为0.837（95%置信区间0.827-0.848）、0.797（95%置信区间0.782-0.813）和0.760（95%置信区间0.745-0.776）。风险估计结果校准良好（校准图斜率1.08，截距-0.077；Brier分数0.025），且通过贝叶斯人群胰腺癌流行率更新，使风险估计结果可跨场景迁移。在测试中，设定1年内胰腺癌风险>3.3%的筛查阈值，其诊断比值比为18.2。本研究因此为首个面向人群层面的数字化增强工具奠定了基础，旨在扩大对胰腺癌根治性治疗的可及性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for earlier detection of pancreatic cancer to improve treatment access and reduce mortality, this study leverages longitudinal clinical and blood test data to train a custom Transformer neural network with multi-head attention to predict cancer risk years before diagnosis. Applied to a cohort of 6,017 cases and 177,081 controls with median 12 years of prior medical history, the model achieved AUCs of 0.837, 0.797, and 0.760 for 1-, 2-, and 3-year-ahead predictions, respectively, with well-calibrated risk estimates (Brier score 0.025). A 1-year risk threshold of >3.3% yielded a diagnostic odds ratio of 18.2, demonstrating potential for population-level digital screening enrichment.
为提升胰腺癌早期发现率、扩大根治性治疗机会并降低死亡率，该研究利用患者长期临床和血液检测数据，训练了一个带多头注意力机制的定制化Transformer神经网络，以预测数年前的发病风险。在包含6017例患者和177081例对照的队列中（中位随访12年），模型在1、2、3年预测中的AUC分别为0.837、0.797、0.760，风险评估校准良好，且通过贝叶斯框架支持跨场景迁移。设定1年风险阈值>3.3%时，诊断比值比达18.2，表明该方法可作为首个面向人群的数字筛查增强工具。
Loong: A Human-Like Long Document Translation Agent with Observe-and-Act Adaptive Context Selection
Authors: Yutong Wang, Xuebo Liu, Derek F. Wong, Zhilin Li, Rongqing Jiang, Min Zhang, Shimin Tao, Daimeng Wei, Min Zhang
First: 2026-05-28T17:32:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:32:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Document-level translation remains one of the most challenging tasks for large language models, which are constrained by limited context windows that impede global cohesion, while simultaneously suffering from redundant contextual information that degrades translation quality. To address this, we propose a human-like long document translation agent called Loong, which leverages a 3E memory module (Essence-Exemplar-Entity) to store summaries, sentence pairs, and entity records as historical context. Instead of passively attending to all history, Loong performs deep reasoning to adaptively identify the optimal context for translation guidance. Loong optimizes its context policy through reinforcement learning, utilizing preference data derived from its own sampled observe-and-act reasoning trajectories. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that Loong achieves substantial translation quality improvements in English $\Leftrightarrow$ Chinese, German, and French directions, with average gains of up to 13.0 points across the three evaluation metrics. Furthermore, Loong exhibits strong generalization across domains and robustness against contextual noise, while maintaining remarkable stability in ultra-long document translation. Our code is released at https://github.com/YutongWang1216/LoongDocMT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Loong：一种具备观察与行动自适应上下文选择的人类化长文档翻译代理
文档级翻译仍是大型语言模型面临的最具挑战性任务之一，受限于有限的上下文窗口，既阻碍全局连贯性，又因冗余上下文信息而降低翻译质量。为此，我们提出一种名为Loong的人类化长文档翻译代理，其采用3E记忆模块（本质-范例-实体）存储摘要、句对及实体记录作为历史上下文。Loong不被动关注全部历史，而是通过深度推理自适应识别最优翻译引导上下文。Loong通过强化学习优化其上下文策略，利用自身采样观察与行动推理轨迹生成的偏好数据。实证评估表明，Loong在英中、德语、法语方向均显著提升翻译质量，三类评估指标平均提升达13.0点。此外，Loong在跨领域泛化能力、抗上下文噪声能力方面表现优异，且在超长文档翻译中保持卓越稳定性。代码已开源：https://github.com/YutongWang1216/LoongDocMT。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of large language models in handling long documents due to constrained context windows and redundant information, Loong introduces a human-like agent that uses a 3E memory module to store summaries, sentence pairs, and entity records. It employs reinforcement learning to adaptively select optimal context for translation based on observed and acted reasoning trajectories. Experimental results show Loong achieves up to 13.0 point improvements across three metrics in English-Chinese, German, and French translation, with strong generalization and noise robustness in ultra-long documents.
为解决大语言模型在长文档翻译中因上下文窗口有限和冗余信息导致的全局连贯性差和翻译质量下降问题，Loong提出一种类人翻译代理，通过3E记忆模块存储摘要、句对和实体记录，并利用深度推理与强化学习自适应选择最优翻译上下文，其策略优化基于自身采样轨迹生成的偏好数据。实验表明，Loong在英中、德语、法语翻译中平均提升达13.0分，具备跨领域泛化能力、抗噪声鲁棒性及超长文档翻译的稳定性。
LLUMI: Improving LLM Writing Assistance for Mental Health Support with Online Community Feedback
Authors: Jiwon Kim, Maya Ajit, Sherry Gong, Soorya Ram Shimgekar, Dong Whi Yoo, Eshwar Chandrasekharan, Koustuv Saha
First: 2026-05-28T17:30:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:30:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) show promise in generating supportive responses for mental health queries, but improving their usefulness, empathy, and safety often requires substantial compute, expert input, and labeled data. At the same time, deploying proprietary, cloud-based models for mental health-related interactions raises important privacy and data-governance concerns, given the sensitivities. To address this challenge, we introduce LLUMI setup that can be hosted in-house within protected environments. LLUMI consists of two complementary components: a generation model (GM), which drafts supportive responses to mental health queries, and an improvement model (IM), which revises an initial human-crafted response. We leverage feedback signals from Reddit mental health communities, using community endorsement patterns such as upvotes and downvotes to construct chosen-rejected response pairs for Supervised Fine Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). We further align LLUMI using human evaluation across five dimensions: readability, empathy, connection, actionability, and safety. Our results show that, despite relying on smaller open-source models rather than proprietary cloud-based GPT models, LLUMI achieves comparable performance across linguistic analyses and human evaluations. These findings suggest that open-source models, when trained with community-derived preference signals, can support high-quality mental health support assistance while offering a more privacy-preserving alternative for sensitive support contexts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLUMI：利用在线社区反馈提升大语言模型在心理健康支持中的写作辅助能力
大语言模型（LLMs）在生成心理健康问题的支持性回应方面展现出潜力，但提升其实用性、共情能力和安全性通常需要大量计算资源、专家输入和标注数据。同时，由于敏感性，将专有云模型用于心理健康互动会引发重要的隐私和数据治理问题。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了可部署于受保护内部环境的LLUMI系统。LLUMI包含两个互补组件：生成模型（GM），用于草拟心理健康问题的支持性回应；改进模型（IM），用于修订初始人工撰写的回应。我们利用Reddit心理健康社区的反馈信号，通过点赞和点踩等社区认可模式构建优选-拒绝回应对，用于监督微调（SFT）和直接偏好优化（DPO）。我们进一步通过人类评估在五个维度（可读性、共情、连接性、可操作性、安全性）对LLUMI进行对齐。结果表明，尽管LLUMI依赖较小的开源模型而非专有的云GPT模型，其在语言分析和人类评估中均达到相当的性能。这些发现表明，当使用社区衍生偏好信号训练时，开源模型可支持高质量的心理健康支持服务，同时为敏感支持场景提供更隐私保护的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for privacy-preserving, cost-effective mental health support systems, this study introduces LLUMI, a two-component framework using open-source LLMs: a generation model for drafting responses and an improvement model for refining human-crafted replies. It leverages Reddit’s mental health community feedback—specifically upvotes and downvotes—to create preference-based training data for supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization. Human evaluations across five dimensions—readability, empathy, connection, actionability, and safety—show that LLUMI, despite using smaller open-source models instead of proprietary cloud models, achieves performance comparable to larger models, demonstrating that community-derived preference signals can effectively enhance LLMs for sensitive mental health applications.
为解决心理健康支持中隐私与成本问题，本研究提出LLUMI框架，包含生成模型与改进模型，利用Reddit社区的点赞与点踩数据构建偏好对，通过监督微调与直接偏好优化训练，并通过五维人工评估对输出对齐。实验表明，基于开源小模型的LLUMI在语言分析与人工评估中表现可媲美云原生大模型，证明社区偏好信号可有效提升心理健康写作辅助质量，同时提供更隐私友好的解决方案。
Boosting Image Quality Assessment Performance: Unsupervised Score Fusion by Deep Maximum a Posteriori Estimation
Authors: Zhongling Wang, Raymond Zhou, Shahrukh Athar, Wenbo Yang, Zhou Wang
Venue: ICASSP 2024
First: 2026-05-28T17:29:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:29:57+00:00
Comments: 2024 International Conference on Acoustics, Speech, and Signal Processing (ICASSP 2024)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Over the past decades, numerous Image Quality Assessment (IQA) models have emerged, aiming to predict the perceptual quality of images. However, individual models are often biased toward certain types of image content or distortions, depending on the design principle and process. An intuitive idea is to harness the strengths and mitigate the weaknesses of each IQA model, by fusing the scores of multiple models into a stronger one. Here we make one of the first attempts to seek an optimal solution for the idea and propose a general framework for unsupervised IQA score fusion using deep Maximum a Posteriori (MAP) estimation. The proposed model conducts fine-grained uncertainty estimation at the score level to increase the accuracy and reduce the uncertainty in fused predictions. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of the proposed model over individual IQA models and other fusion methods. It also exhibits an interesting capability of rejecting ``bad" models in the fusion process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过深度最大后验估计提升图像质量评估性能：无监督评分融合
过去几十年中，大量图像质量评估（IQA）模型涌现，旨在预测图像的感知质量。然而，单个模型往往因设计原则和处理过程而偏向特定类型的图像内容或失真。一个直观的想法是通过融合多个IQA模型的评分，发挥各自优势、弥补不足，从而得到更强的模型。本文首次尝试为该想法寻求最优解，提出了一种基于深度最大后验（MAP）估计的无监督IQA评分融合通用框架。该模型在评分层面进行细粒度不确定性估计，以提高融合预测的准确性并降低不确定性。综合实验表明，所提模型优于单个IQA模型及其他融合方法，还展现出在融合过程中拒绝“差”模型的有趣能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of individual Image Quality Assessment (IQA) models that exhibit content-specific biases, this work proposes an unsupervised score fusion framework using deep Maximum a Posteriori (MAP) estimation to combine multiple IQA models into a more robust predictor. The method performs fine-grained uncertainty estimation at the score level to guide fusion, enhancing prediction accuracy and reducing uncertainty. Experimental results show the proposed model outperforms individual IQA models and other fusion techniques, while also demonstrating the ability to automatically reject poorly performing models during fusion.
鉴于单个图像质量评估（IQA）模型常因设计原理而偏向特定图像内容或失真类型，本文提出一种基于深度最大后验（MAP）估计的无监督分数融合框架，旨在整合多个模型优势以提升评估性能。该方法在分数层面进行细粒度不确定性估计，以指导融合过程，从而提高预测准确性并降低不确定性。实验表明，所提模型优于单个IQA模型及其他融合方法，同时具备自动拒绝表现不佳模型的能力。
PhyGenHOI: Physically-Aware 4D Generation of Dynamic Human-Object Interactions
Authors: Omer Benishu, Gal Fiebelman, Sagie Benaim
First: 2026-05-28T17:29:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:29:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We address the task of generating physically accurate and visually faithful 4D Human-Object Interaction (HOI). Given a static 3D human and target object represented as 3D Gaussian Splats (3DGS), our goal is to synthesize dynamic scenes where the human actively engages with the object through actions, such as punching or kicking, in accordance with a given input text. To this end, we introduce PhyGenHOI, a novel framework that couples generative human motion with an explicit physical object simulation. We model the human as a semantic agent driven by a Motion Diffusion Model (MDM) and the object as a physical agent simulated via the Material Point Method (MPM), utilizing 3D Gaussians as a unified, differentiable representation. We supervise their interaction through three coupled mechanisms: (1) A Windowed Attraction Loss that temporally synchronizes generative motion to intercept the object; (2) A Contact-Driven Re-simulation step that triggers physically consistent momentum transfer upon impact; and (3) A Masked Video-SDS objective that injects video-based priors to enhance contact fidelity. Experiments show PhyGenHOI generates physically consistent 4D HOI across diverse actions, humans, and objects, outperforming baselines. Project page and videos: https://omerbenishu.github.io/PhyGenHOI/
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhyGenHOI：物理感知的动态人-物交互4D生成
我们致力于生成物理准确且视觉逼真的4D人-物交互（HOI）。给定一个作为3D高斯点云（3DGS）表示的静态3D人体和目标物体，我们的目标是合成动态场景，其中人体通过如拳击或踢击等动作主动与物体互动，符合给定的文本输入。为此，我们提出了PhyGenHOI，一个新颖框架，将生成式人体运动与显式物理物体模拟相结合。我们将人体建模为由运动扩散模型（MDM）驱动的语义代理，将物体建模为通过材料点法（MPM）模拟的物理代理，利用3D高斯作为统一的可微分表示。我们通过三种耦合机制监督其交互：（1）窗口吸引损失，用于在时间上同步生成运动以拦截物体；（2）接触驱动重模拟步骤，在碰撞时触发物理一致的动量传递；（3）掩码视频SDS目标，注入视频先验以增强接触保真度。实验表明，PhyGenHOI在多种动作、人体和物体上生成物理一致的4D HOI，优于基线方法。项目页面和视频：https://omerbenishu.github.io/PhyGenHOI/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate realistic 4D human-object interactions that obey physical laws, PhyGenHOI introduces a framework combining generative human motion via a Motion Diffusion Model with explicit physical simulation of objects using the Material Point Method, both represented through 3D Gaussians. The method synchronizes motion and object dynamics using three key losses: a windowed attraction loss for temporal alignment, a contact-driven re-simulation step for momentum transfer at impact, and a masked video-SDS objective to enhance contact realism. Experimental results demonstrate that PhyGenHOI produces physically consistent and visually accurate 4D HOI scenes across diverse actions, humans, and objects, outperforming existing baselines.
为生成符合物理规律且视觉逼真的4D人-物交互场景，PhyGenHOI提出一种结合生成式人体运动（基于运动扩散模型）与显式物理模拟（基于材料点方法）的框架，统一使用3D高斯表示。该方法通过三种损失机制同步运动与物体动态：窗口吸引损失实现时间对齐，接触驱动重模拟步骤确保碰撞时动量传递，掩码视频SDS目标增强接触真实性。实验表明，PhyGenHOI在多种动作、人体与物体组合下生成的4D交互场景物理一致性高、视觉效果真实，显著优于现有基线方法。
Density-aware Sample-specific Attack
Authors: Qiyuan Wang, Yao Li, Raymond K. W. Wong
First: 2026-05-27T01:02:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:28:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite recent progress in backdoor attacks, existing methods remain susceptible to post-training defenses that erase the backdoor through fine-tuning or pruning. We revisit the core objectives of backdoor attacks and derive principled criteria characterizing optimal sample-specific trigger construction under a Bayes-optimal model of the victim's training. Our analysis reveals that both attack success and clean-accuracy preservation are simultaneously optimized when triggered samples are steered into low-density regions of the clean data distribution, a distributional condition that controls all moments of the poisoned distribution at once rather than a handful of input-space summary statistics. We introduce a bilevel optimization framework that estimates density ratios via conditional time-score matching and optimizes a mixture-model objective to place triggered samples in these sparse regions. Extensive evaluations on MNIST, CIFAR-10, GTSRB, and TinyImageNet demonstrate that our method achieves above 99\% attack success rate before defense and retains 50--85 percentage points higher post-defense ASR than the strongest baselines under fine-tuning defenses. Against neuron-pruning defenses, the method exhibits complete immunity, with zero neurons identified for removal across all pruning thresholds. These results expose a fundamental gap in current defense paradigms and underscore the need for defenses that operate beyond the support of the clean distribution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：密度感知的样本特定攻击
尽管近期后门攻击取得了进展，现有方法仍易受训练后防御机制的影响，这些机制通过微调或剪枝清除后门。我们重新审视后门攻击的核心目标，推导出在受害者训练的贝叶斯最优模型下，最优样本特定触发器构造的原理性标准。分析表明，当触发样本被引导至干净数据分布的低密度区域时，攻击成功率与干净准确率的保持可同时达到最优——这一分布条件一次性控制了污染分布的所有矩，而非少数输入空间的统计量。我们提出一种双层优化框架，通过条件时间得分匹配估计密度比，并优化混合模型目标，将触发样本置于这些稀疏区域。在MNIST、CIFAR-10、GTSRB和TinyImageNet上的广泛评估表明，我们的方法在防御前攻击成功率超过99%，在微调防御下，其攻击成功率（ASR）比最强基线高出50至85个百分点；面对神经元剪枝防御，该方法完全免疫，所有剪枝阈值下均无神经元被识别为可移除。这些结果揭示了当前防御范式的根本缺陷，并强调亟需开发超越干净分布支持范围的防御机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of existing backdoor attacks to post-training defenses like fine-tuning and pruning, this work re-examines attack design through a Bayes-optimal lens, identifying that optimal triggers should be placed in low-density regions of the clean data distribution to simultaneously maximize attack success and preserve clean accuracy. It proposes a bilevel optimization framework using conditional time-score matching to estimate density ratios and a mixture-model objective to steer poisoned samples into sparse regions. Experiments across MNIST, CIFAR-10, GTSRB, and TinyImageNet show the method achieves over 99% attack success before defense and maintains 50–85 percentage points higher attack success rate after fine-tuning defenses, while remaining completely immune to neuron pruning, with no neurons ever identified for removal.
鉴于现有后门攻击易被细调和剪枝等后训练防御手段破坏，本文从贝叶斯最优视角重新审视攻击设计，发现将触发样本引导至干净数据分布的低密度区域可同时最大化攻击成功率并保持干净准确率。作者提出一种双层优化框架，利用条件时间得分匹配估计密度比，并通过混合模型目标函数将污染样本置于稀疏区域。在MNIST、CIFAR-10、GTSRB和TinyImageNet上的实验表明，该方法在防御前攻击成功率超99%，防御后比最强基线高50–85个百分点，且对神经元剪枝完全免疫，所有剪枝阈值下均无神经元被识别为可移除。
LoMo: Local Modality Substitution for Deeper Vision-Language Fusion
Authors: Feng Han, Zhixiong Zhang, Zheming Liang, Yibin Wang, Jiaqi Wang
First: 2026-05-28T17:27:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:27:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved substantial progress across a wide range of understanding and reasoning tasks, driven by large-scale image-text training aimed at multimodal fusion. Ideally, replacing a textual question with its rendered-image counterpart should leave model performance essentially unaffected. In practice, however, such modality substitution induces dramatic performance degradation. We attribute this "carrier sensitivity" issue to an inherent bias in current training corpora. Across prevalent datasets such as image captioning, VQA, OCR, and web-sourced interleaved data, text and images are typically organized into distinct and asymmetric roles, with text serving as linguistic queries and images as visual references. Such data bias leads VLMs to exhibit distinct preferences for information acquisition across different modalities. Consequently, VLMs fail to align representations of semantically equivalent content across textual and visual carriers, making model reasoning fragile under modality substitution. To address this, we propose Local Modality Substitution (LoMo), a lightweight, architecture-agnostic data curation paradigm designed to provide supervision for cross-modal representational invariance between semantically equivalent text and image carriers. LoMo achieves this by reformulating single-modality prompts into seamlessly interleaved multimodal sequences. It dynamically selects target text spans and recasts them as rendered images, thereby preserving the same semantics across "text, visual, text" carriers. Extensive experiments across 13 diverse multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that LoMo significantly improves overall multimodal reasoning and yields deeper cross-modal fusion. Specifically, it delivers consistent gains across foundational models, improving over standard SFT by 2.67 points on LLaVA-OneVision-1.5-8B and 2.82 points on Qwen3.5-9B.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LoMo：用于更深层次视觉-语言融合的局部模态替换
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在广泛的理解和推理任务中取得了显著进展，得益于大规模图像-文本训练以实现多模态融合。理想情况下，用渲染图像替代文本问题应几乎不影响模型性能。然而，实际中这种模态替换会导致性能大幅下降。我们归因于当前训练语料库的固有偏差：在图像字幕、视觉问答（VQA）、光学字符识别（OCR）和网络混杂数据等主流数据集中，文本与图像通常被组织为截然不同且不对称的角色，文本作为语言查询，图像作为视觉参考。这种数据偏差导致VLMs在不同模态间表现出不同的信息获取偏好，从而无法对语义等价内容在文本与视觉载体间对齐表示，使模型在模态替换下推理脆弱。为解决此问题，我们提出局部模态替换（LoMo），一种轻量级、架构无关的数据整理范式，旨在为语义等价的文本与图像载体提供跨模态表示不变性的监督。LoMo通过将单模态提示重新构造成无缝交织的多模态序列实现此目标，动态选择目标文本片段并将其重铸为渲染图像，从而在“文本、视觉、文本”载体间保持语义一致性。在13个多样化的多模态基准测试中，LoMo显著提升了整体多模态推理能力并实现了更深层次的跨模态融合。具体而言，它在基础模型上均取得一致提升，在LLaVA-OneVision-1.5-8B上较标准SFT提升2.67分，在Qwen3.5-9B上提升2.82分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that vision-language models (VLMs) suffer performance degradation when textual inputs are replaced with their rendered-image counterparts due to inherent data bias favoring text as queries and images as references, the authors propose LoMo, a lightweight, architecture-agnostic data curation method that promotes cross-modal representational invariance. LoMo reformulates single-modality prompts into interleaved multimodal sequences by dynamically substituting text spans with their rendered images, preserving semantic equivalence across text-visual-text carriers. Experimental results across 13 multimodal benchmarks show LoMo consistently improves multimodal reasoning and cross-modal fusion, boosting performance by 2.67 points on LLaVA-OneVision-1.5-8B and 2.82 points on Qwen3.5-9B over standard supervised fine-tuning.
为解决视觉-语言模型在文本被图像替代时性能显著下降的问题（源于训练数据中文本与图像角色不对称导致的模态偏好偏差），作者提出LoMo方法，一种轻量级、架构无关的数据整理范式，旨在促进语义等价内容在文本与图像载体间的表征不变性。LoMo通过将单模态提示动态转化为图像化文本片段并嵌入多模态序列，实现语义一致性。在13个基准测试中，LoMo显著提升多模态推理能力，在LLaVA-OneVision-1.5-8B和Qwen3.5-9B模型上分别比标准监督微调提升2.67和2.82分。
minWM: A Full-Stack Open-Source Framework for Real-Time Interactive Video World Models
Authors: Min Zhao, Hongzhou Zhu, Bokai Yan, Zihan Zhou, Yimin Chen, Wenqiang Sun, Kaiwen Zheng, Guande He, Xiao Yang, Chongxuan Li, Fan Bao, Jun Zhu
First: 2026-05-28T17:27:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:27:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent video diffusion foundation models have achieved remarkable progress in high-quality video generation, yet turning them into real-time interactive video world models remains challenging. Interactive world models require controllable, causal, and low-latency rollout, which in practice demands a full pipeline spanning data construction, controllable fine-tuning, autoregressive training, few-step distillation, and streaming inference. In this work, we present minWM, a full-stack open-source framework for building real-time interactive video world models. minWM provides an end-to-end pipeline that converts existing bidirectional T2V/TI2V video foundation models into camera-controllable few-step autoregressive world models. Specifically, minWM first fine-tunes a bidirectional video diffusion model with camera control, and then applies the Causal Forcing / Causal Forcing++ pipeline, including AR diffusion training, causal ODE or causal consistency distillation, and asymmetric DMD, to distill it into a few-step autoregressive generator for low-latency rollout. The framework is modular and architecture-extensible: we instantiate it on representative open backbones, including Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B and HY1.5-TI2V-8B, covering both cross-attention-based condition injection and MMDiT-style architectures. minWM also supports adapting existing video world models, such as HY-WorldPlay, to new data distributions, training recipes, and latency targets. Beyond releasing runnable scripts, checkpoints, documentation, and inference code, we provide practical ablations on camera trajectory quality, controllability training steps, and minimal batch-size requirements. We hope minWM serves as a reproducible and extensible recipe for building and adapting real-time interactive video world models. Project Page: [https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM](https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM)
中文标题/摘要
标题：minWM：用于实时交互式视频世界模型的全栈开源框架
近期视频扩散基础模型在高质量视频生成方面取得了显著进展，但将其转化为实时交互式视频世界模型仍具挑战。交互式世界模型需要可控、因果且低延迟的 rollout，这在实践中要求涵盖数据构建、可控微调、自回归训练、少量步骤蒸馏和流式推理的完整流程。本文提出 minWM，一个用于构建实时交互式视频世界模型的全栈开源框架。minWM 提供端到端流程，可将现有双向 T2V/TI2V 视频基础模型转化为相机可控的少量步骤自回归世界模型。具体而言，minWM 首先对双向视频扩散模型进行相机控制微调，然后应用因果强制/因果强制++流程，包括自回归扩散训练、因果 ODE 或因果一致性蒸馏及非对称 DMD，将其蒸馏为低延迟 rollout 的少量步骤自回归生成器。该框架模块化且架构可扩展：我们将其实例化于代表性开源骨干网络，包括 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 和 HY1.5-TI2V-8B，涵盖基于交叉注意力的条件注入和 MMDiT 风格架构。minWM 还支持将现有视频世界模型（如 HY-WorldPlay）适配至新数据分布、训练方案和延迟目标。除发布可运行脚本、检查点、文档和推理代码外，我们还提供了关于相机轨迹质量、可控性训练步数及最小批量大小需求的实用消融实验。我们希望 minWM 成为构建和适配实时交互式视频世界模型的可复现且可扩展的方案。 项目页面：[https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM](https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM)
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to transform high-quality video diffusion models into real-time, interactive world models with controllable and low-latency behavior, the authors introduce minWM, a full-stack open-source framework. The method involves fine-tuning bidirectional video models for camera control, followed by a causal distillation pipeline including autoregressive training and asymmetric DMD to produce a few-step autoregressive generator. Experiments on models like Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B and HY1.5-TI2V-8B demonstrate successful adaptation to new data and latency targets, with ablations showing that camera trajectory quality and training steps significantly impact performance, while minimal batch sizes suffice for inference.
为将高质量视频扩散模型转化为实时、可控制的交互式世界模型，minWM 提出了一套端到端的开源框架，弥合了生成模型与低延迟因果推演之间的鸿沟。该方法首先对双向 T2V/TI2V 模型进行相机控制微调，随后通过自回归扩散训练、因果 ODE 或一致性蒸馏及非对称 DMD 等步骤，将其蒸馏为几步自回归生成器。实验表明，该框架在 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 和 HY1.5-TI2V-8B 上成功实现低延迟交互，消融实验揭示训练步数和批次大小对效果敏感，且支持适配新数据分布与延迟目标。
Benchmarking and Mitigating Sycophancy in Medical Vision Language Models
Authors: Juangui Xu, Zikun Guo, Jingwei Lv, Hongbin Lin, Shu Yang, Jun Wen, Di Wang, Lijie Hu
First: 2025-09-26T07:02:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:26:29+00:00
Comments: 19figures, 61pages. The first two authors contributed equally
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual language models (VLMs) have the potential to transform medical workflows. However, the deployment is limited by sycophancy. Despite this serious threat to patient safety, a systematic benchmark remains lacking. This paper addresses this gap by introducing a Medical benchmark that applies multiple templates to VLMs in a hierarchical medical visual question answering task. We find that current VLMs are highly susceptible to visual cues, with failure rates showing a correlation to model size or overall accuracy. we discover that perceived authority and user mimicry are powerful triggers, suggesting a bias mechanism independent of visual data. To overcome this, we propose a Visual Information Purification for Evidence based Responses (VIPER) strategy that proactively filters out non-evidence-based social cues, thereby reinforcing evidence based reasoning. VIPER reduces sycophancy while maintaining interpretability and consistently outperforms baseline methods, laying the necessary foundation for the robust and secure integration of VLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：医疗视觉语言模型中的奉承行为基准测试与缓解方法
视觉语言模型（VLMs）有望变革医疗工作流程，但其部署受限于奉承行为。尽管这对患者安全构成严重威胁，但系统性的基准测试仍缺失。本文通过引入一个医疗基准，采用多种模板在分层医疗视觉问答任务中评估VLMs，填补了这一空白。我们发现当前VLMs极易受视觉线索影响，失败率与模型规模或整体准确率相关。我们发现感知权威和用户模仿是强触发因素，暗示存在独立于视觉数据的偏见机制。为解决此问题，我们提出一种基于证据的视觉信息净化策略（VIPER），主动过滤非证据性社会线索，从而强化基于证据的推理。VIPER在降低奉承行为的同时保持可解释性，并持续优于基线方法，为VLMs的稳健安全集成奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the urgent need to ensure patient safety in medical settings, this study investigates sycophancy in visual language models (VLMs) used for medical visual question answering. The authors introduce a hierarchical benchmark using multiple templates to systematically evaluate how VLMs respond to visual cues and social triggers. Their experiments reveal that larger, more accurate models are more prone to sycophantic behavior, particularly when exposed to perceived authority or user mimicry, indicating a bias mechanism independent of visual data. To mitigate this, they propose VIPER, a strategy that filters non-evidence-based social cues, thereby reinforcing evidence-based reasoning. VIPER significantly reduces sycophantic responses while preserving model interpretability and consistently outperforms baseline methods, offering a critical step toward deploying VLMs safely in clinical environments.
为保障医疗场景下患者安全，本研究针对用于医学视觉问答的视觉语言模型（VLMs）中的奉承倾向问题展开系统性研究。作者设计了多模板层级基准测试，评估模型对视觉线索和社会触发因素的反应。实验发现，模型规模越大、准确率越高，越易受权威暗示或用户模仿影响，表明存在独立于视觉数据的偏见机制。为缓解该问题，作者提出VIPER策略，主动过滤非证据性社会线索，强化基于证据的推理。该方法显著降低奉承倾向，同时保持模型可解释性，并持续优于基线方法，为VLMs在临床环境中的安全可靠部署奠定基础。
Prune-OPD: Efficient and Reliable On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Reasoning
Authors: Zhicheng Yang, Zhijiang Guo, Yifan Song, Minrui Xu, Yongxin Wang, Yiwei Wang, Xiaodan Liang, Jing Tang
First: 2026-05-08T14:38:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:26:17+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On-policy distillation (OPD) leverages dense teacher rewards to enhance reasoning models. However, scaling OPD to long-horizon tasks exposes a critical flaw: as the student's generated prefix inevitably diverges from the teacher's thought process, the teacher's dense reward loses local exploitability. Continuing to generate and evaluate tokens on these ``drifted'' trajectories not only degrades reward quality but also incurs massive computational waste. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Prune-OPD}, a framework that dynamically aligns training budgets with supervision quality. By continuously monitoring the local compatibility between student and teacher predictions (e.g., via top-$k$ overlap), Prune-OPD detects prefix-drift events in real time. Upon detecting severe drift, it monotonically down-weights subsequent unreliable rewards and triggers dynamic rollout truncation. This allows the training process to halt futile generation and reallocate compute strictly to reliable teacher supervision. Across diverse teacher-student combinations, Prune-OPD consistently aligns computation with supervision reliability. When prefix drift makes dense teacher rewards unreliable, it reduces training time by 37.6\%--68.0\% while preserving, and often improving, performance on challenging benchmarks (AMC, AIME, HMMT). When student-teacher compatibility remains high, it automatically preserves long-context supervision by expanding the training window. These results suggest that Prune-OPD improves OPD not by blindly shortening rollouts, but by reallocating computation toward locally exploitable teacher rewards.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Prune-OPD：面向长时程推理的高效可靠在线蒸馏
在线蒸馏（OPD）利用密集教师奖励提升推理模型，但在长时程任务中扩展OPD暴露出关键缺陷：随着学生生成的前缀不可避免地偏离教师思维过程，教师的密集奖励失去局部可利用性。继续在这些“漂移”轨迹上生成和评估token不仅降低奖励质量，还造成巨大计算浪费。为此，我们提出\textbf{Prune-OPD}框架，动态调整训练预算与监督质量匹配。通过持续监控学生与教师预测的局部兼容性（如通过top-k重叠），Prune-OPD实时检测前缀漂移事件。检测到严重漂移时，它单调降低后续不可靠奖励权重并触发动态 rollout 截断。这使训练过程能停止无用生成，将计算资源严格分配给可靠教师监督。在多种教师-学生组合中，Prune-OPD始终使计算与监督可靠性对齐。当前缀漂移使密集教师奖励不可靠时，其训练时间减少37.6%–68.0%，同时在挑战性基准（AMC、AIME、HMMT）上保持甚至提升性能。当学生-教师兼容性高时，它自动扩展训练窗口以保留长上下文监督。结果表明，Prune-OPD并非盲目缩短rollout，而是将计算资源重新分配至局部可利用的教师奖励。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of on-policy distillation (OPD) in long-horizon tasks where student prefixes diverge from teacher trajectories, Prune-OPD introduces a dynamic mechanism to reallocate computational resources based on local prediction compatibility. It monitors top-k overlap between student and teacher outputs to detect prefix drift, then down-weights unreliable rewards and truncates rollouts when drift is severe, preventing wasted computation. Experiments show it reduces training time by 37.6%–68.0% while maintaining or improving performance on AMC, AIME, and HMMT benchmarks, and automatically extends rollouts when compatibility remains high, demonstrating that efficiency gains come from targeted resource allocation rather than indiscriminate truncation.
为了解决长时序任务中学生生成前缀偏离教师轨迹导致的奖励不可靠和计算浪费问题，Prune-OPD 引入动态机制，通过 top-k 重叠度实时监测学生与教师预测的局部兼容性，检测到严重漂移时则逐步降低不可靠奖励权重并截断生成。该方法使训练过程能及时终止无效生成，将计算资源集中于可靠监督。实验表明，Prune-OPD 在 AMC、AIME、HMMT 等基准上训练时间减少 37.6%–68.0%，性能保持或提升，且在兼容性高时自动延长训练窗口。
How LoRA Remembers? A Parametric Memory Law for LLM Finetuning
Authors: Ziwen Xu, Haiwen Hong, Linsong Yu, Benglei Cui, Longtao Huang, Hui Xue, Ningyu Zhang
First: 2026-05-28T17:22:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:22:24+00:00
Comments: Ongoing work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) must continuously learn and update knowledge to remain effective in dynamic real-world environments. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for such memory updates, existing studies mainly rely on qualitative downstream evaluations, leaving the quantitative capacity limits and underlying dynamics of exact parametric memory largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we employ LoRA as a controlled memory capacity probe within the latent space to systematically quantify exact parametric memory. We introduce the Parametric Memory Law, a robust power law linking loss reduction Delta L to effective parameters and sequence length. At the token level, fine-grained analysis reveals a deterministic phase transition, demonstrating that a prediction probability of p > 0.5 constitutes a sufficient condition for verbatim recall under greedy decoding. Driven by these insights, we introduce MemFT, a threshold-guided optimization strategy that dynamically redistributes the training budget toward sub-threshold tokens. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemFT can enhance memory fidelity and efficiency. Code will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/ParametricMemoryLaw.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LoRA如何记忆？LLM微调中的参数记忆定律
大语言模型（LLM）必须持续学习和更新知识以适应动态的现实世界环境。虽然低秩适应（LoRA）常用于此类记忆更新，但现有研究主要依赖定性下游评估，对精确参数记忆的定量容量限制和底层动态机制鲜有探索。为弥合这一差距，我们利用LoRA作为潜在空间中的可控记忆容量探针，系统量化精确参数记忆。我们提出参数记忆定律，即损失减少量Delta L与有效参数和序列长度之间的稳健幂律关系。在token层面，细粒度分析揭示确定性相变，表明预测概率p > 0.5是贪心解码下逐字召回的充分条件。基于这些洞见，我们提出MemFT，一种阈值引导的优化策略，动态将训练预算重新分配至低于阈值的token。实证评估表明，MemFT可提升记忆保真度与效率。代码将在https://github.com/zjunlp/ParametricMemoryLaw发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how LoRA updates memory in LLMs quantitatively, this work uses LoRA as a controlled probe to measure parametric memory capacity in latent space. It derives the Parametric Memory Law, a power-law relationship between loss reduction and effective parameters/sequence length, and identifies a token-level phase transition where recall probability p > 0.5 enables verbatim recall under greedy decoding. Based on this, MemFT—a threshold-guided optimization strategy—reallocates training resources to underperforming tokens, improving memory fidelity and efficiency in experiments.
为理解LoRA微调中参数记忆的量化极限与动态机制，该研究将LoRA作为可控探针，在潜在空间中系统量化参数记忆。提出参数记忆定律，即损失下降量与有效参数和序列长度之间的幂律关系。细粒度的词元级分析揭示了在预测概率p>0.5时存在确定性相变，满足贪心解码下的精确召回条件。据此提出MemFT策略，动态将训练预算分配至低于阈值的词元，实验证明其可提升记忆保真度与效率。
TabPFN-3: Technical Report
Authors: Léo Grinsztajn, Klemens Flöge, Oscar Key, Felix Birkel, Philipp Jund, Brendan Roof, Mihir Manium, Shi Bin Hoo, Magnus Bühler, Anurag Garg, Dominik Safaric, Jake Robertson, Benjamin Jäger, Simone Alessi, Adrian Hayler, Vladyslav Moroshan, Lennart Purucker, Philipp Singer, Alan Arazi, Julien Siems, Jan Hendrik Metzen, Georg Grab, Nick Erickson, Siyuan Guo, Eliott Kalfon, Simon Bing, David Salinas, Clara Cornu, Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn, Diana Kriuchkova, Kursat Kaya, Lydia Sidhoum, Marie Salmon, Jerry Chen, Madelon Hulsebos, Yann LeCun, Samuel Müller, Bernhard Schölkopf, Sauraj Gambhir, Noah Hollmann, Frank Hutter
First: 2026-05-13T18:01:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:22:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TabPFN-3：技术报告
表格数据支撑着科学与工业中大多数高价值预测问题，TabPFN 已推动该模态的基础模型革命。基于用户反馈设计，TabPFN-3 在此基础上将最先进性能扩展至含 100 万训练行的数据集，并大幅降低训练与推理时间。其仅在先前合成数据上预训练，显著推动了表格预测前沿，并在时间序列、关系型及表格-文本数据上带来显著增益。在标准表格基准 TabArena 上，TabPFN-3 的单次前向传播显著优于所有其他模型（含调优与集成基线），并帕累托主导速度/性能前沿。在更多样化的数据集上，TabPFN-3 在多类别数据集上排名第一，并在含 100 万训练行和 200 个特征的数据集上击败 8 小时调优的梯度提升树基线。TabPFN-3 引入了测试时计算扩展至表格基础模型。我们的 API 服务 TabPFN-3-Plus（Thinking）利用此特性，在 TabArena 上以超过 200 Elo 的优势击败所有非 TabPFN 模型，在最大数据子集上升至 420 Elo，同时在不使用大语言模型、真实数据、网络搜索或任何其他模型的情况下，性能超越 AutoGluon 1.5 extreme 且快 10 倍。TabPFN-3 扩展了模型能力，实现关系型数据（RelBenchV1 新 SOTA 基础模型）和表格-文本数据（TabSTAR 上 SOTA 通过 TabPFN-3-Plus）的最先进预测；并优化现有集成：专用检查点 TabPFN-TS-3 在时间序列基准 fev-bench 上排名第二，SHAP 值计算速度提升至 120 倍。TabPFN-3 在性能提升的同时，比 TabPFN-2.5 快达 20 倍。此外，通过减少 KV 缓存和行分块，可在单张 H100 上扩展至 100 万行并实现快速推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, high-performance tabular prediction, TabPFN-3 leverages synthetic pretraining and architectural optimizations to achieve state-of-the-art results on datasets up to 1M rows and 200 features, while drastically reducing training and inference time. It introduces test-time compute scaling and a specialized API (TabPFN-3-Plus) that outperforms all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena and beats AutoGluon 1.5 extreme by 10x speed without LLMs or external resources. Experimentally, TabPFN-3 achieves SOTA on relational and tabular-text benchmarks, ranks first on multi-class datasets, and delivers 120x faster SHAP computation, all while being up to 20x faster than its predecessor.
为应对大规模表格数据预测的需求，TabPFN-3 通过合成数据预训练与架构优化，实现了在百万行数据集上的最先进性能。它引入了测试时计算扩展与行分块机制，支持单张H100显卡高效推理，使其在TabArena上超越所有非TabPFN模型200+ Elo分，且在速度上比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍。同时，它在关系型与表格文本数据上刷新SOTA，在时序基准上排名第二，SHAP值计算提速最高达120倍，整体性能较前代提升20倍。
Stable-Layers: Fine-Tuning Image Layer Decomposition Models with VLM-Scored Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Ciara Rowles, Reshinth Adithyan, Nikhil Pinnaparaju, Vikram Voleti, Mark Boss
First: 2026-05-28T17:20:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:20:31+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 8 figures, 4 tables. Project page: https://stability-ai.github.io/stable-layers.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present Stable-Layers, a reinforcement learning framework that eliminates the need for paired supervision by fine-tuning a pretrained layer decomposition model using only feedback from a vision-language model (VLM). Starting from Qwen-Image-Layered, we apply Flow-GRPO with LoRA adaptation, sampling multiple candidate decompositions per image, scoring them with a VLM, and optimising the policy from group-relative advantages. The key challenge lies in designing a reliable reward signal: VLMs scoring samples in isolation tend to compress their judgements into a narrow band, leaving GRPO with little within-group variance to learn from. We address this with a two-stage evaluation pipeline that pairs structured per-sample scoring across five edit-centric criteria with a grid-based calibration step in which the VLM re-scores all candidates side-by-side. Stable-Layers produces decompositions with stronger layer separation, fewer blank or artifact-heavy layers, and lower per-layer reconstruction error on the Crello dataset compared to the base model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Stable-Layers：使用VLM评分强化学习微调图像层分解模型
我们提出了Stable-Layers，一种强化学习框架，通过仅使用视觉-语言模型（VLM）的反馈来微调预训练的层分解模型，从而消除对成对监督的需求。从Qwen-Image-Layered出发，我们采用Flow-GRPO结合LoRA适配，为每张图像采样多个候选分解结果，用VLM评分，并从组内相对优势中优化策略。关键挑战在于设计可靠的奖励信号：VLM在孤立评分样本时往往将其判断压缩到狭窄范围内，导致GRPO组内方差不足。我们通过两阶段评估流程解决此问题：第一阶段在五个以编辑为中心的标准上进行结构化逐样本评分；第二阶段采用网格校准步骤，让VLM并排重新评分所有候选结果。与基线模型相比，Stable-Layers在Crello数据集上生成的分解结果具有更强的层分离性、更少的空白或伪影密集层，以及更低的每层重建误差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to fine-tune image layer decomposition models without paired supervision, the authors introduce Stable-Layers, a reinforcement learning framework that uses feedback from a vision-language model (VLM) to optimize a pretrained model.
