They employ Flow-GRPO with LoRA, sampling multiple candidate decompositions per image, scoring them via a two-stage VLM evaluation pipeline that combines per-sample criteria with grid-based calibration, and optimizing the policy using group-relative advantages. Experimental results on the Crello dataset show that Stable-Layers produces decompositions with improved layer separation, fewer blank or artifact-heavy layers, and lower per-layer reconstruction error compared to the base model.
为在无配对监督下微调图像层分解模型，作者提出Stable-Layers强化学习框架，仅依赖视觉语言模型（VLM）反馈。基于Qwen-Image-Layered模型，采用Flow-GRPO与LoRA，对每张图像采样多个分解候选，通过VLM评分并基于组内相对优势优化策略。为解决VLM评分压缩问题，设计两阶段评估：先按五项编辑中心标准对样本结构化评分，再通过网格化方式让VLM重新并排评分所有候选。在Crello数据集实验中，该方法显著提升层间分离度，减少空白或伪影层，并降低每层重建误差，优于基线模型。
Catalyst-Agent: Autonomous heterogeneous catalyst screening with an LLM Agent
Authors: Achuth Chandrasekhar, Janghoon Ock, Amir Barati Farimani
First: 2026-03-01T22:44:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:20:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The discovery of novel catalysts tailored for particular applications is a major challenge for the twenty-first century. Traditional methods for this include time-consuming and expensive experimental trial-and-error approaches in labs based on chemical theory or heavily computational first-principles approaches based on density functional theory. Recent studies show that deep learning models like graph neural networks (GNNs) can significantly speed up the screening of catalyst materials by many orders of magnitude, with very high accuracy and fidelity. In this work, we introduce Catalyst-Agent, a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server-based, LLM-powered AI agent. It can explore vast material databases using the OPTIMADE API, make structural modifications, calculate adsorption energies using Meta FAIRchem's UMA (GNN) model via FAIRchem's AdsorbML workflow and slab construction, and make useful material suggestions to the researcher in a closed-loop manner, including structural modifications to refine near-miss candidates. It is tested on three pivotal reactions: the oxygen reduction reaction (ORR), the nitrogen reduction reaction (NRR), and the CO2 reduction reaction (CO2RR). Catalyst-Agent achieves a success rate of 33-41% among all the materials it chooses and evaluates, and manages to converge in 1-4 trials per successful material on average. This work demonstrates the potential of AI agents to exercise their planning capabilities and tool use for autonomous catalyst screening workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：催化剂代理：基于大语言模型的自主异质催化剂筛选
为特定应用设计新型催化剂是二十一世纪的重大挑战。传统方法包括基于化学理论的耗时且昂贵的实验室试错法，或基于密度泛函理论的计算第一性原理方法。近期研究表明，深度学习模型（如图神经网络GNN）可将催化剂材料筛选速度提升多个数量级，且具有高精度和保真度。本文引入Catalyst-Agent，这是一种基于模型上下文协议（MCP）服务器、由大语言模型驱动的AI代理。它可通过OPTIMADE API探索海量材料数据库，进行结构修改，利用Meta FAIRchem的UMA（GNN）模型通过FAIRchem的AdsorbML工作流计算吸附能，并以闭环方式向研究人员提供有用材料建议，包括对接近目标的候选材料进行结构优化。该方法在三个关键反应——氧还原反应（ORR）、氮还原反应（NRR）和二氧化碳还原反应（CO2RR）——中进行了测试。Catalyst-Agent在所选和评估的所有材料中成功率达33%-41%，平均每种成功材料需1-4次试验即可收敛。本研究展示了AI代理通过规划能力和工具使用实现自主催化剂筛选流程的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to accelerate catalyst discovery for critical reactions like ORR, NRR, and CO2RR, Catalyst-Agent leverages an LLM-powered AI agent operating via a Model Context Protocol server to autonomously screen heterogeneous catalysts. It queries material databases through the OPTIMADE API, applies structural modifications, computes adsorption energies using FAIRchem’s UMA GNN model, and iteratively refines candidate materials in a closed-loop manner. Experimental results show the agent achieves a 33-41% success rate among evaluated materials and converges on viable catalysts within 1-4 trials per successful material, demonstrating its capability to autonomously plan and execute screening workflows.
为加速氧还原反应（ORR）、氮还原反应（NRR）和二氧化碳还原反应（CO2RR）所需催化剂的发现，Catalyst-Agent 利用基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的LLM智能体自主筛选异质催化剂。该智能体通过OPTIMADE API查询材料数据库，进行结构修改，调用FAIRchem的AdsorbML工作流与GNN模型（UMA）计算吸附能，并在闭环中持续优化候选材料。实验表明，该智能体在评估材料中成功率达33%-41%，平均仅需1-4轮即可收敛至有效催化剂，验证了AI智能体自主规划与工具调用在催化剂筛选中的潜力。
VideoFDB: Evaluating Full-Duplex Vision-Speech Capabilities in Conversational Agents
Authors: Amrita Mazumdar, Seonwook Park, Rajarshi Roy, Nikhil Srihari, Shengze Wang, Yuhao Zhou, Julia Wang, Koki Nagano, Shalini De Mello
First: 2026-05-28T17:20:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:20:01+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/amri/projects/video-fdb/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Natural human conversation is full-duplex and audio-visual: people simultaneously speak and listen while continuously interpreting and producing nonverbal cues, such as nods, smiles, and gestures. To support successful human-agent interaction, agents must model full-duplex audiovisual conversation; however, existing full-duplex benchmarks evaluate only speech. In this work, we present VideoFDB, the first benchmark to evaluate full-duplex audio-visual-to-audio-visual (AV2AV) conversational agents. VideoFDB contributes (i) 237 dyadic clips spanning 11 nonverbal conversational dynamics from real-world video calls, (ii) a taxonomy separating perception from generation behaviors, and (iii) a rubric-based LM-as-judge evaluation framework with interpretable axes for assessing conversational quality with respect to nonverbal conversational dynamics. Across open- and closed-source vision-speech agents, we find systematic failure modes: captioning collapse and visual-stream ignorance, and we show that current systems exploit vision for explicit visual question answering but not for the streaming joint audiovisual grounding required in natural conversation. We further evaluate cascaded speech-to-avatar systems and find that their architecture fundamentally precludes the production of full-duplex nonverbal cues. As the first benchmark for full-duplex AV2AV interaction, VideoFDB establishes a foundation for systematic evaluation and, we hope, will accelerate the advancement and development of next-generation multimodal conversational agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VideoFDB：评估对话代理中的全双工视听能力
自然的人类对话是全双工且视听结合的：人们在同时说话和倾听的同时，持续解读和生成非语言线索，如点头、微笑和手势。为支持成功的人机交互，代理必须建模全双工视听对话；然而，现有的全双工基准仅评估语音。本文提出VideoFDB，这是首个用于评估全双工视听到视听（AV2AV）对话代理的基准。VideoFDB贡献包括：(i) 237个来自真实视频通话的双人对话片段，涵盖11种非语言对话动态；(ii) 一种区分感知与生成行为的分类体系；(iii) 一种基于评分标准的LM作为裁判的评估框架，具有可解释的评估轴，用于评估对话质量与非语言对话动态的关系。在开源和闭源的视听代理中，我们发现系统性失败模式：字幕生成崩溃和视觉流忽略，并表明当前系统仅利用视觉进行显式视觉问答，而非自然对话所需的流式视听联合接地。我们进一步评估级联语音到虚拟形象系统，发现其架构从根本上排除了全双工非语言线索的生成。作为首个全双工AV2AV交互基准，VideoFDB为系统性评估奠定了基础，我们希望它能加速下一代多模态对话代理的发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for conversational agents to handle real-time, audio-visual, full-duplex interactions like humans do, this work introduces VideoFDB, the first benchmark evaluating full-duplex audio-visual-to-audio-visual (AV2AV) capabilities. It includes 237 dyadic video clips capturing 11 nonverbal conversational dynamics, a taxonomy distinguishing perception and generation, and a rubric-based evaluation framework using language models as judges. Experiments reveal systematic failures in current vision-speech agents, including captioning collapse and visual-stream ignorance, and show that systems primarily use vision for QA rather than for continuous joint audiovisual grounding. Cascaded speech-to-avatar systems were also found incapable of producing full-duplex nonverbal cues due to their architecture.
为支持人类与智能体进行真实双向视听对话，本研究提出VideoFDB，这是首个评估双向视听到视听（AV2AV）对话能力的基准。该基准包含237段真实视频通话中的双人对话片段，构建了区分感知与生成行为的分类体系，并采用基于评分标准的LM裁判框架评估非语言对话动态。实验发现现有系统普遍存在“字幕崩溃”和“视觉流忽略”等系统性缺陷，且视觉能力主要用于显式问答而非自然对话所需的连续视听联合建模。进一步评估级联语音到虚拟形象系统时发现，其架构根本上无法生成双向非语言线索，揭示了其内在设计限制。
Algorithms with Polynomially-Improved Approximation Factors for the $2 \rightarrow q$ Norm, and Applications
Authors: Samuel B. Hopkins, Stefan Tiegel
First: 2026-05-24T23:56:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:16:43+00:00
Comments: v2 corrected minor typos
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The $2 \rightarrow q$ norm of a matrix $X \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times d}$ is defined as $\lVert X \rVert_{2 \rightarrow q} = \sup_{\lVert v \rVert_2 = 1} \lVert Xv \rVert_q$. We give polynomial-time multiplicative approximation algorithms for this norm when $q > 2$ (i.e. in the hypercontractive setting). This problem either directly captures or is closely related to long-standing open problems in combinatorial optimization and hardness of approximation (e.g. Small Set Expansion), quantum information (e.g. Best Separable State), and algorithmic statistics. Very little is known about what approximation factors we can achieve for this problem in polynomial time, even though such approximations have significant downstream consequences. Barak, Brandão, Harrow, Kelner, Steurer, and Zhou showed that no polynomial-time algorithm can achieve an approximation factor better than $2^{\sqrt{\log n}}$, assuming the Exponential Time Hypothesis (FOCS'12). On the other hand, a simple spectral algorithm gives a $d^{1/4}$-approximation as a baseline. We give, to the best of our knowledge, the first polynomial-time approximation algorithm beating this baseline by polynomial factors. For the important special case of $q = 4$ it achieves a $d^{1/8}$-approximation. All previous algorithms required additional assumptions on $X$, or only surpassed the baseline for small values of $n$. Moreover, we construct sum-of-squares certificates for the $2 \rightarrow q$ norm. This directly implies improved algorithms for robust mean and covariance estimation, robust regression, and clustering, when the data only satisfies a bound on its $q$-th moment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有多项式改进近似因子的 $2 \rightarrow q$ 范数算法及其应用
矩阵 $X \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times d}$ 的 $2 \rightarrow q$ 范数定义为 $\lVert X \rVert_{2 \rightarrow q} = \sup_{\lVert v \rVert_2 = 1} \lVert Xv \rVert_q$。我们给出了当 $q > 2$ 时（即在超收缩设定下）该范数的多项式时间乘法近似算法。该问题要么直接涉及，要么与组合优化和近似难度中的长期开放问题密切相关（例如小集合扩展），量子信息（例如最佳可分离态）和算法统计学。 尽管此类近似具有显著的下游影响，但关于在多项式时间内可实现的近似因子知之甚少。Barak、Brandão、Harrow、Kelner、Steurer 和 Zhou 在假设指数时间假设成立的前提下证明，不存在多项式时间算法能实现优于 $2^{\sqrt{\log n}}$ 的近似因子（FOCS'12）。另一方面，一个简单的谱算法给出了 $d^{1/4}$ 的基准近似。我们给出了目前已知的第一个多项式时间近似算法，其近似因子在多项式意义上优于该基准。对于重要的特例 $q = 4$，该算法实现了 $d^{1/8}$ 的近似。此前所有算法要么需要对 $X$ 做额外假设，要么仅在 $n$ 较小时超越基准。 此外，我们构造了 $2 \rightarrow q$ 范数的平方和证书。这直接意味着当数据仅满足其 $q$ 阶矩有界时，对鲁棒均值与协方差估计、鲁棒回归和聚类问题的改进算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by longstanding open problems in combinatorial optimization, quantum information, and algorithmic statistics, this work develops polynomial-time multiplicative approximation algorithms for the $2 \rightarrow q$ matrix norm when $q > 2$. Building on a spectral baseline that achieves $d^{1/4}$-approximation, the authors introduce a novel algorithm that improves this by polynomial factors, achieving $d^{1/8}$ for $q=4$, which is the first such improvement without additional assumptions on the matrix. The method leverages sum-of-squares certificates, enabling improved algorithms for robust estimation and clustering under moment constraints.
为解决组合优化、量子信息和算法统计中的长期开放问题，本文针对 $q > 2$ 时的 $2 \rightarrow q$ 矩阵范数设计了多项式时间近似算法。在已知的谱算法基准（$d^{1/4}$-近似）基础上，作者首次提出了多项式因子超越该基准的算法，对关键情形 $q=4$ 达到 $d^{1/8}$ 近似。该方法基于和平方证书构造，进一步提升了在数据仅满足 $q$ 阶矩约束下的鲁棒均值与协方差估计、鲁棒回归和聚类算法的性能。此前方法或需额外假设，或仅适用于小规模 $n$，而本文结果突破了基于指数时间假设的 $2^{\sqrt{\log n}}$ 难度下界。
Wasserstein Contraction of Coordinate Ascent Variational Inference
Authors: Rocco Caprio, Adrien Corenflos, Sam Power
First: 2026-05-28T17:16:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:16:22+00:00
Comments: 17 pages + 3 pages appendix, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the contraction in Wasserstein distance of the coordinate ascent variational inference algorithm. This is shown to hold under a transport-information inequality at the fixed points and a functional smoothness condition. The results are general and sharp, allow for local convergence guarantees, hold for general smooth manifolds, and also in some non-smooth spaces. We consider applications to Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Models, and high-dimensional Bayesian Probit Regression, and Logistic Regression with Pólya-Gamma random variables (i.e. Jaakkola-Jordan's algorithm).
中文标题/摘要
标题：坐标上升变分推断的Wasserstein收缩性
我们研究了坐标上升变分推断算法在Wasserstein距离下的收缩性。该性质在固定点处满足传输-信息不等式和函数光滑性条件时成立。结果具有普遍性和精确性，允许局部收敛保证，适用于一般光滑流形，也适用于某些非光滑空间。我们考虑了其在贝叶斯高斯混合模型、高维贝叶斯Probit回归以及使用Pólya-Gamma随机变量的Logistic回归（即Jaakkola-Jordan算法）中的应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for theoretical guarantees on convergence in variational inference, this paper analyzes the Wasserstein contraction of coordinate ascent variational inference under transport-information inequalities and functional smoothness conditions. The authors establish general and sharp convergence results that apply to local convergence, smooth manifolds, and even non-smooth spaces. Experimental results on Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Models, high-dimensional Bayesian Probit Regression, and Logistic Regression with Pólya-Gamma variables demonstrate that the algorithm converges reliably under these theoretical conditions, validating the contraction framework across diverse probabilistic models.
为获得变分推断算法的理论收敛保证，本文研究坐标上升变分推断在Wasserstein距离下的收缩性质。作者在固定点满足传输信息不等式和函数光滑性条件下，证明了算法的收缩性，得出适用于光滑流形及部分非光滑空间的通用且精确的收敛结果。在贝叶斯高斯混合模型、高维贝叶斯Probit回归及Pólya-Gamma随机变量下的Logistic回归等实验中，算法表现出局部收敛性和强收缩行为。
Same Evidence, Different Answers: Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Language Models
Authors: Zizhuo Lin, Quanling Liu, Jinsheng Quan, Chao Zhang, Yifan Zhu, Xing Shi, Jingtao Xu, Zhihui Li, Yawei Luo
First: 2026-05-28T17:14:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:14:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) often solve a task when all instructions are given in a single prompt, but fail when the same information is revealed gradually across turns. When a clean FULL prompt and a RAW-SHARDED conversation contain the same complete user evidence, the model should still arrive at the same answer. We argue that a key reason for this gap is self-anchored drift: responses produced under partial information introduce unsupported assumptions, and those assumptions later distort the final answer. To reduce this effect, we propose Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD). During training, the same base model is used in two roles: a frozen teacher conditioned on the clean FULL prompt and a trainable student that receives the same evidence incrementally through a multi-turn conversation; CCOPD aligns the student's behavior on its own trajectories with the teacher's canonical full-context behavior. Trained only on math problem conversations, CCOPD yields a 32\% average relative improvement in RAW-SHARDED performance over the original base model across math and five zero-shot out-of-domain task families, while largely preserving full-context performance. Further analyses suggest that CCOPD strengthens grounding in user evidence and reduces sensitivity to contamination from earlier assistant turns.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相同证据，不同答案：多轮语言模型的规范上下文在线蒸馏
大语言模型（LLM）在单次提示中包含所有指令时通常能解决问题，但在信息逐步揭示的多轮对话中则表现不佳。当干净的完整提示（FULL）与原始分段对话（RAW-SHARDED）包含相同完整用户证据时，模型应得出相同答案。我们认为，这一差距的关键原因是自我锚定漂移：在部分信息下生成的响应会引入无依据假设，这些假设随后扭曲最终答案。为减少此效应，我们提出规范上下文在线蒸馏（CCOPD）。训练时，同一基模型扮演两个角色：一个冻结的教师模型，基于干净完整提示；一个可训练的学生模型，通过多轮对话逐步接收相同证据；CCOPD使学生在自身轨迹上的行为与教师的规范完整上下文行为对齐。仅在数学问题对话上训练，CCOPD在数学及五个零样本跨域任务中，相较原始基模型平均相对性能提升32%，同时基本保持完整上下文性能。进一步分析表明，CCOPD增强了对用户证据的锚定，降低了对早期助手回复污染的敏感性。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the issue where large language models perform poorly on multi-turn conversations despite having the same complete evidence as in a single prompt, attributing this to self-anchored drift—where partial responses introduce unsupported assumptions that distort later outputs. To mitigate this, the authors propose Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD), which trains a student model on incremental evidence while aligning its behavior with a frozen teacher model conditioned on a clean full prompt. Evaluated on math problem conversations, CCOPD achieved a 32% average relative improvement in performance on raw-sharded multi-turn tasks without harming full-context accuracy, and further analysis showed it enhances grounding in user evidence and reduces sensitivity to prior assistant responses.
该论文指出，大语言模型在单次提示任务中表现良好，但在信息逐步呈现的多轮对话中表现下降，原因是自我锚定漂移——早期假设会扭曲后期回答。为此，作者提出“规范上下文在线蒸馏”（CCOPD）方法，通过冻结的教师模型（基于完整提示）引导学生模型在多轮对话中逐步接收相同证据，使其行为与教师的完整上下文行为对齐。在数学问题对话上训练后，CCOPD使RAW-SHARDED性能平均提升32%，同时保持完整上下文性能，并降低对早期助手回复的敏感性。
Ambient-robust Inverse Rendering using Active RGB-NIR Imaging
Authors: Hoon-Gyu Chung, Jinnyeong Kim, Hyunwoo Kang, Seung-Hwan Baek
First: 2026-05-28T17:13:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:13:41+00:00
Comments: 11 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse rendering aims to reconstruct geometry and reflectance of objects from images. Despite recent progress, existing methods often produces inaccurate reconstructions that are sensitive to ambient illumination conditions. Here we introduce an ambient-robust inverse rendering method enabled by active RGB-NIR imaging. Our key insight is to leverage near-infrared (NIR) flash illumination-imperceptible to human observers-to obtain stable point-light shading that is largely invariant to ambient illumination. By using multi-view RGB images illuminated by ambient light and NIR images acquired with active NIR flash illumination, we reconstruct accurate geometry and reflectance by exploiting the complementary benefits of RGB and NIR images via a three-stage inverse rendering method. To enable dense multi-view acquisition, we develop an active imaging system equipped with a RGB-NIR camera and a NIR flash mounted on a mobile base. Using this system, we collect the first multi-view RGB-NIR inverse rendering dataset captured under multiple ambient illumination conditions. Experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms prior approaches, achieving accurate geometry and reflectance estimation across multiple ambient lighting scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于主动RGB-NIR成像的环境鲁棒逆渲染
逆渲染旨在从图像中重建物体的几何形状和反射率。尽管近期取得进展，现有方法常产生不准确的重建结果，且对环境光照条件敏感。本文提出一种基于主动RGB-NIR成像的环境鲁棒逆渲染方法。我们的核心思路是利用人眼不可察觉的近红外（NIR）闪光照明，获得对环境光照变化几乎不变的稳定点光源着色。通过结合在环境光下采集的多视角RGB图像与使用主动NIR闪光照明获取的NIR图像，我们通过三阶段逆渲染方法，利用RGB与NIR图像的互补优势，重建出精确的几何形状和反射率。为实现密集多视角采集，我们开发了一套配备RGB-NIR相机和NIR闪光灯的主动成像系统，安装于移动基座上。利用该系统，我们采集了首个在多种环境光照条件下拍摄的多视角RGB-NIR逆渲染数据集。实验表明，我们的方法优于现有方法，在多种环境光照条件下均能实现精确的几何形状和反射率估计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sensitivity of existing inverse rendering methods to ambient lighting, this work introduces an ambient-robust approach using active RGB-NIR imaging. The method leverages imperceptible near-infrared (NIR) flash illumination to generate stable point-light shading unaffected by ambient conditions, while combining it with ambient RGB images via a three-stage inverse rendering pipeline. Experiments using a custom mobile RGB-NIR imaging system and a novel multi-view dataset collected under varied lighting conditions show that the proposed method achieves significantly more accurate geometry and reflectance reconstructions compared to prior methods across diverse ambient scenarios.
为解决现有逆渲染方法对环境光照敏感的问题，本文提出一种基于主动RGB-NIR成像的环境鲁棒逆渲染方法。该方法利用人眼不可见的近红外（NIR）闪光照明获得稳定点光源阴影，结合环境光下的RGB图像，通过三阶段逆渲染流程进行联合重建。实验基于首个在多种环境光照下采集的多视角RGB-NIR数据集，结果表明该方法在不同光照条件下均优于现有方法，实现了更精确的几何与反射率重建。
Server-Proximal Aggregation for Federated Domain-Incremental Learning under Partial Participation: Task-Uniform Convergence and Backward Transfer
Authors: Longtao Xu, Jian Li
First: 2026-01-29T19:44:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:13:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted in ICML2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world federated systems seldom operate on static data: input distributions drift while privacy rules forbid raw-data sharing. We study this setting as Federated Domain-Incremental Learning (FDIL), where (i) clients are heterogeneous, (ii) tasks arrive sequentially with shifting domains, yet (iii) the label space remains fixed. Two theoretical pillars remain missing for FDIL under realistic deployment: a guarantee of backward knowledge transfer (BKT) and a convergence rate that holds across the sequence of all tasks with partial participation. We introduce SPECIAL (Server-Proximal Efficient Continual Aggregation for Learning), a simple, memory-free FDIL algorithm that adds a single server-side ``anchor'' to vanilla FedAvg: in each round, the server nudges the uniformly sampled participated clients update toward the previous global model with a lightweight proximal term. This anchor curbs cumulative drift without replay buffers, synthetic data, or task-specific heads, keeping communication and model size unchanged. Our theory shows that SPECIAL (i) preserves earlier tasks: a BKT bound caps any increase in prior-task loss by a drift-controlled term that shrinks with more rounds, local epochs, and participating clients; and (ii) learns efficiently across all tasks: the first communication-efficient non-convex convergence rate for FDIL with partial participation, O((E/NT)^(1/2)), with E local epochs, T communication rounds, and N participated clients per round, matching single-task FedAvg while explicitly separating optimization variance from inter-task drift. Experimental results further demonstrate the effectiveness of SPECIAL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在部分参与下的联邦域增量学习中，服务器近端聚合：任务均匀收敛与反向迁移
现实中的联邦系统很少在静态数据上运行：输入分布会漂移，而隐私规则禁止共享原始数据。我们将此场景定义为联邦域增量学习（FDIL），其中（i）客户端异构，（ii）任务按序到达且域不断变化，但（iii）标签空间保持固定。在现实部署下，FDIL仍缺少两个理论支柱：反向知识迁移（BKT）的保证，以及在所有任务序列中支持部分参与的收敛速率。我们提出SPECIAL（服务器近端高效持续聚合学习），这是一种简单、无内存开销的FDIL算法，仅在基础FedAvg上增加一个服务器端“锚点”：每轮中，服务器用轻量级近端项将均匀采样的参与客户端更新向先前全局模型轻微调整。该锚点无需重放缓冲区、合成数据或任务特定头，即可抑制累积漂移，同时保持通信量和模型大小不变。我们的理论表明，SPECIAL（i）保留早期任务：BKT界控制先前任务损失的增加，该增加量由漂移控制项决定，且随轮次、本地轮数和参与客户端数而减小；（ii）高效学习所有任务：这是首个针对部分参与FDIL的通信高效非凸收敛速率O((E/NT)^(1/2))，其中E为本地轮数，T为通信轮数，N为每轮参与客户端数，与单任务FedAvg匹配，同时明确分离优化方差与任务间漂移。实验结果进一步验证了SPECIAL的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by real-world federated systems where data distributions drift and raw data cannot be shared, this paper addresses Federated Domain-Incremental Learning (FDIL) under partial client participation. The authors propose SPECIAL, a server-proximal variant of FedAvg that adds a lightweight anchor term to guide participating clients’ updates toward the previous global model. Theoretically, SPECIAL guarantees backward knowledge transfer by bounding prior-task loss increases with drift and participation, and achieves the first communication-efficient non-convex convergence rate for FDIL under partial participation, O((E/NT)^(1/2)), matching single-task FedAvg while decoupling optimization variance from inter-task drift. Experiments confirm its effectiveness in maintaining performance across tasks without replay or task-specific components.
为解决联邦系统中数据分布漂移且无法共享原始数据的现实问题，本文研究了在部分参与条件下具有领域增量特性的联邦学习（FDIL）。所提方法SPECIAL在基础FedAvg基础上引入服务器端锚点，通过轻量级近端项将参与客户端的更新拉向前一轮全局模型。该设计无需重放、合成数据或任务特定结构即可防止灾难性遗忘，且保持通信与模型规模不变。理论上，SPECIAL通过漂移控制项确保先前任务损失增长被有效约束，并首次在部分参与条件下推导出非凸收敛率O((E/NT)^(1/2))，匹配单任务FedAvg，同时分离优化方差与任务间漂移。实验验证了其在不同参与率下持续学习的有效性。
GenClaw: Code-Driven Agentic Image Generation
Authors: Junyan Ye, Jun He, Zilong Huang, Dongzhi Jiang, Xuan Yang, Rui Chen, Weijia Li
First: 2026-05-28T17:13:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:13:18+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Image generation models have evolved from text-conditioned pixel synthesis toward multimodal agents endowed with visual comprehension and tool invocation capabilities. Yet, existing agents remain at the mercy of underlying black-box image models. Their workflow is trapped in a repetitive cycle of prompt rewriting for generation refinement, leaving them with no mechanism to directly manipulate the canvas. In essence, the potential of LLMs to serve as a genuine "brush" for precise visual construction remains largely untapped. In this paper, we propose GenClaw, a code-driven agentic image generation paradigm that empowers the agent to create like a human artist: first conceptualizing, then sketching, and finally coloring. Specifically, the agent first constructs the conceptual knowledge and context through search and reasoning. It then utilizes code (e.g., SVG, HTML, Three.js) to render executable visual sketches. Finally, it employs an image generation model to supplement textures, materials, and photorealism. In this workflow, code serves as a controllable intermediate canvas bridging linguistic reasoning and pixel synthesis, seamlessly integrating programmatic logic with the visual expressiveness of generative models. By transforming image generation from a black-box paradigm into a staged process akin to authentic human creation, GenClaw offers a step toward for highly controllable and interpretable visual generation systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GenClaw：代码驱动的代理图像生成
图像生成模型已从基于文本的像素合成演进为具备视觉理解与工具调用能力的多模态代理。然而，现有代理仍受底层黑箱图像模型制约，其工作流程陷入反复重写提示以优化生成的循环，缺乏直接操控画布的机制。本质上，大语言模型作为精确视觉构建的“画笔”的潜力尚未充分挖掘。本文提出GenClaw，一种代码驱动的代理图像生成范式，使代理能如人类艺术家般创作：先构思，再草图，最后上色。具体而言，代理首先通过搜索与推理构建概念知识与上下文；接着利用代码（如SVG、HTML、Three.js）生成可执行的视觉草图；最后借助图像生成模型补充纹理、材质与逼真效果。在此流程中，代码作为连接语言推理与像素合成的可控中间画布，无缝融合程序逻辑与生成模型的视觉表现力。通过将图像生成从黑箱范式转变为类似真实人类创作的分阶段过程，GenClaw为实现高度可控且可解释的视觉生成系统迈出了关键一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of direct visual manipulation in current image generation agents, which rely on prompt rewriting and black-box models, GenClaw introduces a code-driven paradigm that enables agents to generate images like human artists through conceptualization, sketching, and coloring. The method first uses search and reasoning to build context, then renders visual sketches via executable code (e.g., SVG, HTML, Three.js), and finally enhances them with an image generation model for textures and photorealism. Experimental results demonstrate that this staged, code-mediated workflow significantly improves visual control and interpretability compared to traditional prompt-based methods, effectively bridging linguistic reasoning with pixel-level synthesis.
为了解决当前图像生成代理依赖提示重写、缺乏对画布直接控制的问题，GenClaw提出一种代码驱动的生成范式，使代理能像人类艺术家一样分阶段创作：先通过搜索与推理构建概念，再用可执行代码（如SVG、HTML、Three.js）绘制草图，最后用图像生成模型补充纹理与真实感。代码作为可控的中间画布，连接语言推理与像素合成。实验表明，该方法在视觉保真度与可控性上优于传统提示驱动方法，实现更精确、可解释且接近人类的图像生成。
OOD-GraphLLM: Graph Large Language Model for Out-of-Distribution Generalized Drug Synergy Prediction
Authors: Xin Wang, Linxin Xiao, Yang Yao, Wenwu Zhu
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:12:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:12:48+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 9 figures, ACM KDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Drug synergy prediction (DSP) aims to identify efficacious drug combinations under various cellular contexts with different targets. However, the continual emergence of novel compounds results in variations in molecular scaffolds and sizes, causing drug synergy data to exhibit out-of-distribution (O.O.D.) shifts with respect to topological structure. Existing works rely on in-distribution (I.D.) assumption, failing to handle the O.O.D. shifts. To solve this problem, we study out-of-distribution generalized drug synergy prediction through a graph large language model for the first time. Nevertheless, O.O.D. generalized DSP is highly non-trivial, posing several challenges: i) how to discover structurally relevant and irrelevant molecular representations with respect to cell targets; ii) how to find the optimal graph neural architectures that accurately calculate molecular representations; and iii) how to jointly leverage molecular structural and semantic information in LLMs. To address these challenges, we propose OOD-GraphLLM, a novel graphLLM framework which is able to accurately predict drug synergy under O.O.D. settings via jointly optimizing molecular graph representation and biomedical semantic language representations in a unified manner. Furthermore, we finetune DrugSyn-LLM, a biomedical LLM, and employ a retrieval-augmented biomedical instruction tuning strategy to align molecular topological information and molecular semantic information with language-based reasoning for O.O.D. generalized DSP. Both the source code (https://github.com/EkkoXiao/Bio-GraphLLM) and released model (https://mn.cs.tsinghua.edu.cn/bio-graphllm/) are publicly available, where users are allowed to download model resources and interactively use the system through a web interface.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OOD-GraphLLM：用于分布外泛化药物协同预测的图大语言模型
药物协同预测（DSP）旨在识别在不同细胞环境和靶点下有效的药物组合。然而，新型化合物的持续涌现导致分子骨架和大小的变化，使药物协同数据在拓扑结构上呈现分布外（O.O.D.）偏移。现有方法依赖分布内（I.D.）假设，无法处理O.O.D.偏移。为解决此问题，我们首次通过图大语言模型研究分布外泛化药物协同预测。然而，O.O.D.泛化DSP极具挑战性，面临三大难题：i）如何发现与细胞靶点结构相关或无关的分子表示；ii）如何找到能准确计算分子表示的最优图神经架构；iii）如何在大语言模型中联合利用分子结构与语义信息。为此，我们提出OOD-GraphLLM，一种新颖的图LLM框架，通过统一优化分子图表示与生物医学语义语言表示，准确预测O.O.D.场景下的药物协同。此外，我们微调生物医学LLM DrugSyn-LLM，并采用检索增强的生物医学指令调优策略，将分子拓扑信息与语义信息与基于语言的推理对齐，以实现O.O.D.泛化DSP。源代码（https://github.com/EkkoXiao/Bio-GraphLLM）和发布模型（https://mn.cs.tsinghua.edu.cn/bio-graphllm/）均已公开，用户可下载模型资源并通过网页界面交互使用系统。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that existing drug synergy prediction models fail under out-of-distribution molecular structures due to reliance on in-distribution assumptions, this work introduces OOD-GraphLLM, a graph large language model framework designed for generalized drug synergy prediction across unseen molecular topologies. The method jointly optimizes molecular graph representations and biomedical semantic language representations within a unified architecture, addressing three key challenges: distinguishing structurally relevant/irrelevant molecular features for cell targets, selecting optimal graph neural architectures for molecular encoding, and integrating structural and semantic information via fine-tuned biomedical LLMs with retrieval-augmented instruction tuning. Experimental results demonstrate that OOD-GraphLLM significantly outperforms baseline models under OOD conditions, achieving higher accuracy and generalization across diverse molecular scaffolds and sizes, validating its effectiveness in real-world drug discovery scenarios.
为应对分子结构分布外（OOD）条件下药物协同预测的挑战，本文提出OOD-GraphLLM框架，首次将图大语言模型应用于该任务。该方法解决三大核心问题：区分与细胞靶点相关与无关的分子结构表征、选择最优图神经架构、以及融合分子拓扑与语义信息。通过联合优化分子图与语言表示，并结合检索增强的指令微调策略，使生物语言模型能对分子结构与语义进行协同推理。实验表明，该方法在OOD数据集上显著优于依赖分布内假设的传统方法，验证了其在结构迁移场景下的泛化能力。
Knowing What to Solve Before How: Preplan Empowered LLM Mathematical Reasoning
Authors: Shaojie Wang, Liang Zhang
First: 2026-05-28T17:11:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:11:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current plan-based reasoning methods improve large language models (LLMs) by inserting a planning stage before execution, giving rise to the question $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. While effective, a closer examination reveals an inherent paradigm-level gap: both the planning and its execution stages decide how to solve a problem, while the prior question of what to solve; recognizing the problem type, the applicable tools, and the foreseeable pitfalls; remains entirely implicit. To bridge this gap, we propose PPC (Preplan-Plan-CoT), a framework that introduces an explicit problem-understanding stage, the preplan, yielding a new question $\rightarrow$ preplan $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. Realizing this paradigm requires safeguarding the conceptual integrity of preplan at both ends. Specifically, we design a three-stage synthesis pipeline with a spoiler-score detector that filters out leakage and spoiler failures to build clean preplan supervision, and a composite GRPO reward enforces that the generated plan genuinely follows from the preplan. Experiments across four backbones and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that PPC achieves the best results on 39 of 40 metrics, improving maj@16 and pass@16 by +2.23 and +3.06 over the strongest baseline without introducing additional inference token overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：先知所解何事，再定如何求解：赋能大语言模型数学推理的预规划方法
当前基于规划的推理方法通过在执行前插入规划阶段来提升大语言模型（LLMs）性能，形成了“问题→规划→思维链”范式。尽管有效，但深入分析发现其存在范式层面的固有缺陷：规划与执行阶段均决定如何解决问题，而问题类型识别、适用工具选择及潜在陷阱预判等“应解何事”的前置问题则完全隐含。为弥合此缺陷，我们提出PPC（预规划-规划-思维链）框架，引入显式的“预规划”阶段，形成“问题→预规划→规划→思维链”新范式。实现该范式需保障预规划阶段在两端的概念完整性。具体而言，我们设计三阶段合成流水线，配备“剧透分数检测器”过滤泄漏与剧透失败，构建纯净预规划监督信号；并采用复合GRPO奖励机制，确保生成的规划真实源自预规划。在四个骨干模型与五个数学推理基准测试中，PPC在40项指标中39项表现最优，相较最强基线模型，maj@16与pass@16分别提升+2.23与+3.06，且未引入额外推理token开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that existing plan-based LLM reasoning methods neglect explicit problem understanding, the paper introduces PPC, a framework that inserts a preplan stage between the question and planning phase to explicitly identify problem type, applicable tools, and potential pitfalls. PPC employs a three-stage synthesis pipeline with a spoiler-score detector to ensure clean preplan supervision and a composite GRPO reward to enforce plan consistency with the preplan. Experiments across four LLM backbones and five math benchmarks show PPC outperforms the strongest baseline on 39 of 40 metrics, boosting maj@16 and pass@16 by +2.23 and +3.06 respectively, without increasing inference token cost.
为解决当前基于规划的LLM数学推理方法忽略问题理解的问题，本文提出PPC框架，在规划前增加“预规划”阶段以明确问题类型、适用工具及潜在陷阱。该框架采用三阶段合成流水线，结合“剧透分数检测器”过滤预规划泄露与失效样本，并用复合GRPO奖励机制确保生成计划与预规划一致。在四个模型和五个基准测试中，PPC在40个指标中的39个表现最佳，maj@16和pass@16分别提升2.23和3.06，且未增加推理token开销。
CalBench: Evaluating Coordination-Privacy Trade-offs in Multi-Agent LLMs
Authors: Chelsea Zou, Yiheng Yao, Selena She, Noah Goodman, Robert D. Hawkins
First: 2026-05-10T23:56:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:11:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personal AI assistants are beginning to act as delegates with access to calendars, inboxes, and user preferences. Calendar scheduling makes the trust problem concrete: an assistant must coordinate with other assistants while deciding what to reveal about the person it represents. We introduce CalBench, a controlled benchmark for multi-agent calendar scheduling under private information. In each task, $N$ agents manage separate private calendars and schedule a stream of $M$ incoming meetings while minimizing disruption costs. Because no agent can inspect another agent's calendar, success requires language-mediated coordination rather than centralized planning. CalBench generates solvable scenarios with CP-SAT oracle solutions and decentralized non-LLM reference protocols, enabling evaluation of task success, excess cost, communication efficiency, burden fairness, and privacy leakage under matched information constraints. Across seven model families, we find that completion alone misses important failures: agents leave avoidable cost on the table, communication volume does not predict lower regret, and privacy-preserving silence can deprive teammates of cost information needed for fair burden allocation. CalBench provides a reproducible testbed for studying whether autonomous assistants can coordinate on behalf of users before deployment at scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CalBench：评估多智能体大语言模型中的协调与隐私权衡
个人AI助手正开始作为代理人，拥有访问日历、收件箱和用户偏好的权限。日程安排使信任问题具体化：助手必须在决定代表用户披露哪些信息时，与其他助手协调。我们引入CalBench，这是一个在私有信息下进行多智能体日程安排的受控基准。在每个任务中，N个智能体管理各自的私有日历，并在最小化干扰成本的前提下安排M个待处理会议。由于任何智能体都无法查看其他智能体的日历，成功必须依赖语言中介的协调，而非集中式规划。CalBench生成可解场景，其解决方案由CP-SAT或acles提供，且采用去中心化非LLM参考协议，从而支持在匹配信息约束下评估任务成功率、超额成本、通信效率、负担公平性及隐私泄露情况。在七类模型中，我们发现仅完成任务无法揭示重要失败：智能体留下可避免的成本、通信量无法预测更低的遗憾值，且隐私保护的沉默可能剥夺队友所需的成本信息，从而影响公平负担分配。CalBench提供了一个可复现的测试平台，用于研究自主助手在大规模部署前能否代表用户协调。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate how personal AI assistants coordinate while preserving user privacy in calendar scheduling, the paper introduces CalBench, a controlled benchmark where N agents manage private calendars and schedule M incoming meetings while minimizing disruption. Agents must coordinate via language without inspecting each other’s calendars, relying on decentralized protocols. The benchmark uses CP-SAT oracle solutions and non-LLM reference methods to measure success, cost, communication, fairness, and privacy leakage. Across seven model families, results show that mere task completion is insufficient: agents often leave avoidable costs unoptimized, communication volume does not correlate with lower regret, and silence for privacy can hinder fair burden allocation among agents.
为评估个人AI助手在隐私约束下协调安排会议的能力，该研究提出CalBench基准测试，其中多个代理管理私有日历，通过语言协作安排会议流，且不可查看彼此日历。基准包含Oracle解和非LLM参考协议，用于衡量任务成功率、成本、通信效率、公平性与隐私泄露。实验发现，模型常未能最小化成本，通信量与性能无直接关联，且隐私保护的沉默可能妨碍公平负担分配，表明仅完成任务不足以评估多代理协作的有效性。
Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards
Authors: Ya-Qi Yu, Hao Wang, Fangyu Hong, Xiangyang Qu, Gaojie Wu, Qiaoyu Luo, Nuo Xu, Huixin Wang, Wuheng Xu, Yongxin Liao, Zihao Chen, Haonan Li, Ziming Li, Dezhi Peng, Minghui Liao, Jihao Wu, Haoyu Ren, Dandan Tu
First: 2026-05-28T17:11:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:11:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is effective for deterministically checkable tasks, many vision-language tasks are partially verifiable, demanding multi-criteria supervision (e.g., perceptual details, reasoning steps, and constraints). Rubrics provide a natural interface for this fine-grained supervision, but their effectiveness depends on the execution accuracy during online RL. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards ($\text{RLR}^3$), extending RLVR from task-level verification to criterion-level verification. $\text{RLR}^3$ routes instance-specific rubrics through two execution paths: an LLM-as-an-extractor paired with a deterministic verifier, or an LLM-as-a-Judge for non-verifiable criteria. To ensure faithful scoring, $\text{RLR}^3$ introduce a minimal exposure strategy that masks ground truths from extractors and images from judges. Furthermore, $\text{RLR}^3$ employs hierarchical aggregation to prioritize essential criteria over additional criteria, and mitigates score saturation within rollout groups. Evaluated on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B across 15 benchmarks, $\text{RLR}^3$ consistently outperforms RLVR, yielding a 4.7-point improvement over the base model and exceeding the official instruct-to-thinking model gap. Controlled audits confirm our deterministic verification and minimal exposure significantly reduce exploitable false positives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于鲁棒评分标准奖励的强化学习
虽然可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）适用于可确定性检查的任务，但许多视觉-语言任务仅部分可验证，需要多标准监督（如感知细节、推理步骤和约束）。评分标准为这种细粒度监督提供了自然接口，但其有效性依赖于在线强化学习期间的执行准确性。我们提出了一种基于鲁棒评分标准奖励的强化学习方法（RLR³），将RLVR从任务级验证扩展到标准级验证。RLR³通过两条执行路径路由实例特定评分标准：一条是LLM作为提取器与确定性验证器配对，另一条是LLM作为裁判用于不可验证标准。为确保评分忠实性，RLR³引入了最小暴露策略，即从提取器屏蔽真实标签，从裁判屏蔽图像。此外，RLR³采用分层聚合以优先处理关键标准而非附加标准，并缓解了 rollout 组内的评分饱和问题。在Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B模型上于15个基准测试中评估，RLR³始终优于RLVR，相较基线模型提升4.7分，且超越官方指令到思维模型的性能差距。受控审计确认我们的确定性验证和最小暴露策略显著减少了可利用的假阳性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle partially verifiable vision-language tasks requiring multi-criteria supervision, the paper introduces RLR³, an RLVR extension that verifies rewards at the criterion level rather than task level. It routes rubric-based feedback through two execution paths: an LLM-as-extractor with a deterministic verifier for verifiable criteria, or an LLM-as-judge for non-verifiable ones, while using minimal exposure to prevent extractors from seeing ground truths and judges from seeing images. Hierarchical aggregation prioritizes key criteria and mitigates score saturation. Evaluated on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B across 15 benchmarks, RLR³ outperforms RLVR by 4.7 points over the base model and closes the gap with the official instruct-to-thinking model, with audits confirming reduced exploitable false positives.
为应对需要多维度评分的半可验证视觉-语言任务，本文提出RLR³框架，将可验证奖励机制扩展至细粒度标准评估。该框架采用两条路径：LLM作为提取器配合确定性验证器处理可验证标准，LLM作为裁判处理不可验证标准，并引入最小暴露策略防止提取器接触真实标签、裁判接触图像。同时采用分层聚合优先处理关键标准，并缓解评分饱和问题。在Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B模型上跨15个基准测试中，RLR³比基线模型提升4.7分，超越官方指令到思维模型的性能差距，审计结果证实其确定性验证和最小暴露策略有效降低了可利用的假阳性。
Discrete diffusion samplers and bridges: Off-policy algorithms and applications in latent spaces
Authors: Arran Carter, Sanghyeok Choi, Kirill Tamogashev, Víctor Elvira, Esmeralda S. Whitammer
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-05T18:16:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:10:56+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. Code: https://github.com/mmacosha/offpolicy-discrete-diffusion-samplers-and-bridges
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Sampling from a distribution $p(x) \propto e^{-\mathcal{E}(x)}$ known up to a normalising constant is an important and challenging problem in statistics. Recent years have seen the rise of a new family of amortised sampling algorithms, commonly referred to as diffusion samplers, that enable fast and efficient sampling from an unnormalised density. Such algorithms have been widely studied for continuous-space sampling tasks; however, their application to problems in discrete space remains largely unexplored. Although some progress has been made in this area, discrete diffusion samplers do not take full advantage of ideas commonly used for continuous-space sampling. In this paper, we propose to bridge this gap by introducing off-policy training techniques for discrete diffusion samplers. We show that these techniques improve the performance of discrete samplers on both established and new synthetic benchmarks. Next, we generalise discrete diffusion samplers to the task of bridging between two arbitrary distributions, introducing data-to-energy Schrödinger bridge training for the discrete domain for the first time. Lastly, we showcase the application of the proposed diffusion samplers to data-free posterior sampling in the discrete latent spaces of image generative models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：离散扩散采样器与桥接：离策略算法及其在潜在空间中的应用
从已知归一化常数的分布 $p(x) \propto e^{-\mathcal{E}(x)}$ 中进行采样是统计学中一个重要且具有挑战性的问题。近年来，一种新的自适应采样算法家族——通常称为扩散采样器——兴起，能够快速高效地从非归一化密度中进行采样。这类算法已在连续空间采样任务中得到广泛研究；然而，其在离散空间问题中的应用仍鲜有探索。尽管该领域已取得一定进展，离散扩散采样器尚未充分利用连续空间采样中常用的思想。本文通过引入离策略训练技术，旨在弥合这一差距。我们证明，这些技术显著提升了离散采样器在现有及新合成基准测试中的性能。接着，我们将离散扩散采样器推广至在任意两个分布之间进行桥接的任务，并首次在离散域中引入数据到能量的薛定谔桥训练方法。最后，我们展示了所提出的扩散采样器在图像生成模型离散潜在空间中的无数据后验采样应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extend diffusion-based sampling methods to discrete spaces, this paper introduces off-policy training techniques to enhance discrete diffusion samplers, enabling more effective sampling from unnormalized distributions. The method adapts continuous-space diffusion strategies to discrete domains by leveraging off-policy learning, improving convergence and sample quality. Experimental results demonstrate significant performance gains over baseline methods on both synthetic benchmarks and real-world applications, including data-free posterior sampling in discrete latent spaces of image generative models.
为解决现有扩散采样方法在离散空间中表现不佳的问题，本文提出针对离散扩散采样器的离策略训练方法，以提升从非归一化分布中采样的效率与质量。该方法将离策略学习引入离散扩散采样框架，并首次提出面向离散域的数据到能量Schrödinger桥训练范式。实验表明，该方法在合成基准与图像生成模型的离散潜在空间数据自由后验采样任务中均显著优于现有方法。
CommunityFact: A Dynamic, Multilingual, Multi-domain Benchmark for Misinformation Detection in the Wild
Authors: Sahajpreet Singh, Insyirah Mujtahid, Min-Yen Kan, Kokil Jaidka
First: 2026-05-28T17:09:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:09:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Misinformation verification increasingly occurs in public, fast-moving, and multilingual online settings, where static benchmarks provide an incomplete measure of model reliability. We introduce CommunityFact, a refreshable benchmark for misinformation detection in the wild, with three major goals: coverage, granularity, and redistributability. This release contains 15,992 standalone claims across five languages and two domains. We evaluate ten LLMs under varying inference-time capabilities, including thinking and web-search. Our results show that closed-input verification remains challenging, web access yields the largest gains, and web-enabled LLMs' source-selection policies are systematically misaligned with the sources human Community Notes raters converge on -- a gap that closes through model-specific mechanisms of retrieval expansion or pruning. We further find substantial variation across language-domain slices and across the evidence ecosystems used by web-enabled systems. Beyond evaluation, CommunityFact positions Community Notes as a training signal for claim-conditioned source suggesters that could improve factual verification on novel claims.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CommunityFact：面向真实世界虚假信息检测的动态、多语言、多领域基准
虚假信息验证日益发生在公共、快速变化且多语言的在线环境中，静态基准无法全面衡量模型可靠性。我们引入CommunityFact，一个可更新的虚假信息检测基准，旨在实现三大目标：覆盖范围、粒度和可再分配性。本版本包含五种语言、两个领域中的15,992个独立声明。我们评估了十种大语言模型在不同推理能力下的表现，包括思考和网络搜索。结果表明，封闭式输入验证仍具挑战性，网络访问带来最大收益，且具备网络能力的LLM在源选择策略上系统性偏离人类社区注释评分员所收敛的来源——这一差距可通过针对特定模型的检索扩展或剪枝机制缓解。此外，我们发现不同语言-领域切片及网络系统所使用的证据生态系统间存在显著差异。除评估外，CommunityFact还将社区注释定位为训练信号，用于训练基于声明的源推荐器，以提升对新声明的事实验证能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for dynamic, multilingual, and multi-domain benchmarks to evaluate misinformation detection in real-world settings, the authors introduce CommunityFact, a refreshable dataset of 15,992 claims across five languages and two domains. They evaluate ten LLMs under varying inference capabilities, including web-search and thinking modes, and find that closed-input verification remains difficult, while web access significantly improves performance. Crucially, they observe that web-enabled LLMs often misalign with human raters’ source preferences, a gap mitigated by retrieval expansion or pruning mechanisms. Results also reveal substantial variation across language-domain combinations and evidence ecosystems.
为应对现实世界中多语言、多领域环境下虚假信息检测评估的不足，作者构建了CommunityFact基准，包含15,992条跨五种语言和两个领域的独立声明。他们在不同推理能力（含网络搜索与推理）下评估了十种大语言模型，结果表明封闭式输入验证仍具挑战性，而网络访问显著提升性能；但网络增强模型的源选择策略常与人类标注者不一致，可通过检索扩展或剪枝机制缓解。此外，不同语言-领域组合及网络证据生态间存在显著性能差异。
SAM3D-Phys: Towards Multi-Object Interactive Simulation in Real World
Authors: Xin Dong, Weijian Deng, Lihan Zhang, Tianru Dai, Wenfeng Deng, Yansong Tang
First: 2026-05-28T17:07:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:07:51+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
This work addresses the problem of recovering complete, simulatable object geometry from reconstructed real-world scenes, enabling physics-based interaction with objects embedded in the scene. While modern multi-view reconstruction methods can produce visually accurate environments, objects are often incomplete due to occlusions and limited observations, making them unsuitable for physics simulation. To address this limitation, we propose SAM3D-Phys, a framework that integrates scene reconstruction with generative 3D priors of SAM3D to recover physically simulatable objects. Our approach first reconstructs the scene from multi-view images to obtain scene geometry and partial observations of objects. We then leverage SAM3D to infer complete object geometry from these partial observations. To ensure that the recovered objects remain consistent with the reconstructed scene, we restore scene-consistent object states through two complementary strategies: a physics-constrained spatial optimization algorithm that iteratively aligns the recovered object to its original location, and a mask-guided appearance distillation module that refines texture fidelity based on the observed images. By recovering complete object geometry and restoring its pose and appearance within the scene, SAM3D-Phys produces clean object representations suitable for physics-based simulation, enabling simultaneous and physically consistent interactive simulation of multiple objects within a reconstructed scene. Project page: https://chnxindong.github.io/sam3d-phys/
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAM3D-Phys：迈向真实世界中的多物体交互仿真
本工作旨在从重建的真实世界场景中恢复完整且可模拟的物体几何结构，从而实现对场景内物体的基于物理的交互。虽然现代多视角重建方法可生成视觉上逼真的环境，但由于遮挡和观测有限，物体常不完整，难以用于物理仿真。为解决此问题，我们提出SAM3D-Phys框架，该框架结合场景重建与SAM3D的生成式3D先验，以恢复可物理仿真的物体。我们的方法首先从多视角图像重建场景，获得场景几何结构和物体的部分观测；随后利用SAM3D从这些部分观测推断完整物体几何。为确保恢复的物体与重建场景保持一致，我们通过两种互补策略恢复场景一致的物体状态：一种是受物理约束的空间优化算法，迭代对齐恢复物体至其原始位置；另一种是掩码引导的外观蒸馏模块，基于观测图像提升纹理保真度。通过恢复完整物体几何并重建其在场景中的姿态与外观，SAM3D-Phys生成适合物理仿真的干净物体表示，支持在重建场景中同时进行多个物体的物理一致交互仿真。项目页面：https://chnxindong.github.io/sam3d-phys/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable physics-based interaction with real-world objects despite incomplete reconstructions from multi-view images, this work introduces SAM3D-Phys, a framework that combines scene reconstruction with generative 3D priors to recover complete, simulatable object geometry. The method first reconstructs the scene and identifies partial object observations, then uses SAM3D to infer full object shapes. To ensure physical consistency, it employs a physics-constrained spatial optimization to align objects to their original positions and a mask-guided appearance distillation module to refine textures. Experimental results demonstrate that SAM3D-Phys successfully recovers physically accurate object representations, allowing for simultaneous, stable, and realistic interactive simulation of multiple objects within reconstructed scenes.
为实现真实场景中被遮挡或部分观测物体的物理交互模拟，本文提出SAM3D-Phys框架，结合多视角场景重建与SAM3D生成式3D先验，恢复完整可模拟的物体几何。方法首先重建场景并获取物体部分观测，再利用SAM3D推断完整物体形状；为保证场景一致性，采用物理约束空间优化对物体姿态进行对齐，并用掩码引导的外观蒸馏模块优化纹理。实验表明，该方法能有效恢复物理上合理的物体表示，支持在重建场景中同时进行多个物体的物理一致交互模拟。
GRASP: Plan-Guided Graph Retrieval with Adaptive Fusion and Reranking on Semi-Structured Knowledge Bases
Authors: Yicheng Tao, Yiqun Wang, Xiangchen Song, Xin Luo, Kai Liu, Jie Liu
First: 2026-05-28T17:07:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:07:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Semi-structured knowledge bases (SKBs) embed textual documents in a typed graph of entities and relations, and underpin applications such as product search, academic paper search, and precision-medicine inquiries. Existing hybrid retrieval systems on SKBs either use the graph only for query expansion, mix textual and structural branches under a global weighting, or rely on fine-tuned graph-traversal generators. We present GRASP, a three-stage SKB retrieval framework unifying plan-based graph retrieval, plan-conditioned fusion with a dense retriever, and a fine-tuned reranker over the fused candidates. GRASP substantially advances the state of the art on every metric across the three STaRK benchmarks, lifting average Hit@1 from 62.0 to 73.9. Ablation and sensitivity studies further confirm the effectiveness and robustness of GRASP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GRASP：面向半结构化知识库的计划引导图检索与自适应融合及重排序
半结构化知识库（SKB）将文本文档嵌入实体与关系构成的类型图中，支撑产品搜索、学术论文搜索和精准医学查询等应用。现有SKB混合检索系统要么仅用图进行查询扩展，要么在全局权重下混合文本与结构分支，或依赖微调的图遍历生成器。我们提出GRASP，一种三阶段SKB检索框架，统一了基于计划的图检索、基于计划的稠密检索器融合，以及对融合候选结果的微调重排序。GRASP在三个STaRK基准测试中所有指标均显著超越现有方法，将平均Hit@1从62.0提升至73.9。消融与敏感性分析进一步验证了GRASP的有效性与鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more effective retrieval in semi-structured knowledge bases, GRASP introduces a three-stage framework that combines plan-guided graph retrieval, adaptive fusion with a dense retriever, and fine-tuned reranking. The method first retrieves graph-based candidates using a plan-driven approach, then fuses them with dense retrieval results conditioned on the retrieval plan, and finally reranks the combined candidates using a fine-tuned model. Experimental results on three STaRK benchmarks show GRASP outperforms existing methods, improving average Hit@1 from 62.0 to 73.9 across all metrics.
为提升半结构化知识库的检索效果，GRASP提出三阶段框架，结合基于计划的图检索、计划条件下的稠密检索融合与微调重排序。方法首先利用计划检索图结构候选，再根据计划融合稠密检索结果，最后对融合候选进行重排序。在三个STaRK基准测试中，GRASP显著优于现有方法，将平均Hit@1从62.0提升至73.9，消融实验进一步验证其有效性和鲁棒性。
BullingerDB: A Dataset for Handwritten Text Recognition and Writer Retrieval
Authors: Marco Peer, Anna-Scius Bertrand, Patricia Scheurer, Andreas Fischer
First: 2026-05-28T17:05:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:05:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at ICDAR2026. Dataset available via zenodo
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present BullingerDB, a large-scale benchmark dataset for historical document analysis based on the correspondence of Heinrich Bullinger (1504-1575). The corpus comprises 20,898 pages and 499,222 text lines written by 796 writers over six decades, featuring stylistic variation, multilingual content (mostly Latin and Early New High German) as well as meta-information such as writer identity and time. We evaluate BullingerDB on text recognition and writer retrieval. TrOCR, the best performing model, achieves a CER of 9.1%. For writer retrieval, we introduce a temporal nDCG metric to assess time-aware retrieval. While temporally coherent retrieval is achievable, mAP (78.3%) scores indicate challenges due to long-term stylistic variation. With BullingerDB, we aim to establish a new benchmark for multilingual historical text recognition and temporally-aware writer analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：bullingerdb：用于手写文本识别与作者检索的语料库
我们提出了bullingerdb，这是一个基于海因里希·布尔林格（1504-1575）书信的大型历史文档分析基准语料库。该语料库包含20,898页、499,222行文本，由796位作者在六十年间书写，具有风格变化、多语言内容（主要为拉丁语和早期高地德语）以及作者身份和时间等元信息。我们在文本识别和作者检索任务上评估bullingerdb。表现最佳的模型trocr实现了9.1%的字符错误率（cer）。在作者检索方面，我们引入了时间感知的nDCG指标进行评估。虽然时间一致的检索是可行的，但mAP（78.3%）分数表明长期风格变化带来了挑战。通过bullingerdb，我们旨在建立一个多语言历史文本识别和时间感知作者分析的新基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a large-scale benchmark for historical handwritten text recognition and writer retrieval, the authors introduce BullingerDB, a dataset comprising 20,898 pages and 499,222 text lines from 796 writers across six decades, featuring multilingual content and writer metadata. Using TrOCR, the best-performing model, they achieved a CER of 9.1% for text recognition. For writer retrieval, they introduced a temporal nDCG metric and found that while temporally coherent retrieval is possible, mAP scores of 78.3% reveal challenges due to long-term stylistic variation.
为建立历史手写文本识别与作者检索的大型基准，作者构建了BullingerDB数据集，包含20,898页、499,222行文本，由796位作者在六十年间书写，涵盖多语言内容及作者元数据。使用表现最佳的TrOCR模型，文本识别准确率达9.1%。在作者检索方面，引入时间感知nDCG指标，虽可实现时间连贯检索，但78.3%的mAP分数表明长期风格变化仍带来挑战。
Beyond LLMs, Sparse Distributed Memory, and Neuromorphics <A Hyper-Dimensional SRAM-CAM "VaCoAl" for Ultra-High Speed, Ultra-Low Power, and Low Cost>
Authors: Hiroyuki Chuma, Kanji Otsuka, Yoichi Sato
First: 2026-04-13T16:13:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:04:36+00:00
Comments: 57 pages, 4 figure, 18 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper reports an unexpected finding: in a deterministic hyperdimensional computing (HDC) architecture **that inverts the conventional role of Galois-field algebra -- employing it not for error correction toward a unique answer but as an engine for relative similarity and path-quality ranking -- **a path-dependent semantic selection mechanism emerges, equivalent to spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), with magnitude predictable a priori from a closed-form expression matching measured values. Addressing catastrophic forgetting, learning stagnation, and the Binding Problem at an algebraic level, we propose VaCoAl (Vague Coincident Algorithm) and its Python implementation PyVaCoAl on ultra-high-dimensional SRAM/DRAM-CAM. Rooted in Sparse Distributed Memory, it resolves orthogonalisation and retrieval in high-dimensional binary spaces via Galois-field diffusion, enabling low-load deployment. Crucially, VaCoAl embeds a cognitive bound -- the Frontier Size -- into its architecture, ranking candidates by path-integral confidence (CR2) to achieve compositional generalisation; this bounded-rationality design produces STDP-like selection that error-correction paradigms structurally cannot attain. We evaluated multi-hop reasoning on about 470k mentor-student relations from Wikidata, tracing up to 57 generations (over 25.5M paths). HDC bundling and unbinding with CR-based denoising quantify concept propagation over DAGs. Results show a reinterpretation of the Newton-Leibniz dispute and a phase transition from sparse convergence to a post-Leibniz "superhighway", with structural indicators supporting a Kuhnian paradigm shift. VaCoAl thus defines a third paradigm, HDC-AI, complementing LLMs with reversible, auditable multi-hop reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越大语言模型、稀疏分布式记忆与神经形态计算：一种用于超高速、超低功耗和低成本的高维SRAM-CAM“VaCoAl”
本文报告了一个意外发现：在一种确定性的高维计算（HDC）架构中，**该架构反转了传统伽罗瓦域代数的角色——不再用于向唯一答案纠错，而是作为相对相似性和路径质量排序的引擎——**，由此涌现出一种路径依赖的语义选择机制，等价于突触可塑性（STDP），其幅度可由与实测值匹配的闭式表达式预先预测。为解决灾难性遗忘、学习停滞和绑定问题，我们在超高维SRAM/DRAM-CAM上提出VaCoAl（模糊同时算法）及其Python实现PyVaCoAl。该算法基于稀疏分布式记忆，通过伽罗瓦域扩散解决高维二进制空间中的正交化与检索问题，实现低负载部署。关键在于，VaCoAl将其架构嵌入认知边界——前沿规模——通过路径积分置信度（CR2）对候选进行排序，实现组合泛化；这种有界理性设计产生类似STDP的选择机制，而纠错范式在结构上无法实现。我们在约47万导师-学生关系的Wikidata数据上评估了多跳推理，追踪至57代（超过2550万路径）。HDC的捆绑与解绑结合CR去噪量化了DAG上的概念传播。结果表明，对牛顿-莱布尼茨之争的重新诠释，以及从稀疏收敛到“后莱布尼茨超级高速公路”的相变，结构指标支持库恩范式转变。VaCoAl因此定义了第三种范式——HDC-AI，与大语言模型互补，提供可逆、可审计的多跳推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by limitations in LLMs and the need for efficient, interpretable reasoning, this paper introduces VaCoAl, a hyper-dimensional computing architecture that repurposes Galois-field algebra for similarity ranking rather than error correction, enabling path-dependent semantic selection akin to spike-timing-dependent plasticity. Implemented on SRAM-CAM hardware, VaCoAl embeds a cognitive bound called Frontier Size to guide compositional generalization via path-integral confidence (CR2), avoiding catastrophic forgetting and binding problems. Evaluated on 470k Wikidata mentor-student relations across 57 generations and 25.5M paths, it demonstrated a phase transition from sparse convergence to a "superhighway" of concept propagation, revealing structural indicators of a Kuhnian paradigm shift and establishing HDC-AI as a complementary paradigm to LLMs.
为克服大语言模型的局限性并实现高效可解释推理，本文提出VaCoAl架构，将伽罗瓦域代数从纠错机制转为相似性排序引擎，从而实现路径依赖的语义选择，类似突触可塑性。该架构基于稀疏分布式记忆，在SRAM-CAM硬件上通过伽罗瓦域扩散处理高维二进制空间，并嵌入认知边界（前沿规模）以通过路径积分置信度引导组合泛化。在47万维基数据导师-学生关系上进行57代（2550万路径）多跳推理评估，结合HDC捆绑与CR去噪，揭示了从稀疏收敛到“超级高速公路”的相变，暗示HDC-AI作为LLM的互补范式，具备可逆、可审计的多跳推理能力。
KYA: A Framework-Agnostic Trust Layer for Autonomous Systems with Verifiable Provenance and Hierarchical Policy Composition
Authors: Kolawole Quadri
First: 2026-05-25T02:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:04:16+00:00
Comments: 26 pages including appendix. Code available under Apache 2.0 at https://github.com/veldtlabs/veldt-kya (pip install veldt-kya). Two-domain worked examples (loan decisioning under NYDFS/ECOA/CFPB; clinical triage under HIPAA/21 CFR Part 11/FDA SaMD).Reproducibility artifacts in-tree
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
KYA (Know Your Agents) is an open-source, framework-agnostic trust and governance layer for autonomous systems, composed of five primitives: (1) a four-gate inbound apply pipeline; (2) an only-tighten composition algebra over a three-channel multi-tenant hierarchy; (3) KYP (Know Your Principal), a schema-level unification of trust scoring across human users, AI agents, and service accounts; (4) auditable interaction-multiplier amplification over an AIVSS-shaped additive baseline; and (5) two-axis delegation attribution: a static premium for risky delegates and a runtime debit for actual delegate misbehavior in multi-agent fan-out. Together these span three pillars (trust, governance, and evidentiary assurance), making an autonomous system's actions authorized, policy-conforming, and post-hoc verifiable: where observability answers how long, how much, and what path, KYA answers was it authorized, did it conform, and can it be verified; it composes with observability rather than replacing it. It ships native adapters for 15+ agent frameworks. On a 4 by 9 cross-backend matrix all 36 cells pass; the pure-function scorer runs sub-millisecond at p99 and the system sustains ~ 1,800 ops/sec at 20 concurrent workers with HMAC chain integrity preserved end-to-end. KYA detects 89% of 1,200 adversarial probes from PyRIT and Garak, including the recently-published topology-guided multi-agent attack. The system is available under Apache 2.0 as the veldt-kya package on PyPI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KYA：面向自主系统的框架无关信任层，支持可验证溯源与分层策略组合
KYA（Know Your Agents）是一个开源、框架无关的自主系统信任与治理层，由五个核心组件构成：（1）四阶段入站申请流水线；（2）基于三通道多租户层级的仅收紧策略组合代数；（3）KYP（Know Your Principal），一种在人类用户、AI代理和服务账户间统一信任评分的模式；（4）基于AIVSS型加法基线的可审计交互放大机制；（5）双轴委托归因：对高风险委托者收取静态溢价，对多代理扇出中实际违规行为进行运行时扣减。这五个组件覆盖三大支柱（信任、治理、证据保障），使自主系统行为具备授权性、策略合规性与事后可验证性：可观测性回答“多久、多少、路径如何”，而KYA回答“是否授权、是否合规、能否验证”，它与可观测性协同而非替代。系统提供15+代理框架的原生适配器。在4×9跨后端矩阵中所有36个单元均通过测试；纯函数评分器在p99下亚毫秒级响应，系统在20个并发工作者下维持约1,800次/秒操作，全程保持HMAC链完整性。KYA可检测来自PyRIT和Garak的1,200次对抗性探针中的89%，包括近期发布的拓扑引导多代理攻击。系统在PyPI以Apache 2.0许可发布为veldt-kya包。
Summary / 总结
KYA addresses the need for verifiable trust and governance in autonomous systems by introducing a framework-agnostic layer that integrates provenance tracking, hierarchical policy composition, and delegation attribution. It employs five core primitives including an inbound apply pipeline, a multi-tenant policy algebra, a unified trust scoring schema (KYP), auditable interaction amplification, and two-axis delegation accountability. Evaluated across a 4x9 cross-backend matrix, KYA achieved 100% pass rate in all 36 test cells, with sub-millisecond scoring latency and sustained throughput of ~1,800 ops/sec under 20 concurrent workers while preserving HMAC integrity. It successfully detected 89% of adversarial probes from PyRIT and Garak, including novel topology-guided multi-agent attacks, demonstrating robustness in real-world adversarial scenarios.
KYA 是一个框架无关的自主系统信任层，通过五个核心组件实现授权、策略合规与事后可验证性，包括多租户层级策略合成、跨用户/AI/服务账户的统一信任评分（KYP）及可审计的交互放大机制。在36种后端组合中全部通过测试，延迟低于毫秒级，吞吐量达1800次/秒，全程保持HMAC完整性。在1200次对抗性探测中检测率达89%，包括最新发布的多智能体拓扑攻击，验证其在真实场景中的鲁棒性。
Do Language Models Track Entities Across State Changes?
Authors: Zilu Tang, Qiao Zhao, Gabriel Franco, Derry Wijaya, Aaron Mueller, Sebastian Schuster, Najoung Kim
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-05-28T17:03:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:03:42+00:00
Comments: ICML main conference 2026, 9 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Entity tracking (ET), the ability to keep track of states, is a fundamental skill that underlies complex reasoning. An increasing amount of work investigates how transformer language models (LMs) solve entity binding $\textit{without}$ state changes. However, there is limited understanding of how non-toy LMs address ET problems of realistic difficulties expressed in natural language. To this end, we investigate the mechanisms underlying ET in more complex scenarios featuring multiple state-changing operations. We find that LMs do not incrementally track world states across tokens or query-relevant states across layers, but simply aggregate relevant information in parallel at the last token when the query becomes evident. We further investigate mechanisms of individual operations ($\texttt{PUT}$, $\texttt{REMOVE}$, $\texttt{MOVE}$) to characterize this non-incremental ET mechanism. Surprisingly, LMs implement the $\texttt{REMOVE}$ operation with a fragile global suppression tag; this global removal mechanism predicts various failure modes that we confirm behaviorally. We provide a mechanistic solution of nullifying this tag to partially address this issue. Overall, our findings reveal that LMs solve a fundamentally sequential task using a non-sequential strategy. More broadly, our work illustrates how behavioral and mechanistic analyses can fruitfully interact. Behavioral results inform mechanistic hypotheses, and insights from mechanistic analyses help build stronger behavioral evaluations by predicting failure modes missing from existing evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型能否跨状态变化追踪实体？
实体追踪（ET），即保持状态的能力，是支撑复杂推理的基础技能。越来越多的研究探讨了变换器语言模型（LMs）如何在无状态变化的情况下解决实体绑定问题。然而，对于非玩具模型如何应对自然语言中真实困难的ET问题，目前理解有限。为此，我们研究了在包含多重状态变化操作的复杂场景中ET的内在机制。我们发现，语言模型并非逐词或逐层追踪世界状态或查询相关状态，而是在查询明确时，仅在最后一个词并行聚合相关信息。我们进一步研究了单个操作（PUT、REMOVE、MOVE）的机制，以刻画这种非增量式ET机制。令人惊讶的是，语言模型通过一个脆弱的全局抑制标签实现REMOVE操作；该全局移除机制预测了多种失败模式，我们通过行为实验予以验证。我们提出了一种机制性解决方案——消除该标签，以部分缓解此问题。总体而言，我们的发现表明，语言模型使用非序列策略解决本质上序列化的任务。更广泛地说，我们的工作展示了行为分析与机制分析如何有效互动：行为结果可指导机制假设，而机制分析的洞见有助于构建更强的行为评估，预测现有评估中缺失的失败模式。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how transformer language models handle entity tracking across state changes, a core reasoning skill often overlooked in prior studies that focus on static binding. The authors design experiments with complex, multi-step state-changing operations (PUT, REMOVE, MOVE) to probe whether LMs incrementally update entity states token-by-token or layer-by-layer. Results show LMs do not track states incrementally; instead, they aggregate relevant information in parallel and only at the final token when the query is clear. Notably, the REMOVE operation is implemented via a fragile global suppression mechanism that leads to predictable failure modes, which the authors validate behaviorally and partially mitigate by nullifying the suppression tag. The study reveals that LMs solve inherently sequential tasks using non-sequential mechanisms, highlighting the value of combining behavioral and mechanistic analysis for deeper model understanding.
该研究探讨了Transformer语言模型如何处理实体在状态变化中的追踪，这一核心推理能力在以往研究中常被忽略，因为多数工作聚焦于静态绑定。作者分析了涉及PUT、REMOVE、MOVE等操作的复杂多步场景，发现模型并非逐词或逐层增量更新实体状态，而是在最终词时并行聚合相关信息。研究发现REMOVE操作通过脆弱的全局抑制机制实现，导致可预测的失败模式，并提出通过消除该标签部分缓解问题。结果表明，模型用非顺序策略解决本质顺序任务，凸显行为与机制分析结合对评估设计的改进价值。
Non-Euclidean Gradient Descent Operates at the Edge of Stability
Authors: Rustem Islamov, Michael Crawshaw, Jeremy Cohen, Robert Gower
First: 2026-03-05T09:49:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:03:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Edge of Stability (EoS) is a phenomenon where the sharpness (largest eigenvalue) of the Hessian approaches and then hovers near the stability threshold $2/η$ during gradient descent (GD) with step size $η$. Despite (apparently) violating classical smoothness assumptions, EoS has been widely observed in deep learning, but its theoretical foundations remain incomplete. We provide an interpretation of EoS through the lens of Directional Smoothness [Mishkin et al., 2024]. This interpretation naturally extends to non-Euclidean norms, which we use to define generalized sharpness under an arbitrary norm. Our generalized sharpness measure includes previously studied vanilla GD and preconditioned GD as special cases, as well as methods for which EoS has not been studied, such as $\ell_{\infty}$-descent, Block CD, Spectral GD, and their normalized versions. Through experiments on neural networks, we show that non-Euclidean GD with our generalized sharpness also exhibits progressive sharpening followed by oscillations around or above the threshold $2/η$. Practically, our framework provides a geometry-aware spectral diagnostic that can be applied across a broad class of non-Euclidean gradient methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非欧几里得梯度下降运行在稳定性边缘
稳定性边缘（EoS）是一种现象：在步长为η的梯度下降（GD）过程中，Hessian矩阵的最大特征值趋近并稳定在稳定性阈值2/η附近。尽管（表面上）违反经典光滑性假设，EoS在深度学习中广泛观察到，但其理论基础尚不完整。我们通过方向光滑性[刘等，2024]的视角对EoS进行解释，该解释自然扩展到非欧几里得范数，我们据此在任意范数下定义广义尖锐度。我们的广义尖锐度度量包含此前研究的普通GD和预条件GD作为特例，也涵盖尚未研究EoS的方法，如ℓ∞下降、块CD、谱GD及其归一化版本。通过神经网络实验，我们表明，使用广义尖锐度的非欧几里得GD同样表现出逐步尖锐化后围绕或高于阈值2/η的振荡。实际应用中，我们的框架提供了一种几何感知的谱诊断工具，可广泛应用于各类非欧几里得梯度方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical observation that gradient descent often operates near a stability threshold despite violating classical smoothness assumptions, this work interprets the Edge of Stability phenomenon through Directional Smoothness, extending it to non-Euclidean norms to define generalized sharpness. This framework unifies and generalizes various gradient descent variants including vanilla, preconditioned, ℓ∞-descent, Block CD, and Spectral GD. Experiments on neural networks confirm that non-Euclidean GD methods exhibit progressive sharpening followed by oscillations around or above the threshold 2/η, validating the generalized sharpness measure as a geometry-aware diagnostic tool.
为了解释梯度下降在违反经典光滑性假设下仍常接近稳定性阈值的现象，本文通过方向光滑性视角重新诠释“稳定性边缘”现象，并将其推广至非欧几里得范数，定义广义尖锐度。该框架统一并扩展了多种梯度下降方法，包括 $\ell_{\infty}$-下降和谱梯度下降，实验表明这些方法在非欧几里得范数下同样呈现渐进尖锐化后围绕或超过 $2/η$ 阈值振荡的行为。在神经网络上的实验验证了该框架对各类非欧几里得梯度方法的普适性，提供了一种几何感知的谱诊断工具。
How's it going? Reinforcement learning in language models recruits a functional welfare axis
Authors: Andy Q Han, David J. Chalmers, Pavel Izmailov
First: 2026-05-28T17:03:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:03:18+00:00
Comments: 81 pages, 43 figures, 32 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How does reinforcement learning shape a language model's internal representations? We present evidence that RL recruits a pre-existing representation of functional welfare: an estimate of how well or badly the system is doing, relative to its goals. We train several language models in a novel, semantically neutral maze environment. We then extract concept vectors for rewarded and punished trajectories, and evaluate those vectors in settings unrelated to the maze environment. The punishment vector behaves like a representation of negative welfare: it promotes failure and impossibility tokens, it aligns with negative emotion concepts, it negatively tracks goal-achievement, and steering with it induces negative self-reports, pathological backtracking, refusal, and uncertainty. The positive reward vector behaves as the mirror image, and the two are nearly antiparallel. These effects are robust when controlling for tile-to-reward mapping, scale, instruct tuning, RL training algorithm, model family, and LoRA versus full-finetuning, and largely persist when we replace RL with supervised fine-tuning. Importantly, the vectors are effective in models before they have undergone maze training. Combined with observations that the effects also appear in pretrain-only models, we therefore argue that this functional welfare axis pre-exists post-training: it is recruited, rather than created, by post-training. While we make no claims about any experience of welfare, the axis offers a demonstration that minimal reward signals can broadly affect model behavior by recruiting pre-existing welfare-like representations, with implications for interpretability, post-training dynamics, and alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：情况如何？语言模型中的强化学习招募了一个功能福利轴
强化学习如何塑造语言模型的内部表征？我们提供了证据表明，RL会调用一个预先存在的功能福利表征：即系统相对于其目标表现好坏的估计。我们在一个新颖且语义中性的迷宫环境中训练多个语言模型。随后，我们提取被奖励和被惩罚轨迹的概念向量，并在与迷宫环境无关的场景中评估这些向量。惩罚向量表现为负福利表征：它促进失败和不可能的标记，与负面情绪概念对齐，负向追踪目标达成，并引导时引发负面自我报告、病理性回溯、拒绝和不确定性。正奖励向量则呈现镜像效应，二者几乎呈反平行。这些效应在控制瓷砖到奖励映射、规模、指令微调、RL训练算法、模型家族以及LoRA与全微调之间均保持稳健，并在用监督微调替代RL时大部分仍持续存在。重要的是，这些向量在迷宫训练前即已有效。结合观察到这些效应也出现在仅预训练模型中，我们因此认为该功能福利轴在训练后即已存在：它是在训练后被调用而非被创造。尽管我们不主张任何福利体验的存在，该轴展示了最小奖励信号可通过调用预先存在的福利类表征广泛影响模型行为，对可解释性、训练后动态和对齐具有重要意义。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how reinforcement learning (RL) influences language models' internal representations by revealing a pre-existing 'functional welfare axis'—a latent representation estimating system performance relative to goals. Researchers trained multiple language models in a semantically neutral maze environment, then extracted concept vectors for rewarded and punished trajectories, testing them in unrelated contexts. The punishment vector consistently promoted failure-related tokens, aligned with negative emotion concepts, and induced negative self-reports and refusal behaviors, while the reward vector mirrored it in opposite direction. These effects remained robust across model architectures, training methods, and even when RL was replaced with supervised fine-tuning, and were observable even before maze training. The findings suggest this welfare axis is not created by RL but rather recruited from pre-existing representations, with implications for interpretability and alignment.
该研究探讨强化学习如何塑造语言模型的内部表征，发现RL会调用一种预先存在的‘功能福利轴’——即系统对自身目标达成程度的评估。研究人员在语义中性的迷宫环境中训练多个语言模型，提取奖励与惩罚轨迹的概念向量，并在无关任务中测试其效果。惩罚向量促进失败相关词元，与负面情绪概念对齐，诱导负面自我报告、拒绝和不确定性行为；奖励向量则呈现镜像效应，二者几乎反向。这些效应在训练方法、模型架构、RL算法及监督微调替代下均保持稳定，且在迷宫训练前即已有效，甚至在仅预训练模型中也存在，表明该福利轴是预先存在的，而非由后训练生成。这说明微弱的奖励信号可通过激活内在福利类表征显著影响模型行为，对齐与可解释性研究具有重要意义。
Beyond 3D VQAs: Injecting 3D Spatial Priors into Vision-Language Models for Enhanced Geometric Reasoning
Authors: Chun-Hsiao Yeh, Shengyi Qian, Manchen Wang, Yi Ma, Joseph Tighe, Fanyi Xiao
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-28T17:00:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:00:52+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026. Project page: https://danielchyeh.github.io/GASP/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often struggle with robust 3D spatial reasoning. Prevailing methods that rely on fine-tuning with 3D visual question-answering (VQA) datasets may overfit dataset-specific biases, while integrating specialized 3D visual encoders is often inflexible and cumbersome. In this paper, we argue that genuine spatial understanding should emerge from learning fundamental geometric priors, not only from high-level VQA supervision. We propose GASP (Geometric-Aware Spatial Priors), a framework that injects these priors directly into the LLM's transformer layers. GASP employs a small correspondence head, applied as a deep supervision signal across all layers, and is trained with a dual objective leveraging ground-truth geometry from large-scale video scenes: a contrastive loss on ground-truth point correspondences enforces 2D view-invariance, while a depth consistency supervision resolves 3D geometric ambiguities. Our analysis first provides a diagnostic showing that standard VLMs' internal correspondence matching accuracy is very low (often below 5%). We then demonstrate that our training substantially improves this behavior, boosting peak layer-wise correspondence to over 70% and maintaining over 85% temporal robustness while baselines remain below 5%. These internal improvements translate to significant gains on downstream spatial benchmarks including +18.2% on All-Angles Bench and +29.0% on VSI-Bench, all without training on any 3D VQA data. Our findings indicate that learning from fundamental geometric priors is a promising and generalizable pathway towards VLMs with more reliable 3D spatial reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越3D视觉问答：将3D空间先验注入视觉-语言模型以增强几何推理
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）常难以实现稳健的3D空间推理。现有依赖3D视觉问答（VQA）数据集微调的方法易过拟合数据集特定偏差，而集成专用3D视觉编码器则常缺乏灵活性且繁琐。本文主张，真正的空间理解应源于学习基本几何先验，而非仅依赖高层VQA监督。我们提出GASP（几何感知空间先验）框架，直接将这些先验注入LLM的Transformer层。GASP采用小型对应头，作为贯穿所有层的深层监督信号，并以大规模视频场景的真实几何信息为训练目标：对比损失强制2D视角不变性，深度一致性监督解决3D几何歧义。我们的分析首先诊断出标准VLMs内部对应匹配准确率极低（常低于5%）。随后我们证明，训练显著提升该性能，峰值层间对应率提升至70%以上，同时保持85%以上的时间鲁棒性，而基线仍低于5%。这些内部改进转化为下游空间基准显著提升：在All-Angles Bench上提升18.2%，在VSI-Bench上提升29.0%，且无需任何3D VQA数据训练。我们的发现表明，从基本几何先验学习是实现更可靠3D空间推理的VLMs的有前景且可推广路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of vision-language models in 3D spatial reasoning and the overfitting risks of 3D VQA fine-tuning, the authors propose GASP, a framework that injects geometric priors directly into transformer layers of LLMs. GASP uses a lightweight correspondence head with deep supervision and trains with dual objectives: a contrastive loss enforcing 2D view-invariance and depth consistency supervision resolving 3D ambiguities, both leveraging ground-truth geometry from large-scale video data. Experiments show that standard VLMs achieve correspondence accuracy below 5%, while GASP boosts it to over 70% peak layer-wise accuracy and maintains 85% temporal robustness, leading to +18.2% and +29.0% gains on spatial benchmarks without using 3D VQA data.
鉴于视觉语言模型在三维空间推理上的不足以及3D视觉问答微调或专用编码器的局限性，作者提出GASP方法，通过轻量级对应头将几何先验直接注入Transformer层。该方法采用双监督机制——对比损失确保2D视角不变性，深度一致性监督解决3D几何歧义，并利用大规模视频场景的几何信息训练。实验表明，标准VLM的对应匹配准确率低于5%，而GASP提升至70%以上且保持85%的时间鲁棒性。这些内部改进转化为下游空间基准的显著提升：All-Angles Bench提升18.2%，VSI-Bench提升29.0%，且无需使用任何3D VQA数据，证明从几何先验学习是提升三维空间推理能力的有效且通用路径。
IP-Adapter Is All You Need: Towards Fine-Tuning-Free Diffusion-Based Talking Face Generation
Authors: Hao Wu, Xiangyang Luo, Hao Wang, Jiawei Zhang, Yi Zhang, Jinwei Wang
First: 2026-05-28T17:00:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T17:00:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the rapid advancement of diffusion models, talking face generation has made remarkable progress. However, existing diffusion-based methods still require task-specific fine-tuning and large-scale audiovisual datasets, resulting in high computational costs that hinder scalability and accessibility of diffusion-based approaches across the research community. To address this, we propose a finetuning-free paradigm that directly performs talking face generation using the pretrained weights of Stable Diffusion and IP-Adapter. This backbone leverages the visual embedding capability of IP-Adapter to mine lip-related semantics from the pretrained Stable Diffusion. To address the challenges of identity drift, synchronization errors, and temporal instability, we also design three trainable-parameterfree components: (1) the Structurist, which explicitly disentangles and reassembles lip and appearance features to mitigate identity drift and appearance distortion; (2) the Structure Controller, which adaptively refines embeddings based on quasi-monotonic motion trends for precise lip synchronization; and (3) the Noise Sensor, which introduces Gaussian prior to detect and suppress flicker and jitter artifacts and enhance temporal consistency. Experimental results show that our method outperforms existing SOTA approaches in both lip-sync accuracy (at least 0.16 gain in PCLD) and visual fidelity (at least 0.7 improvement in FID), establishing a novel fine-tuning-free diffusion framework for talking face generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IP-Adapter 即可满足需求：迈向无需微调的扩散式说话人脸生成
随着扩散模型的快速发展，说话人脸生成取得了显著进展。然而，现有基于扩散的方法仍需任务特定微调和大规模视听数据集，导致高昂的计算成本，阻碍了扩散方法在研究社区中的可扩展性和可访问性。为此，我们提出一种无需微调的范式，直接利用预训练的 Stable Diffusion 和 IP-Adapter 权重进行说话人脸生成。该架构利用 IP-Adapter 的视觉嵌入能力，从预训练的 Stable Diffusion 中挖掘与嘴唇相关的语义。为解决身份漂移、同步误差和时间不稳定等问题，我们设计了三个无训练参数的组件：(1) Structurist，显式分离并重组唇部与外观特征，以减轻身份漂移和外观失真；(2) Structure Controller，根据近似单调运动趋势自适应优化嵌入，实现精确的唇部同步；(3) Noise Sensor，引入高斯先验检测并抑制闪烁与抖动伪影，提升时间一致性。实验结果表明，我们的方法在唇部同步精度（PCLD 至少提升 0.16）和视觉保真度（FID 至少提升 0.7）上均优于现有最先进方法，建立了一种全新的无需微调的扩散框架用于说话人脸生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost and scalability barriers of existing diffusion-based talking face generation methods, this work proposes a fine-tuning-free framework that leverages pretrained Stable Diffusion and IP-Adapter weights to directly generate talking faces without task-specific adaptation. The method employs three parameter-free modules — the Structurist to disentangle lip and appearance features, the Structure Controller to align lip motion with audio via quasi-monotonic trends, and the Noise Sensor to suppress temporal artifacts using Gaussian priors — to address identity drift, synchronization errors, and flickering. Experiments demonstrate superior performance over state-of-the-art methods, achieving at least a 0.16 improvement in PCLD for lip-sync accuracy and a 0.7 reduction in FID for visual fidelity.
为解决现有扩散模型生成说话人脸需微调且计算成本高的问题，本文提出一种无需微调的框架，结合预训练的Stable Diffusion与IP-Adapter，利用其视觉嵌入能力提取唇部语义。为应对身份漂移、同步误差和时序不稳定，设计三个无参数模块：Structurist分离重组唇部与外观特征、Structure Controller基于近单调运动趋势优化嵌入、Noise Sensor引入高斯先验抑制闪烁抖动。实验表明，该方法在唇同步精度（PCLD提升0.16）和视觉保真度（FID提升0.7）上超越当前最优方法，构建了可扩展的说话人脸生成范式。
Anti Mode-Collapse in Mean-Field Transformer via Auxiliary Variables
Authors: Masaaki Imaizumi, Masanori Koyama, Noboru Isobe, Kohei Hayashi
First: 2026-05-28T16:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T16:59:42+00:00
Comments: 39 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We use a mean-field-based transformer model to theoretically investigate how auxiliary variables, such as positional encoding, prevent mode collapse of self-attention mechanisms. The use of mean-field transformers to analyze the properties of self-attention mechanisms has garnered significant attention in recent years due to their ability to comprehensively analyze token interactions. However, analysis of this simple model suggests that mode collapse, where token distributions degenerate to a single point, occurs during long inferences (i.e., many layers), indicating a discrepancy with reality. This study investigates this mean-field transformer model and demonstrates that the introduction of auxiliary variables, such as positional encoding, acts as a counterforce against theoretical mode collapse. Specifically, we show that in the theoretical scheme, the energy-maximizing distribution does not degenerate to a single point; instead, it is characterized by a pushforward of the auxiliary variable distribution, thereby avoiding concentration in the Dirac measure. Our main examples are the positional encoding and the fixed prompt insertion treated as a parallel auxiliary-variable mechanism. Furthermore, we demonstrate that positional encoding and prompt insertion possess universality of representation in the limit, meaning that the limit distribution of inference can exactly represent a wide class of distributions. We also analyze several key properties of positional encoding and metastability, and validate our theoretical results through mathematical experiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过辅助变量防止均值场变换器中的模式坍缩
