我们使用基于均值场的变换器模型，从理论上研究辅助变量（如位置编码）如何防止自注意力机制的模式坍缩。近年来，由于均值场变换器能够全面分析令牌交互，其用于分析自注意力机制的特性受到广泛关注。然而，对该简单模型的分析表明，在长推理（即多层）过程中，令牌分布会退化为单点，这与现实存在矛盾。本研究探讨该均值场变换器模型，证明引入辅助变量（如位置编码）可作为对抗理论模式坍缩的反作用力。具体而言，我们表明在理论框架中，能量最大化分布不会退化为单点，而是由辅助变量分布的推前映射所表征，从而避免在狄拉克测度中集中。我们的主要示例是位置编码和固定提示插入，将其视为并行辅助变量机制。此外，我们证明位置编码和提示插入在极限下具有表示的普适性，即推理的极限分布可精确表示广泛分布类。我们还分析了位置编码和元稳定性等关键属性，并通过数学实验验证了理论结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the theoretical mode collapse observed in mean-field transformer models during long inference, this study investigates how auxiliary variables like positional encoding prevent such degeneration. Using a mean-field framework to analyze self-attention interactions, the authors show that without auxiliary variables, token distributions collapse to a single point, contradicting empirical behavior. Introducing auxiliary variables, such as positional encoding or fixed prompts, acts as a counterforce, ensuring the energy-maximizing distribution remains non-degenerate by pushing forward the auxiliary variable distribution. Theoretical analysis confirms that these mechanisms enable universal representation in the limit and exhibit metastability, with results validated through mathematical experiments.
为了解决基于均值场的Transformer模型在长推理中出现的模式坍缩问题（即令牌分布退化为单点），作者研究了辅助变量（如位置编码）如何起到抑制作用。通过均值场框架建模自注意力机制，他们证明辅助变量通过其分布的推前变换，使能量最大化分布避免退化为狄拉克测度。数学实验验证了位置编码和提示插入不仅稳定推理过程，还在极限下实现表示泛化能力，能精确刻画广泛分布类，并表现出元稳定性。
Unifying Temporal and Structural Credit Assignment in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Prompt Optimization
Authors: Wenwu Li, Yuran Song, Mingze Zhao, Bo Jin, Wenhao Li
First: 2026-05-28T16:57:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T16:57:57+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) empower Large Language Models to tackle complex reasoning tasks through collaborative interaction, optimizing their dynamics remains a formidable challenge due to the discrete, non-differentiable nature of the computation graph and the sparsity of global supervisory signals. Existing black-box optimizers struggle to attribute trajectory-level failure to specific local components, resulting in inefficient, high-variance exploration. We argue that tractable MAS optimization needs structural inductive biases to disentangle error signals. We propose temporal and structural credit assignment, which decomposes the objective along two axes: (i) temporal credit, using state-space bottlenecks to identify critical rounds, and (ii) structural credit, using stationary role policies to isolate agent contributions. Leveraging these decomposed signals, we introduce a discrete, verbalized block coordinate descent algorithm for iterative refinement. Rather than indiscriminate global updates, it alternates between optimizing role prompts and aggregation protocols, using LLM-generated "proxy gradients" to target only the identified weak links. Across diverse reasoning benchmarks, our approach substantially reduces query complexity while improving performance, providing a principled and interpretable path toward self-improving MAS.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of optimizing multi-agent systems where discrete computation and sparse feedback hinder effective credit assignment, this work introduces a method that decomposes optimization into temporal and structural credit signals: temporal credit identifies critical interaction rounds via state-space bottlenecks, while structural credit isolates agent contributions using stationary role policies. The method employs a discrete, verbalized block coordinate descent algorithm that iteratively refines role prompts and aggregation protocols using LLM-generated proxy gradients to target only weak components. Experiments across reasoning benchmarks show significant reductions in query complexity and performance gains, demonstrating a principled, interpretable path toward self-improving multi-agent systems.
为解决多智能体系统中因计算离散化与反馈稀疏导致的信用分配难题，该研究提出一种将优化分解为时序与结构信用信号的方法：时序信用通过状态空间瓶颈识别关键交互轮次，结构信用通过静态角色策略分离各智能体贡献。方法采用离散化的块坐标下降算法，交替优化角色提示与聚合协议，利用大语言模型生成的代理梯度精准定位薄弱环节。在多个推理基准测试中，该方法显著降低查询开销并提升性能，为自适应优化的多智能体系统提供了一种原理清晰、可解释的路径。
